Result: 较高孤独感与否定词（r=0.11）、负面语气（r=0.12）和冲突相关语言有关；较低孤独感与社交参考（r=-0.18）、动机驱动（r=-0.11）和语音情感丰富度（r=-0.12）相关。多模态模型（r=0.298）优于纯文本和纯音频模型。

Conclusion: 孤独感同时通过语言和声学线索显现，支持基于语音分析在心理评估中的应用潜力，可作为现有评估工具的辅助早期指标，而非独立的诊断工具。

Abstract: Loneliness is a critical public health issue among older adults, linked to higher risks of depression, cognitive decline, and mortality. Scalable, objective methods for its detection remain limited, particularly in natural conversational contexts. We analyzed speech and language markers of loneliness in 310 older adults using semi-structured telephone interviews to help understand how they process feeling lonely and how their language differs at different levels of feeling loneliness. Our multimodal framework combined linguistic features (psycholinguistic dictionaries, n-grams, and topic models) with acoustic features (pitch, tone, loudness) to examine associations with self-reported loneliness scores. Both predefined and data-driven methods captured patterns in verbal content and vocal delivery. Higher loneliness was associated with negations(r = 0.11), negative tone(r = 0.12), and conflict-related language. Lower loneliness was linked to social references(r = -0.18), motivational drives(r = -0.11), and emotional richness in speech(r = -0.12). We also found that the multimodal model (r = 0.298) outperforms the text-only and audio-only models. Findings suggest that loneliness manifests through both linguistic and acoustic cues, supporting the potential of speech-based analysis in psychological assessments and as an early indicator of emotional loneliness when used alongside existing assessments, rather than as standalone diagnostic tools.

</details>


### [165] [TaRA: Training-Aware Low-Rank Adaptation Initialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02639)
*Taehyeon Kim,Eunhyeok Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TaRA（Training-aware Low-Rank Adaptation Initialization），一种考虑全秩模型训练动态的LoRA初始化方法，使得低秩因子的梯度逼近对应全秩权重矩阵的梯度，从而提升梯度保真度，以极低计算开销在多种微调任务中超越现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA虽已成为参数高效微调的标准方法，但其性能对初始化高度敏感，根源在于低秩分解带来的信息瓶颈。现有方法虽利用预训练权重的成分、激活或梯度来构建初始化，但没有直接考虑全秩模型的训练动态，因此初始化质量仍有提升空间。

Method: TaRA通过数学公式推导，设计一种初始化策略，使得由低秩因子诱导的梯度与对应全秩权重矩阵的梯度尽可能接近。该方法引入的计算开销可忽略不计，是一种简单且可扩展的LoRA初始化方案。

Result: 在多种多样且具有挑战性的微调任务中，TaRA一致性地优于先前的最先进方法，证明了其在梯度保真度和下游任务性能上的有效性。

Conclusion: TaRA提供了一种简单、鲁棒且可扩展的LoRA初始化解决方案，通过直接对齐低秩与全秩模型的训练梯度动态，显著提升了LoRA微调的性能表现。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has become a de facto standard for parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT), yet its performance is highly sensitive to initialization due to the information bottleneck imposed by low-rank decomposition. Existing approaches attempt to construct high-quality LoRA initializations by exploiting principal components of pretrained weights, activations, or gradients. However, these methods do not directly account for the training dynamics of the full-rank model. In this paper, we propose Training-aware Low-Rank Adaptation Initialization (TaRA), a method that initializes LoRA such that the gradients induced by the low-rank factors closely approximate the gradient of the corresponding full-rank weight matrix. Derived from a mathematical formulation, TaRA improves gradient fidelity at the start of training while introducing negligible computational overhead. Across diverse and challenging fine-tuning tasks, TaRA consistently outperforms prior state-of-the-art methods, establishing a simple, robust, and scalable solution for effective LoRA initialization.

</details>


### [166] [WinoQueer-NL: Assessing Bias in Dutch Language Models toward LGBTQ+ Identities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02651)
*Jiska Beuk,Gerasimos Spanakis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究基于英文WinoQueer基准，开发了文化和语言适配的荷兰语反酷儿偏见基准数据集。通过43名荷兰酷儿参与者的在线调查验证和扩充，最终发布42,906个句子。评估多种荷兰语和多语言模型后发现，虽然平均偏见分数看似中性（~50%），但细分显示跨性别和非二元性别身份持续获得最高偏见分数，部分模型对跨性别身份高达97%偏好刻板句子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 英语语言模型的反酷儿偏见已被广泛研究，但荷兰语模型的此类偏见研究仍然缺失，需要填补这一研究空白。

Method: 将英文WinoQueer基准进行文化和语言适配，开发荷兰语数据集；通过43名荷兰酷儿参与者的在线调查验证刻板印象的文化相关性（确认145/171个）并新增22个偏见；使用MLMs和ARLMs等模型，通过比较刻板与非刻板句子的log-likelihood分数来衡量偏见。

Result: 发布包含42,906个句子的最终数据集。平均偏见分数看似中性（~50%），但深入分析显示显著差异：部分模型对跨性别身份偏好刻板句子高达97%，对同性恋相关配对仅6%；跨性别和非二元性别身份始终获得最高偏见分数。

Conclusion: 研究强调了文化根基扎实的数据集对于评估和缓解荷兰语语言模型中针对边缘化群体的偏见的重要性。

Abstract: While English language models have been widely examined for anti-queer bias, Dutch models remain understudied. To address this gap, we developed a culturally and linguistically adapted Dutch dataset based on the English WinoQueer benchmark, containing pairs of stereotypical and counter-stereotypical sentences. To validate and expand it, we conducted an online survey with 43 Dutch queer participants, confirming 145 of 171 stereotypes as culturally relevant and identifying 22 new biases through free-text responses. The final released dataset, comprising 42,906 sentences, was evaluated using a range of Dutch-specific and multilingual models, including both masked language models (MLMs) and autoregressive language models (ARLMs), with bias measured via a score comparing log-likelihoods of stereotypical versus counter-stereotypical sentences. While the mean bias score across models appeared neutral (~50%), closer analysis revealed significant disparities: some models favored stereotypical sentences up to 97% of the time for transgender identities, but only 6% of the time for gay-related pairs, with transgender and non-binary identities consistently receiving the highest bias scores. Our findings highlight the importance of culturally grounded datasets for evaluating and mitigating biases that disproportionately impact marginalized groups in Dutch language models.

</details>


### [167] [oHC: Orthogonal Hyper-Connections on SO(4) via Quaternions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02672)
*Haoqiang Guo,Xuyi Chen,Bo Ke,Yishu Lei,Ziyang Xu,Shikun Feng,Ximen,Wenhan Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出正交超连接(oHC)，将Transformer中Hyper-Connections的残差矩阵限制在旋转群SO(n)上，解决mHC中流间差异收缩导致多样性损失的问题，通过四元数闭合参数化实现无参数增加的高效构造，并在多项下游任务中优于基线、mHC和iHC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hyper-Connections用n条并行残差流替代单流，但无约束的混合矩阵会放大残差尺度导致训练不稳定。mHC将其限制为双随机矩阵，虽封顶了放大因子，却没有下界保护——混合步骤只会压缩流间差异而保持均值不变，随着层数加深，流趋于相似、多样性被消耗殆尽。

Method: 提出将残差混合矩阵限制在SO(n)旋转群上，使混合步骤在任何方向上既不能放大也不能衰减残差流，既保持训练稳定又避免强迫流间差异收缩。在n=4时，利用一对单位四元数对SO(4)进行闭合参数化，无需额外参数，以固定模式的符号加法替代迭代投影，构造比mHC更快。

Result: 在全面的下游任务集上评估，oHC优于单流残差基线、mHC以及将残差矩阵固定为单位阵的iHC。

Conclusion: 正交约束为Hyper-Connections提供了一种既保持训练稳定又不牺牲流间多样性的残差混合方式，且参数化高效、无额外开销。

Abstract: Hyper-Connections (HC) replace the single residual stream of a Transformer with $n$ parallel ones, mixing them at every layer with a learned $n \times n$ residual matrix. Leaving that matrix unconstrained places no limit on the factor by which the mixing step rescales the residual streams, and that factor compounds across layers, which destabilizes training. Manifold-constrained Hyper-Connections (mHC) address this by restricting the matrix to the doubly stochastic matrices. That caps the factor at one, so the mixing can no longer amplify any direction, but nothing bounds it from below. We prove that inside this set the mixing step can reduce the norm of the residual streams only by shrinking the differences between the streams, while their mean is left unchanged; and since the reduction accumulates over layers, the streams grow more alike and their diversity is spent with depth. We therefore propose Orthogonal Hyper-Connections (oHC), restricting the residual matrix to the rotation group $SO(n)$, so that the mixing step can neither amplify nor attenuate the residual streams in any direction, which keeps training stable and no longer forces the differences between the streams to contract. Specifically, at the four streams used by recent HC models we parameterize the group in closed form by a pair of unit quaternions, which adds no parameters, replaces the iterative projection with a fixed pattern of signed additions, and can be constructed faster than mHC. We evaluate oHC across a comprehensive set of downstream tasks, where it outperforms the single-stream residual baseline, mHC and iHC, which fixes the residual matrix to the identity.

</details>


### [168] [From Tokens to Semantics: Leveraging Complementary Signals for Hallucination Detection in Black-Box LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02679)
*Urja Pawar,Rajitha Ramanayake,Owen O'Neill,Nabeel Kemal,Abhishek Mandal,Houssem Chatbri,Christopher Martin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究在大语言模型（LLM）面向公众或高风险工作流时，如何在没有可信上下文或参考文档的黑盒场景下检测模型输出中的虚构（fabrication）内容。论文结合语义熵与token级log-probability不确定性两类互补信号，提出了TopK（跨采样响应聚合token信号）、CoCoA（目标响应不确定性与语义不相似度混合）、Gated（单聚类场景路由到聚合token特征分类器）和Stacked（联合学习语义与token特征）等方法，并在七个基准（含多模态与构建数据集）上使用四个模型进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在面向公众或高风险场景中可能产生虚构内容。漏检会给用户和机构带来危害，而误报则会浪费有限的审核资源。现有方法（语义熵和token不确定性）各有失效模式：语义熵在响应构成单一语义聚类时信息量不足，而token不确定性可能遗漏前后一致的自信错误，因此需要更鲁棒的检测方法。

Method: 提出多种检测方法：TopK通过跨采样响应聚合token级信号扩展了基于token的不确定性检测；CoCoA结合目标响应不确定性与语义不相似度；Gated将单聚类案例路由至使用聚合token特征的分类器；Stacked联合学习语义不确定性与更广泛的token特征。在七个基准（四个文本数据集、一个多模态手写支票提取任务、两个构建的金融摘要与长文本问答数据集）上使用四个LLM进行评估。

Result: 在跨模型和数据集的评估中，Stacked在近半数情况下表现最佳；TopK和CoCoA在无监督训练标签下仍具竞争力，但其阈值需要仔细校准。没有任何方法在所有情况下都普遍最优。论文在1%-15%的假阳性率预算下评估性能，并检验了方法对生成与校准选择的敏感性及跨数据集特征的差异。

Conclusion: 不存在在所有模型和数据集上普适最优的单一虚构检测方法；不同方法具有互补优势。Stacked作为监督方法在多数情况下最优，而无监督方法TopK和CoCoA在无标签场景下是强有力候选，但阈值校准至关重要。在实际部署时应根据具体应用场景、可用标签和假阳性率预算来选择和配置检测方法。

Abstract: When LLMs support public-facing or high-stakes workflows, missed fabrications can harm users and institutions, while false alarms consume limited human-review capacity. When no trusted context or reference document is available, we study two signals accessible through black-box model APIs: semantic entropy, which measures disagreement among sampled response meanings, and uncertainty derived from token log-probabilities. Their failure modes can be complementary: semantic entropy becomes uninformative when responses form one semantic cluster, while token uncertainty can miss consistently confident errors. We extend token-based uncertainty detection by aggregating token-level signals across sampled responses through our TopK method, evaluate the hybrid CoCoA method, which combines target-response uncertainty with semantic dissimilarity, and propose and study two supervised methods: Gated, which routes single-cluster cases to an aggregated-token-feature classifier, and Stacked, which learns jointly from semantic uncertainty and broader token features. We evaluate seven benchmarks, including five public benchmarks (four text datasets and multimodal handwritten-cheque extraction) and two constructed benchmarks (Financial Summaries and Long-Text QA), using four language models. In our evaluation across models and datasets, Stacked gave the best performance in nearly half of the cases, while TopK and CoCoA remain competitive without supervised training labels, although their thresholds require careful calibration. No method is universally strongest. We therefore evaluate performance at false-positive-rate budgets from 1% to 15%, assess their sensitivity to generation and calibration choices, and examine variation across dataset characteristics.

</details>


### [169] [DKL: Decoupled Knowledge Learning for Instruction-Tuned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02685)
*Kushagra Bhushan,Meghanadh Pulivarthi,Sai Krishna Reddy Sathi,Gaurav Pandey,Sonam Gupta,Vineet Kumar,Jaydeep Sen,Yatin Nandwani,Sachindra Joshi,Dinesh Raghu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DKL（解耦知识学习）方法，通过在基础LLM上进行扩展预训练（EPT）注入新知识，然后将知识权重与指令微调后的模型进行融合，从而在不破坏指令遵循能力的前提下为Instruct LLM注入新知识，避免昂贵的指令微调成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG虽能注入新知识但检索失败时易产生幻觉；微调方法（如RAFT、PA-RAG）需要大量合成QA数据；在Instruct LLM上直接EPT会破坏指令遵循能力，而后续IFT成本高昂且可能缺少指令微调语料库。

Method: DKL方法：1）在Instruct LLM对应的基础LLM上执行EPT以注入新知识；2）将注入知识后的权重与Instruct LLM的权重进行模型合并，从而在传递新知识的同时保留原有指令遵循能力。该方法轻量且避免昂贵的指令微调。

Result: 在检索失败案例中，DKL将RAG准确率从54.17提升至79.26，且使用显著更少的训练数据即可超越先前方法。

Conclusion: DKL通过解耦知识学习与指令遵循能力，利用模型合并技术实现了高效的知识注入，在RAG检索失败场景下大幅提升了准确率，同时降低了训练成本和数据需求。

Abstract: RAG has become the de facto method for incorporating new, corpus-specific knowledge into an instruction following LLM (Instruct LLM). Although RAG-based prompting improves factual grounding, it fails when retrieval is incorrect or incomplete, leading to hallucinations. Finetuning methods such as RAFT and PA-RAG enhance RAG by injecting new knowledge into the model's parameters, but require generating a massive amount of synthetic QA that covers the entire corpus. Extended Pre-Training (EPT) on the text corpus avoids the need for comprehensive synthetic data generation but compromises an Instruct LLM's instruction-following capabilities, necessitating instruction fine-tuning (IFT) after pre-training. However, IFT is costly and may be infeasible due to the unavailability of an instruction-tuning corpus. In this work, we propose DKL-Decoupled Knowledge Learning for Instruction-Tuned Language Models. Instead of doing EPT on the Instruct LLM, DKL performs EPT on its corresponding base LLM to infuse new knowledge. These knowledge infused weights are then merged with the Instruct LLM, imparting new knowledge without affecting their instruction-following capabilities. DKL is a lightweight method that avoids expensive instruction fine-tuning and relies on model merging to infuse the new knowledge into the Instruct LLM without destroying its instruction following capabilities. Empirical results show that DKL improves RAG accuracy from 54.17 to 79.26 on retrieval failure cases, while outperforming prior approaches with substantially less training data.

</details>


### [170] [Trace as State: Reasoning Traces as Conditional States for Long-Context Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02702)
*Xu Zou,Jie Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Transformer因果处理与长上下文推理之间的错配问题，并提出"Trace as State"方法：将先前收集的推理轨迹作为任务状态的文本代理，放在长上下文块之前进行重读，从而大幅提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer以因果方式处理信息，但长上下文推理往往可能依赖于只有在后期才被发现的任务状态。这种因果处理的先后顺序与推理需求之间存在根本性的不匹配，本文通过"条件状态更新任务"（conditional state update tasks）形式化了这一错配问题。

Method: 1) 形式化证明：对于因果状态更新处理器，条件先给出可比条件后给出在内存上指数级更优。2) 提出Trace as State方法：使用收集到的推理轨迹作为任务状态的文本代理，在全新的一遍处理中将其置于长上下文块之前，使先前派生的信息引导重读。3) 设置Trace Append作为匹配对照组，使用相同的任务状态代理但将其放在上下文之后。

Result: 在3个模型和3个长上下文数据集上，Trace as State在27个模型-任务-指标组合中的26个优于Trace Append。在GraphWalks Parents任务上，DeepSeek V4 Pro Preview的精确匹配率从初始遍的29.2%和Trace Append的43.0%提升至81.8%；GLM-5.2从66.4%和83.2%提升至100.0%。

Conclusion: 将推理轨迹放在上下文之前可以显著改善长上下文推理性能，同时在保持因果Transformer结构不变的前提下实现这一改进。这提供了一种不改变架构但与因果处理特性相协调的有效推理策略。

Abstract: Transformers process information causally, but long-context reasoning may depend on task state discovered only later. We formalize this mismatch through conditional state update tasks. For causal state update processors, providing the condition first can require exponentially less memory in the worst case than providing it last.
  Motivated by this principle, we introduce Trace as State. We use collected reasoning traces as a textual proxy for task state and place it before the long-context block on a fresh pass, allowing information derived previously to guide rereading.
  We conduct extensive experiments on Trace as State and Trace Append, a matched control that uses the same task state proxy but put it after the context. Across three models and three long-context datasets, Trace as State outperforms Trace Append in 26 of 27 reported combinations of model, task, and metric. On GraphWalks Parents, exact match lifts DeepSeek V4 Pro Preview from 29.2% on the initial pass and 43.0% with Trace Appendto 81.8% with Trace as State, and from 66.4% and 83.2% to 100.0% for GLM-5.2. These results show that placing traces before the context can improve long-context reasoning while retaining the causal transformer structure.

</details>


### [171] [CORAL: An LLM-Native Harness for Production Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02730)
*Muhammad Rafay Azhar,Yuhang Zhou,Gilbert Jiang,Yuchen Wang,Rahul Sharma,Matthew DeSousa,Jiayi Liu,Xin Guo,Lizhu Zhang,Xiangjun Fan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CORAL是一个LLM原生的智能体循环系统，通过让智能体在每个周期中观察运行信号、基于历史决策记忆进行推理，并调用工具（包括数值优化器）来重新配置生产推荐系统，从而在固定的运行预算内实现持续的闭环优化。在两个大型社交平台上通过A/B实验验证，该系统在一平台上提升了用户参与度且无额外服务成本，在另一平台上降低了服务成本而不损失参与度，性能随循环迭代而提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产推荐系统的性能需要持续优化，但传统的在线实验方法依赖人类工程师，过程缓慢且被动，受工程人力限制，导致系统部分环节无法随条件变化及时调整。现有LLM应用多聚焦于排序、用户建模和离线模型开发，很少有系统让智能体在持续闭环中作用于活体推荐系统并从决策效果中学习。

Method: CORAL将这一问题形式化为部分可观测、非平稳、带约束的优化问题，其中策略无需参数更新即可从先前的行动中在上下文中改进。每个周期中，智能体观察运行信号，基于过去的决策和结果记忆进行推理，并调用包括数值优化器在内的工具来重新配置推荐系统，同时将变更限制在固定运行预算内，测量的结果反馈到下一周期。

Result: 在两个大型社交平台上通过A/B实验评估，同一套系统在一平台上提升了用户参与度且不增加服务成本，在另一平台上降低了服务成本而不损害参与度，覆盖了参与度-效率的前沿。性能随循环迭代次数增加而改善。

Conclusion: 单一的智能体循环可以在明确的安全护栏下自动化传统上由人类算法工程师执行的持续优化工作，为生产推荐系统的自动化持续优化提供了一种可行的范式。

Abstract: Production recommender systems shape what billions of people see, and sustaining their performance requires continual optimization: as content, user behavior, and upstream models shift, the choices governing retrieval, ranking, and serving must be revisited. Traditionally, human engineers test such changes through online experiments--a slow, reactive process limited by engineering effort, leaving parts of the system unrevised as conditions change. Although large language models have been applied to ranking, user modeling, and offline model development, few systems place an agent in a continual closed loop that acts on a live recommender and learns from the measured effects of its decisions. We present CORAL (Constraint-Optimized Recommender via an Agentic Loop), an LLM-native harness that closes this loop: each cycle, the agent observes operating signals, reasons over a memory of past decisions and outcomes, and invokes tools--including a numerical optimizer that keeps changes within a fixed operating budget--to reconfigure the recommender, with measured outcomes informing the next cycle. We formulate this as a partially observed, non-stationary, constrained optimization problem in which the policy improves in context, without parameter updates, from its prior actions. Across two large-scale social platforms, evaluated with A/B experiments, the same harness improves engagement at no additional serving cost on one and reduces serving cost without degrading engagement on the other, spanning the engagement-efficiency frontier. Performance improves as the loop iterates, suggesting that a single agentic loop can automate continual optimization work traditionally performed by human algorithm engineers under explicit guardrails.

</details>


### [172] [Choosing a PEFT Variant for Per-Patient Dysarthric ASR: A Single-Speaker Case Study on Two ASR Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02735)
*Bernard Muller,László Tóth,LaVonne Roberts*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对构音障碍（dysarthria）语音识别中逐患者（per-patient）适配器场景，首次系统比较了七种LoRA家族参数高效微调方法在两名生产级基础模型上的表现。研究发现注意力投影适配器显著改善CER；LoRA与DoRA无显著差异，故选择更简单的LoRA；真实4-bit QLoRA在所有设置下均更差；全量微调最准确（11.43% CER），但115MB的LoRA在同时适配前馈层后可达到接近全量微调的效果（差距0.66个百分点），仅需约3.7%的存储。入组测试显示约5分钟患者音频即可获得近一半的CER改善收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逐患者适配器是构音障碍语音识别生产架构的首选方案，但此前缺乏在说话人依赖、逐患者场景下对参数高效微调（PEFT）变体的系统比较研究。作者希望填补这一空白，为临床生产环境选择最优的适配策略提供实证依据。

Method: 论文采用单说话人案例研究设计：针对一位中风后重度构音障碍的匈牙利语男性患者（S1，409条话语），在Whisper-large-v3（匈牙利语微调版）和多语言Qwen3-ASR-1.7B两个生产基座上，系统比较七种LoRA家族方法（LoRA、QLoRA、AdaLoRA、DoRA、LoHA、VeRA、VB-LoRA），使用配对bootstrap进行统计显著性检验，并评估全量微调、不同存储规模及入组音频时长对性能的影响。

Result: 注意力投影适配器在两个基座上都显著改善CER。三种随机种子下，配对bootstrap显示LoRA与DoRA无显著差异（Whisper上13.86/13.90% CER，Qwen3-ASR上28.10/28.33%），故选择更简单便宜的LoRA。真实4-bit（NF4）QLoRA在每种种子和两个基座上均更差（14.56/30.09% CER）且在该规模下无内存节省。LoHA、VeRA、VB-LoRA和AdaLoRA未达到LoRA家族水平，不过LoHA在Whisper上仍有18.6%相对CER降低。全量微调更精确（11.43% CER），但同时适配前馈层的115MB LoRA可达到接近效果（差距0.66个百分点），仅需约3.7%的逐患者存储。6点入组网格显示约5分钟患者音频可捕获零样本到30分钟CER改善的45.6%。

Conclusion: 在重度构音障碍逐患者ASR场景中，简单LoRA即为参数高效微调的最优选择；适配前馈层可进一步提升性能至接近全量微调水平；约5分钟的患者语音即可获得大部分适配收益。研究局限在于单说话人、单语言、重度中风后构音障碍，训练脚本和配方将在发表后以研究用途许可开源发布。

Abstract: Per-patient adapters are the preferred production architecture for dysarthric automatic speech recognition (ASR), yet parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) variants have not been compared in the speaker-dependent, per-patient regime. We present a single-speaker case study comparing seven LoRA-family methods (LoRA, QLoRA, AdaLoRA, DoRA, LoHA, VeRA, VB-LoRA) on two production bases (Whisper-large-v3 with Hungarian fine-tuning, and a multilingual Qwen3-ASR-1.7B checkpoint) for one post-stroke Hungarian male speaker (S1, 409 utterances; severe dysarthria on auditory-perceptual clinical assessment). Attention-projection adapters substantially improve CER on both bases. Across three seeds, a paired bootstrap detects no significant LoRA-DoRA difference (p>0.5; 13.86/13.90 % CER on Whisper, 28.10/28.33 % on Qwen3-ASR), so we adopt the simpler, cheaper LoRA. Real 4-bit (NF4) QLoRA is worse on every seed and both bases (14.56/30.09 % CER) with no memory saving at this scale, and LoHA, VeRA, VB-LoRA and AdaLoRA do not reach the LoRA family, though LoHA still gives an 18.6 % relative CER reduction on Whisper. On the same base, full fine-tuning is more accurate (11.43 % CER), but a 115 MB LoRA that also adapts the feed-forward blocks reaches within 0.66 pp of it at approximately 3.7 % of the per-patient storage. A 6-point enrollment grid shows about 5 min of patient audio captures 45.6 % of the zero-shot-to-30-min CER reduction, with further gains at 10 and 30 min (caveat: one speaker, one language, severe post-stroke dysarthria). Training scripts and recipes will be released, source-available under a research-use licence, on publication.

</details>


### [173] [Language Models Can Control Their Own Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02737)
*Namgyu Ho,Huzama Ahmad,Woosung Koh,Se-Young Yun,Tal Schuster,Cicero Nogueira dos Santos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出声明式注意力（Declarative Attention, DA），一种让语言模型在思维链中主动声明需要关注上下文的哪些区域，从而在推理时跳过大部分KV缓存读取的协议，在15个长上下文任务上实现了显著的计算节省（减少52.0%和31.1%的注意力token）且精度损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型在长上下文场景中，虽然大部分注意力集中在少数token上，但每个解码步骤仍需扫描完整KV缓存以找到关键token。已有的方法通过轻量级代理分数预选相关token，但这种方法仍需要O(N)的每步代价。作者提出一个根本性问题：模型自身是否已经知道哪些上下文部分是相关的？由此探索一种内在的（intrinsic）而非外在打分的方法。

Method: 提出声明式注意力（DA）协议：模型在思维链（chain-of-thought）中主动声明需要关注的上下文区域，将生成划分为三种模式——<global>（完整上下文）、<focus>（特定区域）、<local>（仅最近输出）。推理引擎像解析工具调用一样解析这些声明，据此跳过大部分KV缓存读取。

Result: 在零样本设置下，该方法在15个长上下文任务上对现成模型（Gemma-4-31B、Qwen-3.6-27B）进行测试：总注意力token分别减少52.0%和31.1%，精度仅下降1.27个百分点和2.75个百分点，且精度损失随模型规模增大而减小。

Conclusion: DA开辟了稀疏注意力的新维度，在现有模型上无需训练即可实现显著的计算节省，且未来可通过基于训练的方法进一步发掘其潜力。

Abstract: Language models spend most of their attention on a small fraction of context, yet they read the entire KV cache to find the few tokens that matter. If the user asks about a previous detail in a 1M-token conversation, global attention layers must scan the full context to generate each token of the reply. A prominent approach mitigates this cost by pre-selecting relevant tokens via lightweight proxy scores, but this extrinsic scoring still incurs O(N) per step. We take an intrinsic approach motivated by the simple question: wouldn't the model already know which parts of the context are relevant? To this end, we introduce Declarative Attention (DA), a protocol that elicits the model to declare where it needs to attend within its chain-of-thought, partitioning generation into three modes: <global> (full context), <focus> (a specific region), and <local> (recent output only). The inference engine parses these declarations like tool calls and skips most of the KV cache read. Under zero-shot evaluation across 15 long-context tasks, DA on off-the-shelf models (Gemma-4-31B, Qwen-3.6-27B) significantly reduces total attended tokens during decoding (52.0%, 31.1%) with modest accuracy drops (1.27pp, 2.75pp) that shrink with model scale. DA unlocks a new axis of sparse attention, with further potential under training-based methods that future work can explore.

</details>


### [174] [Untangling the Mechanisms of Misleading Context in Medical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02754)
*Robin Linzmayer,Noémie Elhadad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨误导性上下文如何损害大语言模型的医学判断。在MedMisBench基准（8627个医学推理问题）中注入两类误导线索（伪造证据和裸断言），发现三个推理模型对断言的易感性高于伪造证据（高出10-27个百分点）；误导线索在推理轨迹中披露率(81-98%)远高于最终响应(7-90%)；两类线索以不同机制破坏推理（证据早期累积渗透，断言在结尾重定向结论）；开放的推理轨迹配合LLM监视器能以5%假阳性捕获78%的受损决策，远超任何响应形式的最高32%。作者认为最易导致误导的上下文（断言）披露最少，且只能通过前沿供应商不提供的开放推理轨迹才能可靠捕获。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在医疗问答上已达到专家级表现，但其依赖的上下文可能具有误导性，这种误导性上下文会破坏模型的医学判断。理解误导性上下文如何影响模型判断，包括模型对这种上下文的易感性、披露程度、推理受损机制以及决策的可监控性，对于医疗AI的安全部署至关重要。

Method: 使用MedMisBench基准中的8627个临床审核问答问题，注入两类误导性上下文线索：伪造证据和裸断言。测试三个推理模型（两个暴露完整推理轨迹，一个前沿模型仅暴露响应）。通过对比模型对两种误导线索的采纳率、分析推理轨迹中的披露情况、重采样无披露的推理轨迹探究不同的推理受损机制，并使用LLM监视器评估对受损决策的检测能力。

Result: 三个模型对断言的易感性均高于伪造证据（采纳断言回答的频率高出10-27个百分点）；误导线索在推理轨迹中的披露率(81-98%)高于响应(7-90%)，且断言类线索披露率低于证据类；两类线索以不同方式破坏推理：证据早期进入并累积影响，断言则在推理末尾重定向结论；带指导的LLM监视器阅读开放模型的推理轨迹能以5%假阳性率捕获78%的受损决策，而基于响应的监控最高仅捕获32%。

Conclusion: 模型最易受误导的上下文类型（裸断言）披露最少，且只有通过开放的推理轨迹才能被可靠监控，但前沿模型供应商恰恰隐藏了这些推理轨迹。这对依赖专有、封闭模型的医疗AI系统构成重要安全警示，支持开放推理轨迹对于确保医疗决策可靠性与可监控性的必要性。

Abstract: Large language models now answer medical questions with expert-level performance. However, the context these systems act on can be misleading, and misleading context can corrupt a model's medical judgment. To understand how misleading context corrupts this judgment, we examine the model's susceptibility to the context, disclosure of it, mechanism of corrupted reasoning, and monitorability of the decision. On the medical reasoning subset of MedMisBench, a clinician-reviewed question-answering benchmark of 8,627 questions, we inject two types of misleading context cues, fabricated evidence and a bare assertion. We test three reasoning models, two that expose their full reasoning trace and one frontier model that exposes only its response. All three are more susceptible to the assertion than to the fabricated evidence, adopting the asserted answer 10 to 27 points more often. The misleading cues are disclosed in 81 to 98% of traces but only 7 to 90% of responses, and the assertion is disclosed less often than evidence based cues. Resampling from reasoning traces without disclosure shows the two cues corrupt reasoning differently, evidence entering early and accumulating while the assertion redirects the conclusion near its end. An LLM monitor catches 78% of corrupted decisions at 5% false positives when reading an open model's trace with guidance, against at most 32% from any response. The misleading context that models are most susceptible to is disclosed least, and was caught reliably only from an open reasoning trace, which frontier providers withhold.

</details>


### [175] [From Reweighting to Rewriting: Unlocking the Intervention Effects of Influential Samples in Training Data Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02771)
*Yuzhang Luo,Chenpeng Wang,Jianhui Chen,Liangming Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"影响力引导的响应重写"（influence-guided response rewriting）方法，通过影响函数（IF）识别干预目标，并在保持指令不变的情况下替换其响应为行为对齐或行为相反的监督信号。实验发现，与传统的权重重加方法相比，响应重写能产生更强、更持久且双向的行为转变，揭示影响力估计所捕获的局部重加权效应与示例本身具有的更广泛干预杠杆之间存在重要区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，影响函数估计的训练样本在基于权重的干预下往往仅表现出与随机选择相当的效果，这引发了一个核心问题：是有影响力的示例本身缺乏干预价值，还是重加权方式未能充分发挥其行为杠杆作用？本文旨在厘清这一问题。

Method: 使用影响函数（IF）识别干预目标示例，在其指令固定不变的前提下，将这些示例的响应替换为与目标行为一致或相反的监督信号（即响应重写），并与传统的权重重加方法在相同的IF选择示例上进行系统比较。实验以认知弃权（epistemic abstention）和安全性拒绝（safety refusal）为主要测试场景，在四个开源大语言模型上验证。

Result: 响应重写相比权重重加在相同的影响函数选择示例上产生更强、更持久且双向的行为转变，而权重重加仅产生微弱且不一致的效果。同时，影响函数选择的示例相比其他选择器提供更大的重写杠杆，行为变化集中在目标相关行为上。该定性差异在安全性拒绝任务中同样成立。

Conclusion: 影响力估计捕获的局部重加权效应不同于其所识别示例的完整干预杠杆，两者之间存在实质区别。这提示需要对TDA方法进行"干预感知"（intervention-aware）的评估，即评估训练数据归因方法时应考虑其在不同干预方式下的实际价值，而不仅仅局限于重加权这一种干预范式。

Abstract: Training data attribution (TDA) aims to identify training examples that shape model behavior, but its intervention value depends on both which examples are selected and how they are modified. Influence functions (IF) estimate behavioral changes under infinitesimal reweighting, yet IF-selected examples often show limited advantages over random selection under conventional weight-based interventions. This raises the question of whether influential examples lack intervention value or whether reweighting fails to realize their behavioral leverage.We introduce influence-guided response rewriting, which uses IF to identify intervention targets and replaces their responses with behavior-aligned or behavior-opposed supervision while keeping instructions fixed. Across four open-weight LLMs, we compare rewriting and reweighting on the same influence-selected examples using epistemic abstention as our primary testbed. Response rewriting produces stronger, more persistent, and bidirectional behavioral shifts, while reweighting the same examples yields weak and inconsistent effects. Further analyses show that influence-selected examples provide greater rewriting leverage than alternative selectors, with changes remaining concentrated on target-relevant behaviors. The same qualitative contrast extends to safety refusal. These results distinguish the local reweighting effects captured by influence estimates from the broader intervention leverage of the examples they identify, motivating intervention-aware evaluation of TDA methods.

</details>


### [176] [HyperStyler: Low-resource Authorship Style Transfer via Context-aware Style Navigation and Hypernetworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02772)
*Jongkyung Shin,Minguk Jeon,Chanwoo Park,Chiehyeon Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出HyperStyler架构，将低资源作者风格迁移解耦为风格选择与风格实现两个阶段，通过风格坐标预测和动态参数调制替代传统的静态嵌入和隐藏状态注入，在风格保真度和语义保持上均超越现有方法（包括LLM），且参数量仅增加2.4%、推理速度快1.8倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LAST方法存在两个核心缺陷：(1)将多样化的参考样例压缩为单一静态作者嵌入，平均化了上下文相关的风格变化；(2)依赖隐藏表示进行风格控制，导致风格与内容相互纠缠，难以同时实现高风格保真度和语义保持。

Method: 提出HyperStyler架构，将LAST解耦为两部分：Stylo-navigator通过联合建模源文本上下文与目标作者参考样例来预测风格坐标；Stylo-hypernet通过动态参数调制（而非隐藏状态注入）来实现这些风格坐标。该方法以T5-large为基座，仅需额外2.4%的参数。

Result: 在Reddit、Blog和News三个数据集上的实验表明，HyperStyler在风格保真度和语义保持方面持续优于包括基于LLM的方法在内的先前方法，并能在跨领域场景下稳健泛化。参数开销小（仅增加2.4%），推理速度比LLM快1.8倍以上，且仅需少量参考样例即可达到最佳性能。

Conclusion: 将风格迁移解耦为选择与实现两个可分离阶段，并采用动态参数调制替代静态嵌入和隐藏状态注入，是解决低资源作者风格迁移中风格-内容纠缠问题的有效途径，为轻量级、高效且可泛化的风格迁移提供了新思路。

Abstract: Low-resource authorship style transfer (LAST) aims to rewrite text into the style of an arbitrary target author using only a few reference examples while preserving the original meaning. Existing methods often struggle to achieve both high style fidelity and semantic preservation because they compress diverse references into a single static author embedding, which averages out context-dependent stylistic variation, and rely on hidden representations for style control, which entangle style with content. We propose HyperStyler, a novel architecture that decouples LAST into style selection and style realization. Stylo-navigator predicts style coordinates by jointly modeling the source context and target-author references, and Stylo-hypernet realizes them via dynamic parameter modulation instead of hidden-state injection. Our experiments on Reddit, Blog, and News datasets demonstrate that HyperStyler consistently outperforms prior methods including LLM-based approaches and generalizes robustly across domains. Notably, HyperStyler achieves superior performance with as few as 2.4% additional parameters over T5-large, while being over 1.8x faster than LLMs at inference.

</details>


### [177] [EarlyEval: Cheaper Agent Evaluation via Early Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02783)
*Yuling Shi,Zhensu Sun,Junsen Dong,Chengcheng Wan,David Lo,Xiaodong Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EarlyEval，一种用于评估LLM智能体的早期结果预测框架。核心思想是智能体的最终结果往往在其执行完成前就能从中间行为中显现，通过训练LightGBM分类器在置信度阈值达到时提前终止运行，从而在不显著影响评估准确性的前提下大幅降低评估成本（消除13%-26%的智能体步骤，减少最高44.1%输入token和29.4%输出token）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估LLM智能体对指导其发展至关重要，但成本高昂——单次评估前沿模型在智能体基准上的运行可能花费数百至数千美元，且在迭代开发中反复支付。现有基准蒸馏方法减少了评估任务数量，但未降低每个任务的执行成本，因此需要一种在任务内部削减成本的新方法。

Method: EarlyEval训练一对LightGBM成功和失败分类器，利用行为特征、文本特征和参考解特征，在智能体运行过程中每个步骤提取这些特征。当任一分类器跨越校准后的置信度阈值时立即终止智能体运行，每一步的开销可忽略不计。

Result: 在三个基准（SWE-bench Verified、TerminalBench和Toolathlon）上，EarlyEval可消除13%-26%的智能体步骤，减少最多44.1%的输入token和29.4%的输出token，预测准确率达89%-97%，而每个智能体的resolve率平均仅受1-2个百分点扰动。

Conclusion: 早期结果预测是基准蒸馏的互补效率轴，能在任务内部削减成本。实验结果表明该方法在显著降低评估成本的同时能保持较高的预测准确性和对最终结果判断的影响非常小，为代价高昂的LLM智能体评估提供了实用的成本削减方案。

Abstract: Evaluating LLM agents is essential for guiding their development, yet it has grown prohibitively expensive: a single pass of a frontier model over an agentic benchmark can cost hundreds to thousands of dollars, a price paid repeatedly across iterative development cycles. Prior efforts, centered on benchmark distillation, reduce the number of evaluation tasks but leave the cost of executing each retained task untouched. In this work, we introduce early outcome prediction, a complementary axis of efficiency that instead cuts cost within each task. Our key insight is that an agent's final outcome is often evident from its intermediate behavior well before execution completes. We instantiate this idea in EarlyEval, a lightweight framework that trains a pair of LightGBM success and failure classifiers over behavioral, textual, and reference-solution features, and halts an agent run the moment either classifier crosses a calibrated confidence threshold, adding negligible per-step overhead. Across three benchmarks, SWE-bench Verified, TerminalBench, and Toolathlon, EarlyEval can eliminate 13%-26% of agent steps and up to 44.1% input tokens and 29.4% output tokens at 89%-97% prediction accuracy, while perturbing per-agent resolve rates by only one to two percentage points on average.

</details>


### [178] [DiscoSign: Discourse-Aware Text to Sign Language Gloss Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02796)
*Vasileios Baltatzis,Mert Inan,Connor Gillis,Raja Kushalnagar,Lorna Quandt,Leah Findlater,Colin Lea*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DiscoSign，一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的模块化框架，用于篇章级文本到手语词汇注释（gloss）的翻译。该框架针对三个关键篇章现象：空间共指消解、疑问-回答小句（QACs）和概念-词汇一致性，并引入一套新的篇章级评测指标。实验表明该方法在篇章级一致性上显著优于仅限句子的翻译，同时保持了句子级翻译质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手语处理系统仅在句子层面操作，忽视了手语理解中至关重要的篇章现象。现有方法无法处理篇章级别的语言现象，且缺乏相应的评测手段。

Method: 采用基于大语言模型（LLM）的模块化翻译框架，分别处理三个篇章现象：空间共指消解（确保实体在篇章中保持一致的空间位置）、疑问-回答小句（QACs）的假拟分裂结构处理、以及概念-词汇一致性（确保英语概念与ASL手语之间的稳定映射）。同时提出一套针对各篇章维度专门设计的评测指标体系。

Result: 在句子级和篇章级数据集上的实验表明，篇章感知处理方法相较仅限句子的翻译显著提升了空间一致性和实体追踪能力，同时保持了有竞争力的单句翻译质量。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了首个系统性的篇章级文本到手语词汇注释翻译框架及配套评测方法，填补了该领域在篇章处理层面的空白。

Abstract: Sign language processing systems have traditionally operated at the sentence level, ignoring critical discourse phenomena fundamental to sign language comprehension. We introduce DiscoSign, a computational approach for discourse-aware text to sign language gloss translation grounded in linguistic research. We address three key phenomena within our modular Large Language Model (LLM)-based translation framework: (i) spatial coreference resolution, where entities maintain consistent spatial locations throughout discourse; (ii) Question-Answer Clauses (QACs), pseudocleft structures serving specific discourse functions; and (iii) concept-gloss consistency, ensuring stable mappings between English concepts and American Sign Language (ASL) signs. Traditional translation metrics fail to capture discourse-level quality, so we introduce a suite of novel evaluation metrics designed to assess each dimension of discourse coherence addressed by our framework. Experiments on sentence-level and discourse-level datasets show that our approach for discourse-aware processing significantly improves spatial consistency and entity tracking relative to sentence-only translation, while maintaining competitive single-sentence gloss translation quality. Our work establishes the first systematic framework for discourse-level text to sign language gloss translation with corresponding evaluation methodology.

</details>


### [179] [User Feedback Provides a Unique Signal that LLMs Can not Detect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02859)
*Shachar Don-Yehiya,Leshem Choshen,Omri Abend*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文挑战了"用户反馈信号噪声大、难以利用"的既有认知，证明用户反馈实际上是高度可操作的改进信号。所谓反馈无效，根源在于现有评估范式存在系统性偏差——当模型仅凭反馈成功修复问题时，LLM评审者常无法识别真正修正后的回答，反而系统性地偏爱较差的基线输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来研究表明用户交互反馈存在固有噪声且难以有效利用，作者质疑这一结论，认为反馈的"无效性"可能源于评估方法的偏差而非反馈本身价值低。为了验证这一点，需要将反馈的有用性与评估范式的影响分离开来。

Method: 构建两类数据：(1) 具有确定性ground truth的合成数据，用于隔离反馈的有用性；(2) 自然主义数据，验证发现在真实场景中是否成立。通过对比"有反馈"与"无反馈"条件下的模型修订版本，评估反馈对问题修复率的贡献，并进一步分析LLM评审者的判断偏差。

Result: 反馈驱动的修订解决问题的比率显著高于无反馈的基线修订。同时揭示了评估偏差的根源：当模型只因反馈而成功修复问题时，LLM评审者经常无法识别出真正修正的回答，反而系统性偏好较差的基线输出。

Conclusion: 用户反馈本质上是高度可操作的改进信号，其表面上的无效性源于评估范式的系统性偏差。这提示研究者需重新审视现有的LLM评估方法，避免因评审偏差而低估反馈学习的价值。

Abstract: Harnessing naturally occurring feedback from user interactions offers a promising learning signal for Large Language Models (LLMs). However, recent studies suggest this feedback is inherently noisy and difficult to leverage effectively. We challenge this conception by demonstrating that user feedback is a highly actionable signal for improvement, and that its perceived ineffectiveness stems from a systematic bias in current evaluation paradigms. To isolate the usefulness of feedback, we construct synthetic data with a definitive ground truth, alongside naturalistic data to validate that our findings hold in real-world scenarios. By comparing model revisions generated with and without access to feedback across both settings, we show that feedback-informed revisions resolve targeted issues at significantly higher rates than baseline revisions. Finally, we expose the root of the evaluation bias: when a model successfully fixes an issue exclusively due to feedback, LLM judges frequently fail to identify the genuinely corrected response, systematically preferring inferior baseline outputs instead.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [180] [CircuitsDNA: Discovering Unconventional Multi-Accuracy Arithmetic Circuits via Evolutionary Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01735)
*Ruichen Qi,Junyi Luo,Xinting Jiang,Quan Cheng,Gregory Kielian,Ben Laurie,Dennis Sylvester,Mehdi Saligane*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出CircuitsDNA，一种进化框架，自动演化支持多种精度模式的可配置算术电路，通过多阈值可验证性miter、资源受限的可验证性驱动搜索和反馈驱动的自适应变异，在8位乘法器上实现高达56%的面积功耗乘积降低，同时保持低于2%的精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴的边缘AI工作负载需要能够按需在计算精度和效率之间进行权衡的算术单元。然而，现有的近似算术电路要么精度固定，要么依赖预定义结构来实现运行时可配置性，缺乏自动生成支持多种精度模式的电路的能力。

Method: 提出CircuitsDNA进化框架，包含三个关键特性：1) 多阈值可验证性miter用于强制满足各模式的精度要求；2) 资源受限的可验证性驱动搜索以减少验证开销而不牺牲正确性，支持大规模电路设计的高效探索；3) 反馈驱动的自适应变异以优先考虑有效的结构修改并加速搜索收敛。

Result: 在28纳米CMOS工艺中合成的8位乘法器变体，在INT8 DNN工作负载下将面积功耗乘积降低高达56%，在穷举活动下降低93%。在CNN和DeiT中，在最坏情况误差(WCE)不超过1%的预算下，微调后相对于FP32的精度损失保持在2%以下。CircuitsDNA消除了8/12/16位乘法器中传统方法出现的所有搜索停滞，自适应变异比非自适应版本提供高达1.33倍的收敛加速。

Conclusion: CircuitsDNA通过进化方法成功实现了多精度可配置算术电路的自动化设计，在效率、精度和搜索可靠性方面均优于现有方法，为边缘AI中的近似计算提供了灵活高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Emerging edge AI workloads increasingly require arithmetic units that can trade computational accuracy for efficiency on demand. However, existing approximate arithmetic circuits are typically fixed-accuracy or rely on predefined structures for runtime configurability. This work introduces CircuitsDNA, an evolutionary framework that automatically evolves accuracy-configurable arithmetic circuits supporting multiple accuracy modes within a single circuit. It integrates three key features: 1) multi-threshold verifiability miter to enforce mode-specific accuracy requirements, 2) resource-limited verifiability-driven search to reduce verification overhead without sacrificing correctness, enabling efficient exploration of large circuit design, and 3) feedback-driven adaptive mutation to prioritize effective structural modifications and accelerate search convergence. Experimental results show that the 8-bit multiplier variants synthesized in 28-nm CMOS reduce the area-power product by up to 56% on INT8 DNN workload and 93% under exhaustive activity, compared with an exact 8-bit multiplier. Across CNNs and DeiTs, the accuracy loss relative to FP32 remains below 2% after fine-tuning under worst-case error (WCE) budgets of at most 1%. CircuitsDNA eliminates all search stalls observed in conventional methods across 8/12/16-bit multipliers, while adaptive mutation provides up to 1.33 times faster convergence than its non-adaptive counterpart.

</details>


### [181] [Reinforcement learning to choose optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01811)
*Martin van der Schelling,Deepesh Toshniwal,Miguel A. Bessa*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出强化学习框架RL2Opt，将优化器选择视为序列决策问题：循环策略在每个决策点同时决定选择哪个优化器及使用时长，基于经验运行时分布进行训练，在未见问题上超越所有组合内单一优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一优化方法无法对所有问题普遍最优，且最佳优化器在运行过程中可能动态变化。现有动态切换方法存在局限：组合被限制在单一算法类、切换时机固定、或决策频率被当作超参数而非学习对象。

Method: 将优化算法选择形式化为顺序决策问题。循环策略在每个决策点读取当前运行状态，同时决定下一次使用的优化器及其持续时间。组合同时包含基于梯度和无导数优化器，切换时传递当前最佳解与代表性步长。通过上下文代理调节专家头的门控网络，采用解耦的actor-critic训练，回报函数与评估时使用的经验运行时分布指标一致。训练任务与组合联合设计，确保无单一优化器占优。

Result: 在未见问题上，学习的策略在所有预算（除最小预算外）下优于组合中每一个优化器，并且在分布偏移下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 学习到的运行时优化器切换策略能够超越单一最优优化器，并对外推场景保持稳健，验证了将优化器选择作为可学习顺序决策问题的可行性。

Abstract: No single optimization method is uniformly best for all problems, and the most suitable optimizer choice can change during a run. Existing approaches that change optimizer during execution typically predetermine part of the strategy: the portfolio is restricted to one algorithm class, the switch occurs once at a fixed time, or the frequency of decisions is treated as a hyperparameter rather than a learned one. We introduce "Reinforcement Learning to Choose Optimizers", which formulates the optimization algorithm choice as a sequential decision-making problem. At each decision, a recurrent policy reads the current run state and decides both which optimizer should be used next and for how long. The portfolio includes both gradient-based and derivative-free optimizers, and each switch passes on the current best solution and a representative step size. A context proxy conditions a gating network over expert heads, and training employs a decoupled actor-critic whose return is expressed in the same empirical runtime distribution metric used at evaluation. Training tasks and portfolio are designed jointly so that no optimizer dominates. On unseen problems, the learned policy outperforms every portfolio optimizer at all but the smallest budgets, and it remains robust under distribution shift.

</details>


### [182] [Memory as an Energy Landscape---Hopfield](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02195)
*Nima Dehghani*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文重构Hopfield网络，将其从早期神经网络算法提升为记忆的物理理论，全面推导了其能量函数、检索机制及现代变体（含与注意力机制的联系），并严谨区分了容量声明背后的系综、规模与判据差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要对Hopfield网络进行系统性理论重构，澄清其作为物理记忆理论的历史地位与当代意义，并消解广泛存在的关于存储容量声明的误解与冲突。

Method: 采用理论重构与分析的方法：梳理1982年之前的前置工作，推导二元与分级响应的能量函数，给出信号-串扰分解、平均场检索理论及零温检索旋节线（alpha≈0.138），并追踪能量程序在模拟优化网络、多项式/指数密集联想记忆及现代连续Hopfield更新中的演化，同时澄清更新成为缩放点积注意力的精确条件。

Result: 将Hopfield网络呈现为一种有效理论，阐明其对称性、局域性与点神经元假设限定了生物学适用边界；系统衔接了经典结论与现代注意力机制发展；固定种子数值实验揭示机制但不等同于解析结果。

Conclusion: Hopfield网络的能量框架作为一种有效物理理论，其确立的成果与存续原理应区别于受假设约束的局限与开放问题；数值上不同的存储上限因系综、缩放极限和成功判据不同而无需冲突。

Abstract: This chapter reconstructs the Hopfield network as a physical theory of memory rather than merely an early neural-network algorithm. It begins with the problem as it stood before 1982-threshold logic, Hebbian association, correlation memories, and recurrent binary networks-and isolates what Hopfield's synthesis added: a dynamical definition of content-addressable memory, a symmetric recurrent architecture with a Lyapunov function, a Hebbian embedding of patterns in its couplings, and a physical account of basins, robustness, and graceful degradation. The binary and graded-response energy functions are derived in full, together with the signal-crosstalk decomposition governing pattern stability, the mean-field theory of retrieval at extensive load, and the zero-temperature retrieval spinodal at (alpha 0.138) established by Amit, Gutfreund, and Sompolinsky. The energy-based program is then followed through analog optimization networks, polynomial dense associative memories, exponential interactions, and modern continuous Hopfield updates, including the precise conditions under which the update becomes scaled dot-product attention. Throughout, capacity claims are tied to their disorder ensemble, scaling limit, and success criterion, showing why numerically different storage limits need not conflict. A closing assessment distinguishes established results from surviving principles, assumption-bound limitations, and open problems, treating the Hopfield network as an effective theory whose symmetry, locality, and point-neuron assumptions delimit its biological reach. Fixed-seed numerical experiments expose the mechanisms discussed but do not substitute for analytical results.

</details>


### [183] [LLM-Driven Joint Evolution of Coupled Heuristics Components for Routing Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02353)
*Juntao Wei,Yangming Zhou,Zhibin Jiang,Shan Jiang*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM-HCJG框架，通过大语言模型联合生成并协同进化相互依赖的启发式组件（而非孤立组件），应用于引导式局部搜索（GLS），并将结果从TSP迁移到CVRP。在41个公开基准上取得领先效果，理论分析证明组件间存在不可分离的状态转换效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合优化的启发式设计依赖专家经验，现有LLM增强的进化方法通常演化孤立的算法组件，忽略了组件间在搜索状态上的相互依赖关系（一个组件决定另一个组件操作的搜索状态），导致生成的质量和一致性受限。

Method: 提出LLM-HCJG（LLM驱动的启发式组件联合生成）框架：采用基于种群的方法，在共享设计蓝图下联合生成并协同进化相互依赖的启发式组件。应用于GLS时，将解初始化与惩罚构建耦合，并将生成的组件对嵌入增强的在线搜索机制中；同时将结果从TSP迁移到CVRP。

Result: 在合成实例及41个公开的TSPLIB/CVRPLIB基准上取得持续低的最优性差距，包括在29个TSPLIB实例中的28个和全部12个CVRPLIB实例上取得最佳或并列最佳结果。消融和结构分析表明，性能提升来源于跨组件兼容性与对齐，而非孤立组件的重组。

Conclusion: 理论分析建立了两个组件间不可分离的状态转换效应及生成一致性优势。实验结果表明，在有限样本和适度训练成本下，该方法在所评估的路由设置中支持有效的跨实例迁移。

Abstract: Heuristic design for combinatorial optimization remains heavily reliant on expert knowledge, while existing large language model (LLM)-enhanced evolutionary methods typically evolve isolated algorithmic components, even when one determines the search state on which another operates. This paper proposes LLM-driven Heuristic Components Joint Generation (LLM-HCJG), a population-based framework that jointly generates and co-evolves interdependent heuristic components under a shared design blueprint. Applied to guided local search (GLS), LLM-HCJG couples solution initialization with penalty construction and embeds the generated pair into an enhanced online search mechanism. The resulting form is further transferred from the traveling salesman problem (TSP) to the capacitated vehicle routing problem (CVRP). Theoretical analysis establishes the non-separable state-transition effects between the two components and the advantage in generation consistency. Across synthetic instances and 41 public TSPLIB/CVRPLIB benchmarks, LLM-HCJG attains consistently low optimality gaps, including best or tied-best results on 28 of 29 TSPLIB instances and all 12 CVRPLIB instances. Ablation and structural analyses further indicate that these gains are associated with cross-component compatibility and alignment rather than isolated-component recombination. These results support effective cross-instance transfer within the evaluated routing settings under limited-sample, modest-cost training.

</details>


### [184] [Semantics-Guided Automatic Tensorization for Multiobjective Evolutionary Algorithms: A Multi-Agent Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02387)
*Zhenyu Liang,Beichen Huang,Bowen Zheng,Ran Cheng*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoCoCo，一个多智能体框架，用于将多目标进化算法(MOEA)从CPU顺序程序结构自动张量化迁移到现代张量计算平台（如GPU），通过语义引导的计算重构实现，在48个MOEA基准上验证了迁移可靠性、优化保真度和计算可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MOEA天然具有种群级并行性，但现有成熟实现在CPU上采用顺序程序结构编码，直接代码翻译无法有效利用现代张量计算平台，需要进行不改变优化机制的重构。

Method: 将自动张量化形式化为语义引导的计算重构，开发EvoCoCo多智能体框架：重构算法特定状态、依赖、算子和更新逻辑为结构化语义表示，通过共享张量化蓝图组织，专业化变换分支探索替代张量实现，执行反馈引导验证、修复和候选选择。

Result: 在48个MOEA基准上，EvoCoCo迁移可靠性高于直接单次翻译；88.2%的有效比较满足优化保真准则；GPU加速随种群规模或决策维度增长，中位加速比从22.6倍（种群缩放）到80.2倍（决策维度缩放）；外部源和消融研究评估了主基准之外的迁移和主要框架组件的作用。

Conclusion: EvoCoCo通过语义引导的多智能体协作实现了MOEA的高质量自动张量化，验证了张量化实现不仅能保持优化行为，还能在GPU上获得显著的随规模增长的加速效果。

Abstract: Multiobjective evolutionary algorithms (MOEAs) naturally expose population-level parallelism, but many mature implementations encode their computation in sequential program structures designed for central processing units. Exploiting modern tensor computing platforms therefore requires more than direct code translation: the implementation must be restructured without changing the defining optimization mechanism of the underlying MOEA. We formulate automatic tensorization for MOEAs as semantics-guided computational restructuring and develop Evolutionary Code Conversion (EvoCoCo), a multi-agent framework that realizes this formulation. EvoCoCo reconstructs algorithm-specific states, dependencies, operators, and update logic into a structured semantic representation and organizes them through a shared tensorization blueprint. Specialized transformation branches then explore alternative tensor realizations, while execution feedback guides validation, repair, and candidate selection. Experiments on a benchmark of 48 MOEAs evaluate migration reliability, optimization fidelity, and computational scalability. Under matched large language model backends, EvoCoCo attains higher migration reliability than direct one-shot translation. Across the benchmark suites, 88.2% of valid comparisons satisfy the predefined optimization-fidelity criterion. The tensorized implementations also exhibit increasing acceleration on graphics processing units as population size or decision dimension grows, with median measured speedups ranging from $22.6\times$ under population scaling to $80.2\times$ under decision-dimension scaling. External-source and ablation studies further assess transfer beyond the main benchmark and the roles of the major framework components.

</details>


### [185] [Model-Free Surrogate-Assisted Neural Architecture Search for Evolving Variable-Length Dense Blocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02460)
*Asif Ameer,Maryam Bashir,Irfan Younas,Muhammad Fayyaz*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无模型替代PSO网络（MFSPNet），用于高效进化CNN架构。该方法将轻量级的无模型替代预测器集成到粒子群优化框架中，避免了预训练替代模型的需求，并提出验证损失驱动的指数移动平均估计器（VLE-EMA）和基于块状的密集连接策略两个关键贡献，在多个数据集上以更低的计算成本取得了有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经架构搜索（NAS）虽能自动设计深度神经网络，但其高昂的计算成本限制了实际应用。现有的基于替代模型的方法（尤其是基于模型的替代器）需要训练大量候选架构并引入额外的优化开销，亟需一种更高效的架构评估与搜索方案。

Method: 提出MFSPNet框架：(1) 将模型无关的轻量替代预测器集成到粒子群优化（PSO）中，消除对预训练替代模型的需求；(2) 引入验证损失驱动的指数移动平均估计器（VLE-EMA）来捕捉早期泛化行为，实现可靠的架构排序；(3) 采用块状密集连接策略，支持进化块的堆叠并缓解梯度消失问题，同时增强跨数据集的可迁移性。

Result: 在一致训练协议（十次独立运行）下，CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和SVHN上分别达到3.91%、17.68%和1.91%的错误率；ImageNet上top-1/top-5错误率为28.29%/12.82%（单次运行），架构搜索所需GPU时间少于三天。

Conclusion: MFSPNet为成本敏感的神经架构搜索提供了高效且可靠的框架，在显著降低计算开销的同时保持了有竞争力的性能，展现出良好的可扩展性和跨数据集迁移能力。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for automatically designing deep neural networks; however, its practical adoption is often limited by substantial computational cost. To alleviate expensive full-training evaluations, surrogate-based methods have been introduced to estimate network performance efficiently. Nevertheless, existing approaches-particularly model-based surrogates-require training many candidate architectures and involve additional optimization overhead. In this work, we propose a Model-Free Surrogate PSO Network (MFSPNet) for evolving convolutional neural network architectures. The proposed method integrates a lightweight model-free surrogate predictor within a particle swarm optimization (PSO) framework, eliminating the need for pre-trained surrogate models. Specifically, MFSPNet introduces two key contributions: (1) a validation-loss-driven exponential moving average estimator (VLE-EMA) that captures early generalization behavior for reliable architecture ranking; and (2) a block-based dense connection strategy that enables effective stacking of evolved blocks while mitigating vanishing-gradient issues. This design also facilitates transferability of learned blocks across datasets. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MFSPNet achieves competitive performance with reduced computational cost. Under a consistent training protocol with ten independent runs, the proposed method attains error rates of 3.91%, 17.68%, and 1.91% on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and SVHN, respectively, along with top-1/top-5 error rates of 28.29%/12.82% on ImageNet, while requiring less than three GPU days for architecture search. Due to computational constraints, the ImageNet result is based on a single run and should be interpreted as indicative of scalability. Overall, MFSPNet provides an efficient and reliable framework for cost-aware neural architecture search.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [Barriers to Using Static Application Security Testing (SAST) Tools: A Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01669)
*Zachary Wadhams,Clemente Izurieta,Ann Marie Reinhold*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文调研了近期文献，探讨开发者不愿采用静态应用安全测试（SAST）工具的原因，指出开发者面临工具固有和工具缺陷两类可用性问题，并主张推动SAST广泛采纳需要开发者投入与技术工具简化双管齐下，兼顾技术与人为因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件漏洞是安全事件的主要原因，SAST工具虽有潜力提升应用安全，但许多开发团队并未采纳。理解开发者对SAST的抵触情绪及其使用中遇到的具体问题是推动安全实践普及的关键。

Method: 对近期文献进行系统性调研（survey），梳理并分类开发者在SAST使用过程中遇到的可用性问题。

Result: 发现了多种SAST可用性问题，将其分为两类：一类是工具固有问题，需要开发者一定的投入；另一类是工具本身的缺陷，需要工具创建者改进。

Conclusion: 要克服SAST采纳的主要障碍，需要同时关注技术与人为因素。开发者需接受必要的投资，而SAST工具创建者应简化工具使用流程，两者结合才能促进SAST的广泛采用和持续使用。

Abstract: Developers face a challenging problem with no clear solution. Modern software breaches can wreak havoc on businesses and individuals alike. With code vulnerabilities being a leading cause, securing applications must be a priority for developers. Static Application Security Testing (SAST) has the potential to harden applications by assisting in the identification and resolution of security vulnerabilities. Despite this, many development teams have not adopted SAST tools into their environment. In this paper, we survey the recent literature to uncover why some developers are apprehensive towards SAST and identify what specific problems they encounter when using it. We found a variety of usability problems developers face when using SAST. Some are inherent of the tool and ultimately require some level of developer investment while others are tool shortcomings that SAST tool creators must address. Ultimately, we argue that in order to drive widespread adoption and consistent SAST usage, developers will need to embrace that some investment is required. Simultaneously, developers will be more likely to integrate SAST tools into their workflows if the creators of SAST tools simplify many aspects related to tool usage. Surmounting the primary obstacles preventing the adoption of SAST requires full consideration of both the technical and human factors.

</details>


### [187] [RosettaBitcoin: An Artifact-Backed Experience Report on Verification Infrastructure for Agent-Assisted Consensus Validators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01702)
*Donavon Guyot*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是一篇经验报告，研究了一个名为RosettaBitcoin的单开发者项目——该项目构建了十二个独立实现的Bitcoin testnet4共识验证器。通过对2026年6月17日的不可变软件快照进行分析，研究者检视了其SQLite证据数据库、工件索引、一致性测试、验证脚本、阻塞记录和版本历史，评估了该项目中验证证据的真实边界及其可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体辅助软件项目通常通过演示或聚合基准来报告成果，但这些报告方式掩盖了正确性声明是如何被承认和验证的。本文旨在通过深入分析真实项目快照中的证据基础设施，揭示验证声明的实际覆盖范围与局限，探讨如何让智能体辅助系统更具可审计性。

Method: 对RosettaBitcoin项目的不可变快照（DOI 10.5281/zenodo.20738249）进行系统性分析，检查其SQLite证据数据库、策划工件索引、一致性测试装置、验证脚本、阻塞记录和版本历史，逐端口评估验证覆盖范围（包括脚本证据、区块基线和多阶段验证通道），并分析额外诊断补充材料。

Result: 在快照时刻，所有十二个端口均拥有45/45脚本语料库证明和严格的5,000区块基线；九个端口具有规范的最新区块验证通道（50,000块、100,000块及之后）。但没有任何端口拥有从空状态到最新区块的完整证明，也没有端口达到该项目自身的二进制全节点门槛；Docker和实时节点能力仍有缺口。历史记录中Zig从零到50,000区块约需3小时17分57秒，但作者指出这些时间间隔描述的任务不具等价性，不能用于估算工作量、生产力或因果性。一个纯Mojo加密后端的诊断补充证据虽验证到140,234高度并通过多项测试，但被标注为非规范、不可比且类别受限。

Conclusion: 该项目展示的显式失败记录、固定装置、端口自有证明和验证导入机制，可提升智能体辅助系统的可审计性。但要判断此类基础设施是否从因果上改善开发成果，仍需对照消融实验和外部复现研究。

Abstract: Agent-assisted software projects are often reported through demonstrations or aggregate benchmarks that conceal how correctness claims were admitted. This experience report studies RosettaBitcoin, a single-developer project that built twelve separately implemented Bitcoin testnet4 consensus validators, through its immutable 17 June 2026 software snapshot (DOI 10.5281/zenodo.20738249). We analyze the snapshot's tracked SQLite evidence database, curated artifact index, conformance fixtures, validation scripts, blocker records, and version history. At the snapshot, all twelve ports had port-owned 45/45 script-corpus proofs and strict 5,000-block baselines. Nine had canonical clean 50,000-block, 100,000-block, and post-100,000 validation lanes. Java had one 19.86-second near-tip maintenance artifact. No port had an empty-state-to-tip proof, and no port satisfied the project's binary full-node gate; Docker and live-node capability gaps remained.
  The artifact history also records a 3 h 17 min 57 s Zig scaffold-to-50,000 span, but the observed intervals describe non-equivalent tasks and cannot estimate effort, productivity, or causality. A separate diagnostic supplement preserves evidence that a pure-Mojo cryptographic backend validated fresh state to height 100,000 and resumed to 140,234, agreed on a 45-case shadow comparison, rejected six crafted invalid classes, and was killed by three targeted mutations. That evidence is noncanonical, noncomparable, and class-bounded. The case suggests that explicit failure records, fixtures, port-owned proofs, and validating imports can make agent-assisted systems more auditable. Controlled ablations and external replications are needed to test whether such infrastructure causally improves development outcomes.

</details>


### [188] [Harness Engineering in LLM Tool Use via Agent-Native Reusable Tool Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01736)
*Haibo Jin,Suijin Wang,Xucheng Yu,Haojing Luo,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出Tool Primitives（以自然语言为接口替代刚性API schema的工具调用设计）、ToolFace（包含25,519个函数的集中式工具仓库，运行时动态检索）以及HEART（编排框架，含Planner、Router、Verifier），在五个基准和50个真实任务上显著超越前沿商业模型并大幅降低API成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM+外部工具的方法面临两大挑战：(1) 多步/多轮推理脆弱——工具输出类型不兼容且API schema不匹配；(2) 大型工具目录下性能退化。需要新的工具设计范式来解决这些问题。

Method: 提出三个核心组件：1) Tool Primitives：以自然语言作为工具调用接口，每个工具内嵌LLM接口处理schema解析和执行，支持嵌套和自然语言层面互操作；2) ToolFace：集中式工具仓库（25,519个函数），推理时动态检索相关工具而非在上下文中枚举全部API schema；3) HEART：编排框架，由Planner（规划）、Router（路由）、Verifier（验证）组成，支持动态工具调用规划、多步执行和基于反馈的恢复。

Result: 在五个基准上，HEART比SFT模型平均高10%，比GPT-5.4、Claude-4.6-Sonnet、Gemini-3.1-Pro平均高6%，API成本降低达85%；在50个真实任务上达到84%任务完成率，是三个前沿商业模型平均值（22%）的3.8倍。

Conclusion: 通过自然语言工具接口替代刚性API schema，配合动态工具检索与三模块编排架构，能有效解决工具调用中的多步推理脆弱性和大规模工具目录适配问题，在性能与成本上均取得显著优势。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) augmented with external tools have demonstrated remarkable capability in solving complex real-world tasks. However, existing approaches suffer from two key challenges: brittle multi-step and multi-turn reasoning caused by incompatible tool output types and API schemas, and performance degradation under large tool catalogues. To address these, we introduce \textbf{Tool Primitives}, a design that replaces rigid API schema-based invocation with natural language as the interface for tool calling, where each tool is wrapped with an LLM interface that handles schema resolution and execution internally, enabling natural inter-tool communication for nested and multi-turn tool calling. Building on Tool Primitives, we host \textbf{ToolFace}, a centralized repository of 25,519 functions from which LLMs dynamically retrieve only the relevant tools at inference time, eliminating the need to enumerate raw API schemas in context. To orchestrate Tool Primitives and ToolFace reliably in complex settings, we further propose \textbf{HEART}, a \textbf{H}arness \textbf{E}ngineering framework via \textbf{A}gent-native, \textbf{R}eusable \textbf{T}ool Primitives, comprising a Planner, Router, and Verifier that jointly support dynamic tool invocation planning, multi-step execution, and feedback-driven recovery.
  Experiments on five benchmarks demonstrate that HEART outperforms SFT-based models by $10\%$ on average and surpasses GPT-5.4, Claude-4.6-Sonnet, and Gemini-3.1-Pro by $6\%$ on average while reducing API cost by up to $85\%$. On 50 real-world tasks, HEART achieves $84\%$ task completion, $3.8\times$ the average of three frontier commercial models ($22\%$).

</details>


### [189] [From Silicon to Boot Code: Extending Automated Program Repair to Firmware-Layer Security Workarounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01769)
*Maisha Mastora,Dean Sullivan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究将原本用于RTL（寄存器传输级）设计的自动化程序修复（APR）方法论扩展到固件层（UEFI/EDK II），通过commit聚类挖掘修复模板、构建四个定位器，在缺失推测屏障、数组边界检查、返回栈缓冲填充和整数溢出保护等bug上达到100%召回率，验证了该方法论可跨硬件到固件层推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有APR研究局限于设计阶段（RTL或HLS层面），一旦芯片流片后出现硬件漏洞（post-silicon），补丁只能手工生成——现有自动化只解决补丁部署而非补丁合成。作者希望探索能否将成熟的RTL修复方法论（dictionary-guided, localize-synthesize-validate）迁移到固件层面，填补这一自动化空白。

Method: 采用三阶段框架：1）自动化commit聚类挖掘器分析EDK II固件仓库全部提交历史，不依赖已知CVE标识即可提取重复修复模板；2）基于真实修复证据构建四个独立定位器（C语言的Spectre v1缺失推测屏障、解压库缺失边界检查、x86汇编的返回栈缓冲填充缺失、Hand-Off Block创建代码的整数溢出防护缺失）；3）通过held-out测试、独立构建第五个字典条目（CVE-2018-3630）和朴素句法基线对比来验证方法的泛化性。

Result: commit聚类挖掘器恢复了三个已知CVE修复活动并发现两个额外候选bug家族。四个定位器全部达到100%召回率；C语言家族精度为2.1-15.5%，汇编和HOB家族精度达100%。根因分析表明C语言家族77-90%的误报源于两种过程内因素，跨过程别名分析差距实测为15-20%。held-out测试证明Spectre v1定位在未见文件上保持100%召回；第5个独立构建的条目证明可低成本扩展到新bug签名；朴素句法基线最多召回14%而该方法召回100%。

Conclusion: 该方法论成功从RTL层扩展到固件层，验证了"硬件到固件正确性生命周期"统一研究议程的可行性。C语言家族的精度瓶颈定位为过程内问题而非跨过程别名分析，为后续优化指明了方向。

Abstract: Automated program repair (APR) research has been constrained to design time. Current techniques localize and fix bugs in RTL or HLS designs before a chip reaches production. Once a hardware vulnerability surfaces post-silicon, the patch must be manually generated: existing automation addresses patch deployment but not patch synthesis. We study the feasibility of extending a dictionary-guided, localize-synthesize-validate APR methodology originally developed for RTL repair to this firmware layer. An automated commit-clustering miner surfaces recurring fix templates across the EDK II (UEFI) firmware repository's full commit history without depending on known CVE identifiers, recovering all three known CVE-fix campaigns and surfacing two additional candidate bug families. Grounded in real fix evidence, we build four independent localizers: missing speculation barriers in C (CVE-2017-5753, Spectre v1), missing bounds checks before array writes in C (a decompression library CVE), missing Return Stack Buffer stuffing in x86 assembly (CVE-2017-5715), and missing integer-overflow guards in Hand-Off Block creation code (surfaced by the miner itself). All four achieve 100% recall; precision ranges from 2.1-15.5% on the C families to 100% on the assembly and HOB families. Root-cause analysis of the C-family false positives attributes 77-90% to two intra-procedural causes, isolating the inter-procedural alias-analysis gap as a measured 15-20% rather than an estimate. A held-out test confirms Spectre v1 localization holds at 100% recall on unseen files; a fifth, independently built dictionary entry (CVE-2018-3630) shows the methodology extends to a new bug signature at low cost; and a naive syntactic baseline recalls at most 14% where our detector recalls 100%. We frame these results within a broader research agenda for a unified hardware-to-firmware correctness lifecycle.

</details>


### [190] [Modelstamp: Pre-Deserialization Verification of Machine-Learning Artifacts and Runtime Environment State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01781)
*Anagha Dhekne*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Modelstamp是一个轻量级Python持久化库，在反序列化前验证机器学习模型的工件完整性和运行时环境状态。它使用SHA-256摘要、运行时元数据和受限软件包版本的sidecar JSON清单，支持可选的HMAC认证。在14个环境漂移场景、8个信任边界场景及10 MiB到1 GiB基准测试中验证了其效果（验证时间0.032s至3.334s，吞吐量约307-312 MiB/s）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已持久化的机器学习模型在加载的软件环境演进时可能保持字节完全一致，这造成了一个仅靠工件完整性检查无法暴露的验证问题。

Method: 持久化时将模型工件与包含SHA-256摘要、运行时元数据及有界跟踪包集合中安装版本的sidecar JSON清单关联；单独记录的模型相关子集决定哪些包版本参与漂移比较。可选HMAC认证支持共享密钥工作流。验证时在反序列化前将工件与表示的当前环境同记录证据比对。

Result: 在14个受控环境漂移场景中按预期表现（相关依赖变化、不变环境和无关环境变化均正确响应）；8个信任边界场景确认了预期检测和预期局限（含共享密钥伪造与重放）。中位验证时间从10 MiB的0.032s增至1 GiB的3.334s，吞吐量约307-312 MiB/s。

Conclusion: Modelstamp被定性为反序列化前参考状态验证的互补控制手段，而非替代依赖管理系统、恶意模型检测、安全反序列化或公共发布者认证的方案。

Abstract: Persisted machine-learning models can remain byte-identical while the software environments in which they are loaded evolve, creating a verification problem that artifact integrity checks alone cannot expose. This paper presents Modelstamp, a lightweight Python persistence library for verifying artifact integrity and represented runtime-environment state before deserialization. At persistence time, Modelstamp associates a serialized artifact with a sidecar JSON manifest containing a SHA-256 digest, runtime metadata, and installed versions from a bounded tracked-package set; a separately recorded model-relevant subset determines which package versions participate in drift comparison. Optional HMAC authentication supports workflows in which the producer and verifier share a secret key. At verification time, the artifact and represented current environment are checked against this recorded evidence before the model is deserialized. Modelstamp is evaluated using 14 controlled environment-drift scenarios, eight controlled trust-boundary scenarios, and an artifact-size scaling benchmark from 10 MiB to 1 GiB. The controlled drift experiments behaved as specified across relevant dependency changes, unchanged environments, and unrelated environmental changes, including broader noise controls. The trust-boundary experiments similarly confirmed both intended detections and expected limitations, including shared-key forgery and replay. Median verification time increased from 0.032 s at 10 MiB to 3.334 s at 1 GiB, with measured throughput of approximately 307-312 MiB/s in the benchmark environment. These results characterize Modelstamp as a complementary pre-deserialization reference-state verification control rather than as a replacement for dependency-management systems, malicious-model detection, safe deserialization, or public publisher authentication.

</details>


### [191] [ExecRetrieval: Measuring the Functional-Correctness Gap in Code-Embedding Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01865)
*Aaryan Kapoor,Md Abdullah Al Hafiz Khan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ExecRetrieval基准，包含939个Python任务，每个任务配有一个执行验证的正确实现和最多四个执行验证的有缺陷干扰项（通过单次编辑变异生成）。评估23种稠密嵌入配置及BM25后发现，即使顶级系统在exec@10达到1.00，exec@1也仅为0.331，说明现有嵌入在功能上难以区分正确代码与近克隆但有缺陷的代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码检索基准未在搜索池中植入受控的、执行验证的单一编辑变异体（即近克隆但有缺陷的正确实现的变体），因此无法回答嵌入是否能功能性地区分正确代码与近克隆但错误代码的问题。研究者需要一个搜索池本身包含相关反事实的基准，直接测试检索器的排名排序是否具备功能判别力而非仅仅是主题或身份重叠。

Method: 构建ExecRetrieval基准：939个Python任务，每个任务与一个执行验证的正确实现以及最多四个执行验证的有缺陷干扰项配对，每个干扰项通过机械变异进行单次定向编辑生成。评估23种稠密嵌入配置及BM25，使用provider原生调用，配合配对McNemar检验和查询级bootstrap区间进行统计分析。

Result: 在池中包含近克隆反事实时，顶级托管系统达到exec@10=1.00但exec@1仅0.331；排名第一的漏检中，91.5%-99.4%是配对的缺陷变异体（四个领先系统范围）；正确实现在67-78%的查询中得分低于至少一个配对的干扰项（领先系统）。

Conclusion: 现有嵌入系统在功能性区分任务上表现糟糕——它们能在足够大的候选中找到正确代码（exec@10=1.00），但无法在首位优先返回正确实现，说明嵌入检索本质上偏向主题/身份相似性而非执行正确性，这严重制约了编码代理和检索增强代码生成的实际效果。

Abstract: Embedding-based code retrieval is a core component of coding agents and retrieval-augmented code generation, where retrieving correct code matters more than retrieving lexically similar code. Existing code-retrieval benchmarks do not plant controlled, execution-verified single-edit variants of each query's canonical implementation in the search pool, leaving the question of whether embeddings can functionally discriminate correct from near-clone-but-incorrect code unanswered in a retrieval setting. Resolving this requires a benchmark whose search pool itself contains the relevant counterfactuals -- execution-verified buggy variants near-identical to each canonical -- so that a retriever's rank ordering can be directly tested for functional discrimination rather than topical or identity overlap. We introduce ExecRetrieval, 939 Python tasks each paired with one execution-verified canonical implementation and up to four execution-verified buggy distractors, each generated by a mechanical mutation making a single targeted edit, and evaluate 23 dense embedding configurations plus BM25 under provider-native invocation with paired McNemar tests and query-level bootstrap intervals. With near-clone counterfactuals in the pool, the top hosted system reaches exec@10 = 1.00 but only exec@1 = 0.331; rank-1 misses are paired buggy variants 91.5-99.4% of the time across the four leading systems, and the canonical scores below at least one of its four paired distractors in 67-78% of queries on the leading systems. The full dataset, execution oracle, embedding matrices, environment snapshot, and pairwise statistical tests are released at the URL in Appendix D.

</details>


### [192] [From Prompting to Engineering: A Research Agenda for Prompt Engineering in Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02248)
*Vincenzo De Martino,Giovanna Broccia,Fabiano Pecorelli,Jennifer Horkoff,Riccardo Coppola,Antonino Ferraro,Quim Motger,Emma McKenzie,Shahbaz Siddeeq*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文总结了在PROMPT-SE研讨会（与EASE 2026合办）上关于提示工程在软件工程中应用的社区讨论，识别出五大研究领域，并提出研究议程以推动提示工程从临时性实践走向系统化、可维护、可评估、可追溯和可治理的软件工程实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示工程已广泛用于软件工程各活动（需求分析、编码、测试、文档、仓库分析、规划），但提示及相关指令工件通常以任务特定和非正式的方式创建和演变，缺乏系统化的评估、管理、追溯和治理支持。本文旨在探讨软件工程领域如何助力这些实践走向成熟。

Method: 在PROMPT-SE研讨会（与EASE 2026合办）组织结构化社区讨论，参与者围绕当前提示实践、其在采纳与评估中面临的挑战，以及提示工程融入软件开发中的未来方向进行讨论，并将讨论成果综合归纳为五大领域。

Result: 综合讨论形成五大关注领域：（1）提示工件与标准化；（2）评估与基准测试；（3）生命周期集成；（4）人机协作与技能；（5）治理、隐私与技术债务。并基于这些领域勾勒出研究议程。

Conclusion: 提示工程需要从以临时交互为主的方式，转向更系统化、可维护、可评估、可追溯且可治理的软件工程实践，这为软件工程社区指明了未来的研究方向。

Abstract: Prompt engineering is increasingly used across Software Engineering (SE) activities, including requirements analysis, coding, testing, documentation, repository analysis, and planning. Yet prompts and related instruction artifacts are often created and evolved through task-specific and informal practices, with limited support for their systematic evaluation, management, traceability, and governance. To examine how SE can contribute to the maturation of these practices, we organized a structured community discussion at the First International Workshop on Empirical Prompt Engineering for Software Engineering (PROMPT-SE), co-located with EASE 2026. Participants discussed current prompting practices, challenges to their adoption and evaluation, and future directions for integrating prompt engineering into software development. We synthesized these discussions into five areas: prompt artifacts and standardization; evaluation and benchmarking; lifecycle integration; human-AI collaboration and skills; and governance, privacy, and technical debt. Based on these areas, we outline a research agenda to move prompt engineering from predominantly ad hoc interactions toward more systematic, maintainable, evaluable, traceable, and governable SE practices.

</details>


### [193] [AgOSS: A Dataset and Multi-Layer Characterization of Open-Source Agricultural Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02591)
*Vatsal Dudhaiya,Mikhail Golovenchits,Aryan Banerjee,James C. Davis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出AgOSS数据集，包含66个农业开源软件仓库，横跨六个架构类别，通过OpenSSF Scorecard、治理指标、SBOM依赖分析和KEV匹配评估供应链安全，并与非农业对照项目比较。研究发现治理与继承依赖风险基本无关，且农业项目的低分主要由规模和成熟度混淆造成，而非农业领域特有风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业大量依赖开源软件，形成领域特定的软件供应链，但该生态系统的供应链安全态势缺乏实证研究。不清楚其安全态势是否与非农业软件不同，以及差异究竟源于农业领域本身还是项目规模与成熟度。

Method: 构建AgOSS数据集（66个仓库、六类架构），通过OpenSSF Scorecard、治理指标、SBOM依赖分析和KEV匹配评估安全性，并与匹配的非农业对照项目进行比较，辅以回归分析控制混淆因素。

Result: 两个主要发现：一是治理与继承依赖风险基本独立，Scorecard反映社区活跃度但与漏洞数量、密度和已知可利用漏洞数无关联；二是农业项目原始Scorecard得分远低，但规模和成熟度混淆了差距，匹配和回归后残余差异不再显著。

Conclusion: 保障农业开源生态安全应投资于贡献者能力和依赖卫生，而非制定农业特有的安全控制措施。

Abstract: Much of agriculture depends on open-source software spanning farm management platforms, cloud services, edge gateways, embedded systems, and field-deployed sensors, forming a domain-specific software supply chain that has drawn little empirical security attention. It is unknown whether this ecosystem's supply chain security posture differs from that of comparable non-agricultural software, and if it does, whether the difference reflects the agricultural domain or the size and maturity of the projects within it.
  As a step towards securing agricultural open-source software, we present AgOSS, a dataset of 66 repositories across six architectural categories. We assess supply chain security within the dataset via OpenSSF Scorecard, governance metrics, SBOM-based dependency analysis, and KEV matching, and compare against matched non-agricultural controls. We report two findings. First, governance is largely independent of inherited dependency risk. Scorecard tracks community activity, but we detect no association with vulnerability count, density, or known-exploited count, and in regression the exposure signal loads on architectural category rather than domain. Second, agricultural projects score far lower on raw Scorecard, but size and maturity confound the gap: the residual loses significance under matching and regression. Securing this ecosystem means investing in contributor capacity and dependency hygiene, not agriculture-specific controls.

</details>


### [194] [Automated Vulnerability Injection in Smart Contracts Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02624)
*Luca Migliaccio,Roberto Natella,Naghmeh Ivaki,Nuno Laranjeiro,Marco Vieira*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用大型语言模型（LLM）自动向Solidity智能合约注入漏洞的方法，并在OpenSCV的49种漏洞类型上进行案例研究。从SmartBugs真实合约出发，生成近1000个候选变体，经过去重和验证后得到32个确认含漏洞的合约（存活率16.58%），涵盖25种漏洞类型。作者用验证后的合约评估了三个静态分析器，揭示了它们互补但不完整的覆盖范围。结果表明基于LLM的漏洞注入可行，但在可扩展性和多样性方面存在明显局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估智能合约漏洞检测工具需要具有已知真实标签（ground truth）的数据集，但此类数据集稀缺且人工构建困难。现有的漏洞注入方法难以自动化和规模化，因此需要探索利用LLM自动注入漏洞的新途径。

Method: 利用LLM自动向Solidity智能合约注入漏洞，针对OpenSCV的49种漏洞类型。注入后的合约通过多步骤验证管道，依次检查编译、执行、业务逻辑和是否存在预期漏洞。在来自SmartBugs的真实合约上实施案例研究。

Result: LLM生成近1000个候选变体，经过去重和验证后32个合约确认含漏洞（存活率16.58%），涵盖25种漏洞类型。存活的合约集中在结构较简单的目标和局部语法模式的漏洞类型上。用这些合约评估三个静态分析器，呈现互补但不完整的覆盖特征。

Conclusion: LLM基于的漏洞注入方法在智能合约领域是可行的，但面临LLM非确定性和保持合约语义的困难等实际挑战。该方法在可扩展性和多样性方面仍有明显局限性，需要进一步改进。

Abstract: Assessing vulnerability detection tools for smart contracts requires datasets with known ground truth, yet such datasets are scarce and difficult to build by hand. We propose an approach that uses Large Language Models (LLMs) to automatically inject vulnerabilities into Solidity smart contracts, and demonstrate it in a case study targeting 49 vulnerability types from OpenSCV. Injected contracts are validated through a multi-step pipeline checking compilation, execution, business logic, and the presence of the intended vulnerability. Applied to real-world contracts from SmartBugs, LLMs generate nearly 1,000 candidate variants; after deduplication and validation, 32 confirmed vulnerable contracts spanning 25 vulnerability types survive (a 16.58% survival rate). Surviving contracts concentrate in structurally simpler targets and vulnerability types with localized syntactic patterns. We report practical challenges including LLMs' non-determinism and the difficulty of preserving contract semantics. We then use the validated contracts to assess three static analyzers, revealing complementary and incomplete coverage profiles. Results show that LLM-based vulnerability injection is feasible, while exposing key limitations in scalability and diversity.

</details>


### [195] [The Import Tax: A Longitudinal Measurement of Startup Cost in the Python Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02753)
*Trinath Sai Subhash Reddy Pittala*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对PyPI上500个最常下载的包进行了系统性的导入成本测量，覆盖5年、6个CPython版本、2个平台，共63,431次测量。发现导入成本高度倾斜（中位数6ms以下但P99达354ms），首次安装后导入成本高出3-22倍，子模块导入比顶层导入贵达294倍。均值年增长11-13%但中位数仅1.6-2.4%，且macOS上新解释器导入更慢，全局延迟导入模式（PEP 810）虽使导入近乎免费但破坏了414个顶级包中的8个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python程序每次进程启动都要为导入模块付出代价，这在命令行工具、测试工人和serverless冷启动场景中占主导地位，但稳态基准测试无法体现。Python 3.15将基于轶事证据添加显式延迟导入（PEP 810），缺乏对生态系统导入成本的系统性测量，需要填补这一空白以指导语言设计决策。

Method: 选取PyPI下载量最高的500个包，从五年发布中按季度采样，在6个CPython版本（3.9-3.14）和2个平台（Apple M5/macOS和Intel Xeon/Linux）上进行测量，总计63,431次测量。同时直接测量了Python 3.15的全局延迟导入模式的影响。

Result: 导入成本分布高度倾斜：半数包在6ms内完成导入，但P99达354ms；安装后首次导入成本是后续的3-22倍（字节码编译）；子模块导入成本最高可达顶层导入的294倍。中位数成本年增仅1.6-2.4%，但均值年增11-13%，增长集中于重尾。macOS上更新解释器导入相同代码慢1.16倍，Linux上无此现象；单点版本（3.11.5 vs 3.11.16）成本波动达1.34倍。全局延迟导入使导入语句近乎免费，但破坏了414个顶级包中的8个。

Conclusion: Python导入成本的系统测量揭示其分布高度不均匀且增长集中在尾部，现有基准研究对该问题严重低估。PEP 810的全局延迟导入虽显著改善导入性能但引入兼容性风险，需要权衡。测量数据和工具集可供研究人员复现和进一步研究。

Abstract: Python programs pay for their imports at every process start, a cost that is invisible in steady-state benchmarks but dominant for command-line tools, test workers, and serverless cold starts. Python 3.15 adds explicit lazy imports (PEP 810) largely on anecdotal evidence; no systematic measurement of the ecosystem's import cost exists. We present one: the 500 most-downloaded PyPI packages, sampled quarterly over five years of releases, measured under six CPython versions (3.9-3.14) on two platforms (Apple M5/macOS and Intel Xeon/Linux), for 63,431 measurements in total, plus direct measurement of 3.15's global lazy-import mode. Import cost is heavily skewed: half of packages import in under 6 ms, but the 99th percentile is 354 ms, the first import after installation costs 3-22x more (bytecode compilation), and importing a package's submodules costs up to 294x more than the top-level import that benchmarks report. The median package's cost grows only +1.6-2.4%/year, but the mean grows +11-13%/year: growth is concentrated in a heavy tail. Newer interpreters import the same code 1.16x slower on macOS, but not on Linux, and a single point release (3.11.5 vs. 3.11.16) swings cost by 1.34x. Global lazy mode makes import statements essentially free, yet breaks 8 of 414 top packages. Harness and dataset are available on request.

</details>


### [196] [Type Hints in Python Libraries and Frameworks: An Empirical Analysis of Adoption and Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02782)
*Thiago Roberto Magalhães,Fabio Petrillo,João Eduardo Montandon*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究分析了1,000个流行的GitHub仓库，发现91%的Python库至少使用一次类型提示，但采用不一致。系统性采用的库主要优先标注函数参数和返回类型，且倾向于使用更表达性的类型。类型提示主要作为API契约而非全面的实现描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管类型提示在Python中广泛可用，但对其在库和框架中的采用和维护方式了解甚少。理解这些实践对改进工具支持和指导开发者具有重要意义。

Method: 分析了1,000个流行的GitHub仓库，识别库和框架并提取类型注解。考察了注解覆盖率、位置和来源、跨git历史演化，以及开发者注解与Pyright推断类型之间的关系。

Result: 91%的库使用类型提示；参数中位覆盖率为45.8%，返回类型为35.9%；主要使用内置类型（73.0%）。注解修改后趋向更表达性的类型。开发者常注解Pyright可推断的类型，且注解往往简化推断类型。

Conclusion: 类型提示在Python库中主要作为API契约，而非实现细节的综合描述。研究建议工具应优先关注公共接口、识别有意义的注解变更，并支持维护者演化类型信息。

Abstract: Context: In Python, type hints allow developers to annotate variables and functions with explicit type information, improving code clarity and reliability. Although type hints are widely available, little is known about how they are adopted and maintained in libraries and frameworks. Objective: We investigate the adoption, usage, maintenance, and rationale of type hints in Python libraries and frameworks. Method: We analyzed 1,000 popular GitHub repositories, identifying libraries and frameworks and extracting their type annotations. We examined annotation coverage, the locations and origins of annotations, their evolution across git histories, and the relationship between developer annotations and types inferred by Pyright. Results: Of the analyzed repositories, 91% of libraries use type hints at least once, although adoption is inconsistent.
