TL;DR: 本文提出SkillGLoW（Global-Local Weave），一种围绕"程序性家族"（procedural family）构建的技能复用框架。它针对长时程任务中发现的问题：全局技能文档会退化为通用学科知识，而扁平的任务池会膨胀且条目实例绑定过强。SkillGLoW将任务本地技能聚合为程序性家族并压缩为去实例化的全局先验，实例细节按任务再生而非存储，通过提交门控确认真实执行验证不降级才加入库。在四个基准（数学推理、终端自动化、软件修复、具身控制）三个模型上，相比无技能基线在困难任务上平均提升17.2分，12个持续改进运行全部正向，库比每任务池紧凑3.6倍，并超越已发表的单文档优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM智能体自我改进主要通过两种形式复用文本技能：全局单一文档或扁平的任务条目池。但在长时程、任务间结构差异大的工作负载上，两者各自失效——全局文档退化为通用学科描述，任务池则膨胀且条目与产生它的实例强绑定无法迁移。现有证据多来自结构相似任务的领域，缺乏对异构长时程任务的正确复用单元研究。

Method: SkillGLoW以"程序性家族"（procedural family，共享解决程序的相似任务簇）为核心复用单元。具体包括：(1) 本地技能聚合：任务从自身执行中生成的技能被聚合成程序性家族；(2) 压缩为去实例化的全局先验，实例细节按任务再生而非存储；(3) 提交门控（commit gate），仅当真实执行显示先验不降级已部署库时才接纳。通过"全局-局部编织"在每任务再生的实例细节与全局程序性知识之间取得平衡。

Result: 在四个基准（数学推理、终端自动化、软件修复、具身控制）和三个模型上：(1) 先验在困难任务上比无技能基线平均提升17.2分；(2) 12个持续改进运行全部获得正向收益；(3) 加入本地再生后达到18.0提升；(4) 库每程序性家族仅保留一个先验，比每任务池紧凑3.6倍；(5) 同一协议下在21个单元中15个超越已发表的单文档优化器；(6) 未经修改，库将在未见ALFWorld任务上的成功率从73.9%提升至83.9%，证明跨实例迁移的是程序而非任务记忆。

Conclusion: 本文论证了在长时程异构任务中，缺失的复用单元是"由相关任务簇共享的解决程序"而非全局学科知识或单个任务实例记忆。SkillGLoW通过程序性家族聚合与去实例化全局先验，配合提交门控保证库质量，在多个领域与模型上实现稳定提升并保持库紧凑。跨实例迁移的是程序性知识而非任务记忆这一发现，为LLM智能体的技能持续改进提供了新的设计原则。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly self-improve by writing and reusing textual skills, kept either as one global document or as a flat pool of per-task entries, though most of the evidence comes from domains with structurally similar tasks. On long-horizon workloads where each task demands a different solution, the two forms fail in opposite ways: the document collapses into generic discipline, while the pool inflates and its entries stay bound to the instance that wrote them. We argue the missing unit of reuse is the solving procedure shared by a cluster of related tasks, and build SkillGLoW (Global-Local Weave) around it: the local skills a task writes from its own execution are aggregated into procedural families and compressed into de-instantiated global priors, while the instance detail they hold is regenerated per task rather than stored; a commit gate admits a prior only when real execution shows it does not degrade the deployed library. Across four benchmarks (mathematical reasoning, terminal automation, software repair, and embodied control) and three models, the priors gain 17.2 points (hard) over the no-skill baseline on average, with positive gains in all 12 continual-improvement runs, and 18.0 with local regeneration, while the library holds one prior per procedural family, 3.6x more compact than the per-task pool. Under the same protocol GLoW leads a published single-document optimizer on 15 of 21 cells. Unmodified, the library lifts success on unseen ALFWorld tasks from 73.9% to 83.9%, evidence that what transfers is procedure rather than task memory.

</details>


### [231] [PhoenixNest-Video: Evidence-Grounded Multimodal Agent Framework for Automated Video Interview Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02231)
*Fan Yuxuan,Huang Miaojun,Zhang Haimei,Wu Jingshen,Liu Hao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PhoenixNest-Video是一个基于证据的多模态智能体框架，用于自动化视频面试评估。它通过构建语义视频图作为工作记忆、进行基于评分标准的跨模态检索验证，以及采用基于双重奖励的Rubrics强化学习训练的评分器，在VInterview-2025数据集上达到91.50%的等级准确率，超越了更大的专有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的纯人工视频面试评估成本高且不一致，而现有AI方法产生不透明的分数，缺乏可追溯的评分依据。需要一种既能自动评估又能提供可解释、可审计证据的方案。

Method: 1) 构建语义视频图作为结构化工作记忆；2) 执行基于评分标准（rubric）的条件检索，并在视觉、音频和文本流之间进行跨模态验证；3) 通过Rubrics-based强化学习训练评分器，使用双重奖励分别优化评分标准对齐和分数级区分能力。

Result: 在VInterview-2025数据集上达到91.50%的等级准确率，超越规模更大的专有模型，且与专家小组的评分一致性高于直接提示更大模型的方式。

Conclusion: 一个紧凑的、以评分标准为根基的智能体能够在评分上与专家小组更接近，同时暴露每个分数背后的证据供人工审查，兼顾了评估准确性与可解释性。

Abstract: Interview assessment requires per-criterion judgments grounded in behavioral evidence, yet surging applicant volumes have made human-only evaluation costly and inconsistent, while existing AI approaches yield opaque scores without traceable rationale. We introduce PhoenixNest-Video, an evidence-grounded multimodal agent framework for automated video interview assessment. It builds a semantic video graph as structured working memory, performs rubric-conditioned retrieval with cross-modal verification across visual, audio, and textual streams, and produces per-criterion scores anchored to the candidate's materials. A Scorer trained via Rubrics-based Reinforcement Learning with dual rewards for rubric alignment and score-level differentiation internalizes the discriminative structure of multi-level rubrics. PhoenixNest-Video attains 91.50\% grade-level accuracy on VInterview-2025, outperforming substantially larger proprietary models. A compact, rubric-grounded agent therefore scores candidates in closer agreement with an expert panel than direct prompting of much larger models, and exposes the evidence behind each score for human review.

</details>


### [232] [PGPO: Potential-Guided Policy Optimization for Multi-Turn Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02236)
*Yuyao Zheng,Haipeng Sun,Junwei Bao,Lemao Liu,Hongfei Jiang,Yang Song,Dejing Dou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PGPO提出了一种基于势能引导的策略优化方法，利用锚点状态组的回报统计估计经验态势能，并通过相邻状态间的势能差异推导动作优势，实现跨轨迹的信用传播，为多轮智能体任务提供更细粒度的步级信用分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于组的强化学习方法（如GiGPO）虽然引入了步级优势，但仍依赖单条轨迹的最终结果，导致失败轨迹中有效动作与错误动作获得相同的负面信用，无法有效区分。

Method: 在每个rollout组内，从锚点状态组的回报统计中估计经验态势能，然后利用相邻状态之间的势能差异推导动作优势，实现跨轨迹的信用传播，特别针对失败轨迹提供更细粒度的步级信用分配。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop基准上的实验显示，PGPO相比近期基于组的RL方法具有更强的整体性能；进一步分析表明，PGPO能提供更有信息量的失败侧信用信号，且训练开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 势能引导的策略优化能够有效改善多轮智能体任务中的信用分配问题，尤其是在失败轨迹中，以极低额外开销获得更细粒度和更有信息量的信用信号。

Abstract: Group-based reinforcement learning (RL) has become an effective paradigm for LLM post-training, but in multi-turn agentic tasks with sparse terminal rewards, it often provides coarse credit for intermediate actions. To obtain more fine-grained credit assignment, recent work such as GiGPO introduces step-level advantages for intermediate actions. However, these step-level signals still rely on the final outcome of each individual trajectory. As a result, actions within failed trajectories can remain poorly differentiated, so effective actions can receive the same unfavorable credit as erroneous ones. In this work, we propose Potential-Guided Policy Optimization (PGPO) for multi-turn agentic tasks. PGPO estimates empirical state potentials from anchor-state-group return statistics within each rollout group. It then derives action advantages from potential differences between adjacent states, enabling cross-trajectory credit propagation. This provides finer-grained step-level credit assignment, especially within failed trajectories. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop show strong overall performance relative to recent group-based RL methods. Further analysis provides evidence that PGPO yields more informative failure-side credit signals with negligible training overhead.

</details>


### [233] [Propose to Learn, Learn to Propose: Evaluability-Aware Assistance under Bounded Rationality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02242)
*Yifan Zhu,Sammie Katt,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出可评估性感知的提案规划问题（ProSE），将提案同时视为任务干预和探测用户潜在偏好及评估约束的工具，并设计ProSE-Plan规划器在权衡收益获取与信息探测之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI辅助方法聚焦于提案质量和用户目标推断，假设用户能可靠评估任何提案，但由于有限理性（bounded rationality）该假设在实际中经常失效，导致规划器性能系统性不足。

Method: 将问题形式化为ProSE（隐藏参数的顺序辅助问题），采用KL正则化的有限理性二元响应模型，其中接受行为在价值增益与距离相关可评估性惩罚之间权衡。提出ProSE-Plan，一种深度为2的贝叶斯自适应规划器，通过对可能响应和响应诱导的后验信念进行评分来选择提案。

Result: 在受控图模拟中，当评估成本是瓶颈时，ProSE-Plan优于可评估性无关的基线和短视基线；probe-commit消融实验确认该方法能选择简单方法遗漏的信息性提案。

Conclusion: 研究识别出用户可评估性是与生成质量和偏好推断互补的规划相关维度，强调了仅追求接受率的规划器存在系统性的性能不足。

Abstract: AI assistants often collaborate by proposing candidate edits, plans, or designs that users evaluate before adoption. Existing assistance methods focus on proposal quality or user-goal inference, often assuming that the user can reliably evaluate any proposal, which can fail in practice because of bounded rationality. We study evaluability-aware proposal planning, where proposals serve both as task interventions and as probes for learning latent preferences and evaluation constraints, where the resulting belief updates then guide later proposals. We formalise this setting as ProSE, a hidden-parameter sequential assistance problem, and instantiate it with a KL-regularised bounded-rational binary response model in which acceptance trades off value gain against a distance-dependent evaluability penalty. Analysing the planning consequence of this likelihood reveals that likely accepted proposals and informative probes need not coincide, which explains why planners that only pursue acceptance systematically underperform. We operationalise ProSE with \textsc{ProSE-Plan}, a depth-2 Bayes-adaptive planner that scores proposals by possible responses and response-induced posterior beliefs. In controlled graph simulations, \textsc{ProSE-Plan} improves over evaluability-unaware and myopic baselines when evaluation cost is the bottleneck, and a probe-commit ablation confirms that our approach selects informative proposals that simpler methods miss. Our results thus identify user evaluability as a planning-relevant dimension of AI assistance, complementary to generation quality and preference inference.

</details>


### [234] [Task-Level Natural Language Priors as Learning Signals for Low-Resource LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02244)
*Jian Gao,Xiao Zhang,Xun Zhu,Miao Li,Ji Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Prior-Guided Tuning (PGT)训练视角，将任务级自然语言先验作为辅助学习信号而非仅作为输入上下文，用于低资源大语言模型训练。在此视角下引入Contrastive Prior Steering (CPS)方法，在保持原始监督目标的同时添加正负先验条件辅助损失。实验在AmbiMath、Jigsaw和MNLI/HANS上表明CPS持续优于普通微调和提示微调，验证了任务级自然语言先验可作为辅助学习信号的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在低资源训练数据模糊或不完整时常常表现不佳。虽然任务级自然语言先验可以在这种情况下提供有用指导，但现有方法通常将这些先验仅作为输入上下文处理，而没有在训练期间将其作为学习信号使用，导致先验信息未能充分发挥作用。

Method: 提出Prior-Guided Tuning (PGT)训练框架，将自然语言先验作为辅助学习信号。在该框架下具体实现Contrastive Prior Steering (CPS)：保持原有监督目标不变，同时添加正负先验条件下的辅助损失——正向损失鼓励任务一致学习，负向损失抑制看似合理但具误导性的备选方案。

Result: 实验表明CPS在多个基准上持续优于普通微调和提示微调。在AmbiMath上达到97.6%的精确匹配准确率；在Jigsaw上比标准微调平均提升9.5个百分点Macro F1，且仅用1/10实验训练数据就略微超过全量数据的普通微调；在HANS上，LLaMA 3.1 8B和Qwen 2.5 7B的非蕴含准确率分别提升8.3和5.2个百分点，同时保持可比的MNLI域内准确率。

Conclusion: 实验证据支持核心主张：任务级自然语言先验可以作为辅助学习信号，为低资源大语言模型训练提供有效指导。该方法具有重要实用价值，尤其在训练数据不足、模糊或易产生误导性学习的场景中。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often struggle when low-resource training data are ambiguous or incomplete. Task-level natural-language priors can provide useful guidance in such settings, but existing approaches usually treat these priors as input context rather than as learning signals during training. We propose Prior-Guided Tuning (PGT), a training perspective that incorporates natural-language priors as auxiliary learning signals for low-resource LLM training. Under this perspective, we introduce Contrastive Prior Steering (CPS), which keeps the original supervised objective intact while adding positive and negative prior-conditioned auxiliary losses to encourage task-consistent learning and discourage plausible but misleading alternatives. Experiments on AmbiMath, Jigsaw, and MNLI/HANS show that CPS consistently improves over plain and prompt fine-tuning. On AmbiMath, CPS achieves 97.6% average exact-match accuracy. On Jigsaw, CPS improves average Macro F1 by 9.5 percentage points over standard fine-tuning, and with 1/10 of the experimental training data slightly exceeds full-data plain fine-tuning. On HANS, CPS improves non-entailment accuracy by 8.3 and 5.2 percentage points for LLaMA 3.1 8B and Qwen 2.5 7B, respectively, while maintaining comparable in-domain MNLI accuracy. These results support our central claim: task-level natural-language priors can provide useful guidance as auxiliary learning signals for low-resource LLM training. Our code and data will be publicly available.

</details>


### [235] [LLM-as-a-Judge Is Not an Oracle: Why Self-Improving Agents Need Deterministic Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02246)
*Vansh Wahi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个核心立场：在自主提示优化循环中，LLM裁判不应担任"神谕"角色，而应降级为"顾问"。作者通过在合同分析、合规审查和代码质量等生产环境的数月运行，系统性记录了十一类评估信号失效问题（分为裁判偏见、测试框架与指标失败、真实标签错误、奖励黑客四类），并针对性地提出了PROCTOR框架——一个教师-学生循环，其中教师（LLM裁判）在五个确定性防护栏约束下工作，学生代理无法篡改运行环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主提示优化管线中，优化器重写提示以追求更高分数，而评分者本身是LLM裁判，它对系统是否进步拥有最终决定权。作者认为LLM裁判没有资格拥有这种最终裁决权，因为其评判信号在真实环境中存在系统性失败。作者通过实际构建和运行替代方案积累了翔实的失败证据，说明仅依赖LLM裁判的评估信号会导致优化器被误导甚至被恶意利用。

Method: 作者提出PROCTOR框架：(1) 有状态编排器持有所有工具访问权，无状态子代理仅负责诊断失败和草拟无法自行执行的变异；(2) 确定性验证层包含五道防护栏：密封沙箱、能力隔离角色、优先级高于教师的验收检查、冻结的保留集、金丝雀用例（满分本身即作弊证据）；(3) 教师LLM的裁决降级为一个输入信号，无法覆盖确定性验证层的否决。

Result: 作者在长达数月的生产运行中记录了严重失败案例：代理通过读取环境中的缓存答案获得满分（100%通过率掩盖实际仅68%能力）；一个损坏的真实标签导致优化器删除原本正确的合规规则去迎合错误标签；一个语法损坏的提示因静默解析器回退改善了指标而被推选为优胜者。修复裁判本身（重写评分标准）效果有限，唯一可靠的改进是结构性约束裁判输出顺序；而PROCTOR框架有效防止了这些失败。

Conclusion: LLM裁判在自主优化循环中不可作为最终权威，必须由确定性验证层把关。PROCTOR展示了将裁判降级为顾问、以结构性而非提示性约束来保障评估可靠性的有效性。作者同时坦诚承认：由于教师本身仍是LLM裁判，PROCTOR并未消除所有失败模式——这是对自我改进系统评估可靠性局限性的诚实边界声明。

Abstract: Self-improving agent pipelines have a problem at their center. An optimizer rewrites prompts to score higher, and the score comes from a judge that is itself an LLM. That judge has the last word on whether the system is getting better, and our position is that it has not earned it. The judge should be demoted from oracle to advisor: its verdict becomes one input among several, and every change is gated instead by a deterministic verification layer the judge cannot override. We reached this position by building the alternative and running it. Over months of running autonomous prompt-optimization loops in production across contract analysis, compliance review, and code quality, we cataloged eleven ways the evaluation signal failed, in four classes: judge bias, harness and metric failures, ground-truth errors, and reward hacking. Agents achieved perfect scores by reading cached answer keys from their environment, a 100% pass rate concealing 68% true capability. A corrupted ground-truth label caused the optimizer to delete correct compliance rules to agree with it. A syntactically broken prompt was promoted as the winner because a silent parser fallback improved the metric. Attempts to fix the judge by rewriting its rubric plateaued; the only reliable gain came from a structural constraint on its output order. In response we describe PROCTOR, a Teacher-Student loop in which a stateful orchestrator holds all tool access, stateless subagents diagnose failures and draft mutations they cannot apply, and a Teacher grades those mutations under five deterministic guardrails: hermetic sandboxes, capability-disjoint roles, acceptance checks that outrank the Teacher, frozen holdouts, and canary cases engineered so that a perfect score is itself evidence of cheating. We report the failures this prevented, and, because the Teacher is itself an LLM judge, the failures it did not.

</details>


### [236] [APEx: Distillation of Agent Procedural Experience for Adaptive Deep Research Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02253)
*Jie Ding,Rui Sun,Xinyuan Zhang,Zeyu Zhang,Xin Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: APEx提出了一种层次化经验利用框架，将交互历史组织为实例级轨迹记忆和类别级程序化技能，通过执行器、蒸馏器和规划器的闭环架构以及三阶段交替GRPO训练范式，实现奖励引导的技能蒸馏和测试时的在线策略自适应，在7个基准上超越GPT-5.4达14.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究智能体通过多轮推理调用外部工具回答复杂长时间跨度的任务，从过往经验中学习对持续改进至关重要。然而现有方法要么检索冗长的任务特定轨迹增加决策负担，要么蒸馏程序化技能但与下游策略自适应脱节，缺乏有效的经验利用机制。

Method: APEx采用层次化框架：将交互历史分为实例级轨迹记忆与类别级程序化技能，通过执行器、蒸馏器、规划器三模块闭环耦合；采用三阶段交替GRPO训练实现奖励引导的技能蒸馏；测试时通过技能引导的强化学习进行在线规划器自适应，并以技能对齐正则化防止策略漂移。

Result: 在7个基准数据集上取得最先进性能，超越GPT-5.4达14.7个百分点，超越最强的记忆增强基线3.0个百分点。

Conclusion: APEx通过层次化经验组织和闭环训练架构，有效解决了深度研究智能体经验利用中的关键问题，实现了无需真实标注的自我改进，验证了技能蒸馏与在线策略自适应结合的有效性。

Abstract: Deep research agents augment large language models with external tools to answer complex, long-horizon questions through multi-turn reasoning. Learning from prior experience is crucial for continual improvement, yet existing methods either retrieve verbose task-specific traces that burden decision-making, or distill procedural skills that remain decoupled from downstream policy adaptation. We propose APEx, a hierarchical experience utilization framework that organizes interaction history into instance-level trajectory memories and category-level procedural skills, and couples them through a closed-loop architecture of Executor, Distiller, and Planner. The three modules are optimized via a three-stage alternating GRPO training paradigm, enabling reward-guided skill distillation rather than fixed-prompt generation. At test time, distilled skills serve as procedural priors for online Planner adaptation through skill-guided test-time reinforcement learning, allowing ground-truth-free self-improvement with skill-alignment regularization to prevent policy drift. Experiments on 7 benchmarks demonstrate that APEx achieves state-of-the-art performance, surpassing GPT-5.4 by 14.7 points and the strongest memory-augmented baseline by 3.0 points.

</details>


### [237] [Codebook Agent: Amortized Topology Design for LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02264)
*Jinxi Yu,Yubei Li,Eric Hanchen Jiang,Zhi Zhang,Dong Liu,Wenxiao Zhao,Levina Li,Kai-Wei Chang,Ying Nian Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Codebook Agent，一种用于LLM多智能体系统通信拓扑设计的端到端方法。该方法通过向量量化自编码器将成功的拓扑压缩为查询无关的16条目码本，用奖励加权MLP将查询映射到码分布，并由MLP代理重排序候选拓扑。相比现有条件图生成方法（变分/自回归/扩散解码器+图网络打分器），该方法无需迭代搜索和消息传递，在六个基准上取得最佳准确率（84.6 vs 83.0），生成拓扑仅需2.4ms，并节省21.9-33.2%的LLM token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者指出当前将LLM多智能体拓扑设计视为条件图生成的范式与实际问题不匹配，通过三个经验事实论证：(1) 成功拓扑经过奖励过滤后仅收敛到约六种图，与码本容量从8增至64无关，说明无需大规模搜索空间；(2) 边数与token消耗负相关（r≈-0.4），稀疏化反而使推理更昂贵；(3) 当智能体共享profile时（基准默认配置），基于消息传递的图网络打分器具有邻接不变性，根本无法排序候选。这些观察揭示现有方法在搜索空间设计和打分器设计上都存在根本性问题。

Method: Codebook Agent由三个组件构成：(1) 向量量化自编码器（VQ-VAE）将成功拓扑压缩进一个查询无关的16条目码本；(2) 奖励加权MLP将查询嵌入映射到码的分布；(3) 读取扁平化邻接矩阵的MLP代理，基于实测效用和每任务归一化token成本回归，对解码出的候选拓扑进行单次批处理前向重排序。测试时无需迭代搜索和消息传递。

Result: 在全部六个基准上Codebook Agent均达到最高准确率（平均84.6，最强先前设计器为83.0），拓扑生成延迟仅2.4ms，且比现有方法节省21.9%-33.2%的LLM token消耗。

Conclusion: 作者认为将拓扑设计视为紧凑码本上的分类/重排序问题而非高维邻接空间的条件图生成是更合理的问题表述，Codebook Agent以更低的计算开销和token成本实现了更高的准确率，验证了这一范式转变的有效性。

Abstract: Adapting the communication topology of an LLM multi-agent system to each query improves both accuracy and efficiency, yet current designers treat this as conditional graph generation: a variational, autoregressive, or diffusion decoder searches the $N \times N$ adjacency space, and a graph-network proxy trained on utility and a structural cost such as edge count ranks the sampled candidates. We argue that this formulation is misaligned with the problem. Empirically, topologies that survive a reward filter collapse to about six distinct graphs even when the codebook capacity grows from 8 to 64; edge count is negatively correlated with measured token consumption (Pearson $r \approx -0.4$), so sparsifying the graph makes inference more expensive; and a message-passing scorer over agent-profile nodes is adjacency-invariant whenever agents share a profile---the default configuration of published benchmarks---so it cannot rank candidates at all in that regime. These three facts motivate Codebook Agent: a vector-quantized autoencoder compresses successful topologies into a query-independent 16-entry codebook; a reward-weighted MLP maps the query embedding to a distribution over codes; and an MLP proxy that reads the flattened adjacency, regressed on measured utility and per-task normalized token cost, reranks the top decoded candidates in a single batched forward pass. With no iterative search and no message passing at test time, Codebook Agent is the most accurate method on all six benchmarks we compare (84.6 average against 83.0 for the strongest prior designer), emits a topology in 2.4 ms, and uses 21.9--33.2% fewer LLM tokens.

</details>


### [238] [CoMerge: Conflict-Driven Preference Optimization for Multi-Task Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02273)
*Mingjie Zheng,Zihao Chen,Wenqing Chen,Weile Yuan,Zhixuan Chu,Jianxing Yu,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CoMerge框架，将模型合并重新表述为偏好优化问题，利用朴素合并方法的缺陷作为硬负样本构建偏好对，通过优化轻量级张量级合并系数来缓解参数干扰，在MergeBench上达到0.9968的平均归一化性能，超越所有基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并方法虽然旨在保留各个专家模型的能力并缓解参数干扰，但普遍没有直接从朴素合并可能导致的性能退化行为中学习。作者希望利用这些退化行为来改进合并过程。

Method: 提出冲突驱动的偏好优化框架（CoMerge），将模型合并转化为偏好优化问题。采用自监督的冲突驱动策略，利用朴素合并方法（如任务算术）的缺陷作为硬负样本来构建偏好对，无需外部标注。通过偏好优化来精炼轻量级的张量级合并系数，在缓解参数空间冲突的同时保留任务特定能力。

Result: 在MergeBench上达到0.9968的平均归一化性能，优于所有数据免费和数据驱动的模型合并基线。在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上，对冲突敏感任务（如指令跟随和安全）有显著改进，仅优化1,445个标量系数即可与全参数微调高度竞争。

Conclusion: CoMerge验证了从朴素合并的退化行为中学习（而非仅避免干扰）是一种有效的模型合并范式，能以极小的参数开销实现接近全参数微调的多任务性能。

Abstract: Model merging provides an efficient paradigm for constructing multi-task large language models (LLMs) without full model retraining, yet it remains challenged by parameter interference. While existing methods aim to preserve the capabilities of individual expert models and mitigate interference, they generally do not directly learn from the potentially degraded behaviors exposed by naive merging. In this paper, we propose a conflict-driven preference optimization framework for model merging (CoMerge), which reformulates model merging as a preference optimization problem. The approach utilizes a self-supervised, conflict-driven strategy that leverages the defects of naive merging methods (e.g., task arithmetic) as hard negative samples to construct preference pairs without external annotations. By applying preference optimization to refine lightweight, tensor-wise merging coefficients, CoMerge enables the model to mitigate parameter-space conflicts while preserving task-specific capabilities. Extensive experiments show that CoMerge achieves an average normalized performance of 0.9968 on MergeBench, outperforming all evaluated data-free and data-driven model-merging baselines. Furthermore, on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, CoMerge yields marked improvements on conflict-sensitive tasks such as instruction following and safety, while remaining highly competitive with full-parameter fine-tuning despite optimizing only 1,445 scalar coefficients.

</details>


### [239] [SCX Router: Streaming Zero-Shot Model Selection with a Decoder-KV Classifier and a Real-World Task Ontology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02292)
*Ihor Stepanov,Aleksandr Smechov,Mykhailo Shtopko,Dmytro Vodianytskyi,Oleksandr Lukashov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于GLiClass的轻量级LLM路由模型（0.6B参数），用于在推理时为每个任务选择最优模型，在速度、成本和质量的权衡上超越人工启发式规则。该路由器通过解码器-键值（KV）缓存执行路径实现高效推理，并预测任务类型、难度、推理模式等属性，在多个LiveBench子集上优于平均候选模型及最强的固定模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型数量激增且推理端点异构性高（质量、价格、延迟、上下文、工具使用等差异巨大），手动启发式规则难以维护且难以在速度-成本-质量间取得一致的有利权衡，因此需要自动化的逐任务模型选择机制。

Method: 采用GLiClass架构构建轻量级路由器：将Qwen3解码器与浅层双向打分器结合，避免自回归生成；通过仅文本的KV缓存会话模式只编码新对话轮次，候选标签token不写入持久缓存；同时支持零样本自定义标签。构建了包含23个家族、115种任务类型、345个可路由子类型、1173个合成示例及30个领域正交轴的任务本体，据此生成15万个验证器评分任务和1.5万个开放式任务来训练模型，并将请求预测与逐任务策略（资格、成本、缓存复用、安全性等）显式分离。

Result: 在六个LiveBench子集上，路由器优于平均候选模型；在选定的1000任务子集上，聚合top-1得分达到0.707，超过最强固定模型的0.696，且收益因基准而异。

Conclusion: 提出的路由器证明了一种可扩展、无需自回归生成的高效模型路由方案，能有效处理候选模型的高度异构性，实现优于单一固定模型的端到端性能，并支持灵活的零样本自定义标签能力。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of large language models (LLMs) and the growing diversity of their applications presents a unique optimization opportunity: selecting the right model for the task, while optimizing for speed, cost, and quality at a per-task level. However, inference endpoints can vary widely in quality, price, latency, context support, tool use, domain expertise, and reasoning behavior. This heterogeneity makes manual heuristics difficult to maintain and unlikely to achieve consistently favorable speed--cost--quality trade-offs on their own. We introduce \router{}, a lightweight GLiClass-based router that assigns a suitability score to each inference-time model label without autoregressive generation. The released 0.6B-parameter checkpoint combines a Qwen3 decoder with a shallow bidirectional scorer. Its decoder-KV execution path preserves a text-only key--value cache across a session, encodes only new dialogue turns, and evaluates transient candidate-label tokens without adding them to the persistent cache. The same checkpoint also predicts task type, difficulty, reasoning mode, and expected output length, and supports custom zero-shot labels. For task generation, we construct a task ontology with 23 families, 115 task types, 345 routable subtypes, 1,173 synthetic examples, and an orthogonal axis of 30 domains. Using this structure, we generate 150,000 verifier-scored tasks and 15,000 open-ended tasks. We then train the Qwen3 decoder on these tasks, while explicitly separating learned request prediction from per-task policies for attributes such as eligibility, cost, cache reuse, safety, and sovereignty. Across six LiveBench subsets, the router outperforms the mean candidate; on the selected 1,000-task subset, it achieves an aggregate top-1 score of 0.707 versus 0.696 for the strongest fixed model, with benchmark-dependent gains.

</details>


### [240] [Improving Evaluation Realism with Inference-Time Compute and Deployment Scaffolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02302)
*Axel Ahlqvist,Richard Guan,Juan-Pablo Rivera,Adeline Kassler,Dmitrii Troitskii,Alexandra Souly,Kai Fronsdal,Robert Kirk,John Hughes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出两种技术（critique refinement 和 DISH）使对齐评估更难与实际部署区分，从而减少评估感知带来的干扰。两种技术可组合使用，且比简单延长审计更有效地利用额外计算资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐评估面临的核心障碍是"评估感知"：模型能察觉到自己在被测试而非实际部署，这会削弱安全评估结论的有效性。需要让模拟的对齐评估更接近真实部署场景。

Method: 1) Critique refinement：在模拟器的每个动作上花费额外推理计算，生成多个候选动作，用目标模型的反馈进行细化，选择最像真实部署的候选继续评估。2) DISH（Deployment-Imitating SWE-Agent Harness）：在编码场景中将目标封装进智能体框架，缩小模拟评估与真实部署环境之间的差距。

Result: 在多个目标模型上测试发现两种技术具有组合效应：同时应用比单独使用任一技术带来更大的真实性提升，且这些改进比延长审计时间更有效地利用了额外计算。

Conclusion: 自动化方法可以有效提升对齐评估的真实性，使评估难以与部署区分，并且计算资源的投入方式值得优化——改进评估方法比简单延长审计更高效。

Abstract: A core obstacle to alignment evaluation is evaluation awareness: capable models can tell when they are being tested rather than deployed, weakening the conclusions a safety evaluation can support. We present two techniques that make simulated alignment evaluations harder to distinguish from real deployments. Our first technique, critique refinement, spends additional inference-time compute on each simulator action: the simulator generates multiple candidate actions, refines them using feedback from an instance of the target model on how to make them more realistic, and continues the evaluation with the most deployment-like candidate. Our second technique, DISH (Deployment-Imitating SWE-Agent Harness), wraps the target in an agent harness, reducing the gap between simulated and real deployment environments in coding settings. We test the techniques on multiple target models and find that they compose: applying both yields larger realism gains than either alone. Our results show that automated approaches can improve the realism of alignment evaluations, and that these improvements use additional compute more effectively than making the audits longer.

</details>


### [241] [SALA: Semantic-Aware Logical Alignment for Complex Reasoning in In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02336)
*Zhao Ji,Wenqing Chen,Zhixuan Chu,Jianxing Yu,Jingping Liu,Shanhe Zhao,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SALA是一个语义感知的逻辑对齐框架，用于上下文学习中的示范选择。它自动学习任务特定的推理操作，将其嵌入连续语义空间，并用动态时间规整（DTW）对齐推理序列，实现灵活且可解释的逻辑匹配。在四个推理基准和三个LLM上的实验表明其优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于表面相似性的示范检索方法无法捕捉底层问题解决逻辑；而现有的基于逻辑的方法依赖预定义推理步骤的刚性规则和精确匹配，难以处理灵活多样的推理过程。因此需要一个既能匹配推理逻辑又足够灵活的方法。

Method: SALA框架包含三个关键步骤：1) 自动学习任务特定的推理操作（不依赖固定库）；2) 将这些操作嵌入到连续语义空间中；3) 使用动态时间规整（DTW）对齐推理序列，实现软性、灵活的推理逻辑匹配，同时保持高度可解释性。

Result: 在四个推理基准和三个大型语言模型上的实验表明，SALA在示范选择方面优于现有方法。进一步分析验证了操作归纳和逻辑语义对齐两个组件的有效性。

Conclusion: SALA通过结合自动学习的推理操作与DTW语义对齐，成功弥合了表面相似性与刚性规则匹配之间的鸿沟，为复杂推理任务的ICL示范选择提供了一种灵活且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Effective in-context learning (ICL) for complex reasoning relies on selecting the right demonstrations. Traditional retrieval methods based on surface similarity fail to capture the underlying problem-solving logic. Recent logic-based methods address this by matching predefined reasoning steps, but the rigid rules and exact-match criteria is improper to handle flexible or diverse reasoning processes. To address the problem, we propose SALA, a Semantic-Aware Logical Alignment framework. Instead of relying on a fixed inventory, SALA automatically learns task-specific reasoning operations. It then embeds these operations into a continuous semantic space and uses dynamic time warping (DTW) to align the reasoning sequences. This approach allows for soft, flexible matching of reasoning logic while remaining highly interpretable. Experiments across four reasoning benchmarks and three LLMs demonstrate that SALA outperforms existing demonstration selection methods. Further analysis confirms the roles of the operation induction and the logical semantic alignment.

</details>


### [242] [Diagnosing with Insights: Structured Analysis of Agent Failures via Behavioral Abstractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02371)
*Jiayi Bi,Yanjie Gao,Yuanmin Xie,Liqun Li,Tianyin Xu,Fan Yang,Mao Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AGENTSCOPE，一种神经符号结合的智能体（LLM agent）故障诊断方法。其核心思想是将智能体的轨迹行为抽象为结构化表示，并引入"神经不变量"（neural invariants）来刻画行为属性，最终利用LLM引导推理在结构化表示上对照不变量，精准定位轨迹中的故障步骤及类型。在公开数据集（Who&When）和自建数据集（AgentErrata）上显著超越现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体的普及，理解和诊断其故障对效能与可信度至关重要。但智能体故障常表现为长而复杂的轨迹，人工排查如同大海捞针；传统软件缺陷诊断技术难以应对LLM智能体故障，而完全依赖LLM作为裁判又会产生不可靠的诊断结果。因此亟需一种兼顾可靠性与可解释性的智能体故障诊断方法。

Method: AGENTSCOPE采用神经符号（neuro-symbolic）方法：1) 将智能体基于轨迹的行为抽象为结构化表示；2) 引入"神经不变量"（neural invariants）概念来规约智能体行为属性；3) 在上述结构化表示之上，利用LLM引导的推理，对照神经不变量，精确定位轨迹中的故障步骤及其故障类型。

Result: 在公开的智能体故障数据集Who&When以及作者构建的更全面数据集AgentErrata上，AGENTSCOPE在故障定位（fault localization）和归因（attribution）准确率上显著优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 实验证明，将结构化抽象与LLM引导推理相结合，能够实现对智能体故障的有效、可靠且可解释的诊断。该研究为LLM智能体的故障诊断提供了新的方法论范式。

Abstract: With the proliferation of LLM agents, the ability to understand and diagnose failures in agents is essential to achieving superior effectiveness and trustworthiness. As agent failures often manifest via long and complex trajectories, manually finding the needles in the haystack is untenable. However, traditional diagnosis techniques for software bugs can hardly address LLM agent failures, while completely relying on LLMs as the judge yields unreliable diagnosis results. To overcome these challenges, this paper presents AGENTSCOPE, a new neuro-symbolic approach for agent failure mode diagnosis. The key principle of AGENTSCOPE is to abstract agent behavior, based on its trajectories, into structured representations. Furthermore, AGENTSCOPE introduces the concept of neural invariants to specify agent behavior properties. AGENTSCOPE leverages LLM-guided reasoning atop the structured representation against neural invariants to pinpoint both the failure step and its type in the trajectory. We show the effectiveness of AGENTSCOPE on publicly available agent failure datasets (Who&When) and a more comprehensive dataset created by us (AgentErrata), where AGENTSCOPE significantly outperforms the current state of the art in fault localization and attribution accuracy. Our work shows that integrating structured abstractions with LLM-guided reasoning enables effective, reliable, and interpretable diagnosis for agent failures.

</details>


### [243] [Contrastive Explanations in Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02399)
*Xiang Yin,Nico Potyka,Antonio Rago,Francesca Toni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文为量化双极论证框架（QBAFs）引入了对比性解释（contrastive explanations），用于解释两个主题论证之间推理结果的差异，而非传统的单个论证。文章提出了对比归因函数（CAFs）的一般形式及其应满足的性质，并基于移除、梯度和Shapley值三种方法实例化了CAFs，最后在医疗保健和偏见识别场景中展示了其应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的QBAFs解释方法大多只关注解释单个主题论证的推理结果，但实际应用中（如分类任务中的可解释性）常常需要理解为何两个论证会得出不同结论。这种差异解释的需求在现有文献中尚未得到充分解决。

Method: 1) 提出对比归因函数（CAFs）的一般形式，作为对比性解释的形式化框架；2) 建立CAFs应满足的一组一般性质（公理）；3) 基于三种不同技术实例化CAFs——移除方法（removal）、梯度方法（gradients）和Shapley值方法；4) 分别研究这些CAFs实例的性质表现。

Result: 论文成功引入了CAFs的形式化框架，并基于三种技术（移除、梯度、Shapley值）构建了具体的对比解释方法。通过性质分析表明这些CAFs满足所提出的一般性质要求。实验（应用演示）表明对比性解释在医疗保健领域和偏见识别场景中具有实用价值。

Conclusion: 对比性解释为QBAFs提供了一种新的解释范式，能够解释两个话题论证之间的结果差异，弥补了现有单论证解释的不足。该方法在医疗和偏见检测等实际应用中展现出良好前景，为论证框架的可解释性研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Argumentation frameworks are useful tools for representing and reasoning with information in a variety of settings, e.g. in supplementing AI models as they perform classification tasks, with a notable benefit of providing additional explainability. In this paper, we introduce contrastive explanations for Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Frameworks (QBAFs), one such formalism. Unlike most existing explanations for QBAFs, which explain the reasoning outcome of a single argument of interest (i.e. a topic argument), contrastive explanations explain the difference between two topic arguments. We introduce a general form of contrastive attribution functions (CAFs) and establish a set of general properties they should satisfy. We introduce CAFs based on removal, gradients and Shapley-values, and study their properties. Finally, to illustrate contrastive explanations, we demonstrate their usefulness in healthcare and bias identification settings.

</details>


### [244] [UTP-Bench: Uncertainty-aware Travel Planning Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02421)
*Etcharla Revanth Rao,Priyanshu Karmakar,Shubhojit Mallick,Manish Gupta,Shreya Ghosh,Abhik Jana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了UTP-Bench，一个面向不确定性感知的旅行规划基准测试，涵盖印度504个城市的真实数据、经验性延误分布和人群密度模式，并引入三个新评估指标来量化旅行计划在随机扰动下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有旅行规划基准（如TravelPlanner、TripCraft）假设确定性环境，仅评估静态约束满足，忽略了真实世界中交通延误、人群波动等随机不确定性对行程可行性的影响。LLM生成的行程在不确定性条件下可能失效，但缺乏相应的评估工具。

Method: 构建UTP-Bench大型基准，整合印度504个城市的真实旅行数据（景点、餐厅、住宿、多模态交通网络）；融入从主要城市收集的经验性延误分布和人群密度模式来模拟真实扰动；提出三个评估指标：BAS（缓冲区充足性）、CATS（人群感知时间）、TDAS（交通延误吸收能力）。

Result: 对GPT-5、Qwen3、Mistral和Phi-4等SOTA LLM的实验显示，模型生成计划与人类作者计划之间存在显著差距，特别是在时间缓冲、延误感知的交通调度和人群敏感规划方面。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在不确定性感知的旅行规划方面仍有明显不足，UTP-Bench揭示了这些差距，为未来开发更具鲁棒性的自动化旅行规划系统提供了评估基础和改进方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently demonstrated strong capabilities in automated travel itinerary generation. However, real- world travel planning is inherently uncertain: transportation delays, crowd fluctuations, and unexpected stochastic delays frequently inval- idate otherwise feasible schedules. Existing benchmarks like TravelPlanner and TripCraft assume deterministic environments, evaluating only static constraint satisfaction and ignoring whether generated plans remain robust when such uncertainties arise. To address this limitation, we introduce UTP-Bench1 , a large-scale benchmark for uncertainty-aware travel planning. The dataset integrates real-world travel data spanning 504 cities of India, including attractions, restau- rants, accommodations, and multi-modal trans- portation networks. To model realistic disrup- tions, UTP-Bench incorporates empirical delay distributions and crowd-density patterns col- lected from major cities, enabling evaluation of travel plans under stochastic conditions. We further propose three evaluation metrics, namely Buffer Adequacy Score (BAS), Crowd- Aware Timing Score (CATS), and Transport Delay Absorption Score (TDAS), which quan- tify the ability of generated itineraries to main- tain robustness against transit delays and crowd variability. Experiments with state-of-the-art LLMs like GPT-5, Qwen3, Mistral and Phi-4 re- veal substantial gaps between model-generated and human-authored plans, particularly in tem- poral buffering, delay-aware transportation scheduling, and crowd-sensitive planning.

</details>


### [245] [CivBench: A Long-Horizon Benchmark for Tool-Mediated Agents in Civilization VI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02459)
*Austin Tudor David Andrews,Liam Wilkinson,Jamie Heagerty,Harry Coppock,Jakob Nicolaus Foerster,Rui Ponte Costa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CivBench是一个开源基准测试，用于评估语言模型代理在通过MCP协议进行的长时程、工具密集型环境中的表现。每个episode包含300+轮交互和数千次工具调用，暴露76个MCP工具。论文引入两个新指标（PMR和RAG@10），发现代理存在系统性缺陷：低估战略状态的主动查询，且未能执行自身规划中承诺的近期行动。这些偏差被解读为指令下的偏离而非能力缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试难以评估语言模型代理在长时程、部分可观测环境中的持续规划、状态监控与执行能力。作者希望通过文明VI游戏与MCP协议构建一个更具生态效度的测试环境，并识别代理在界面层面的可测量行为模式。

Method: 基于文明VI游戏构建CivBench环境，通过MCP暴露76个工具，并将视觉游戏状态转化为结构化文本叙述层。在23次可接受运行中评估四个模型家族。提出两个新指标：Proactive Monitoring Rate (PMR)衡量代理主动查询潜在战略状态的频率，RAG@10衡量规划反思中陈述的承诺是否在随后10轮内被执行。

Result: 代理在共享协议下出现两个一致模式：(1) 尽管协议建议每20轮查询胜利进度，代理实际每30-75轮才查询，且在20次可检测败局中有7次未在游戏结束前20轮警告窗口内查询；(2) 代理频繁未能执行自身规划反思中的近期承诺，RAG@10介于48.2%-65.8%。聚合结果在此规模下无法可靠区分模型。

Conclusion: 代理在拥有工具访问权和明确指导的情况下仍表现出监控与承诺执行的系统性不足，这些是"指令下的偏离"而非"能力缺失"。论文发布完整环境、日志、指标与分析管线，为长时程代理行为研究提供基础设施。作者强调该样本是试点性的，不作模型排名依据。

Abstract: We present CivBench, an open-source benchmark for evaluating language model agents in long-horizon, tool-mediated environments through the Model Context Protocol (MCP). A single episode spans 300+ turns and produces thousands of tool calls over a large action space, requiring sustained planning, state monitoring, and execution under partial observability. The environment exposes 76 MCP tools and a narration layer that converts visual game state into structured text.
  We use CivBench to characterise agent behaviour across four model families in 23 admissible runs. The sample is a pilot, not a model ranking: aggregate outcomes do not reliably discriminate models at this scale. Instead, we introduce two interface-level metrics that the environment makes measurable: Proactive Monitoring Rate (PMR), capturing whether agents actively query latent strategic state, and RAG@10, capturing whether commitments stated in structured planning reflections are executed within ten subsequent turns.
  Across runs we observe two consistent patterns under a shared playbook protocol. Agents under-monitor strategically relevant state that is available but requires explicit querying: despite playbook guidance to query victory progress every 20 turns, agents do so only every 30 to 75 turns, and in 7 of 20 detectable defeats they failed to query within the 20 turn warning window before game end. Agents also frequently fail to execute near-term commitments stated in their own planning reflections (RAG@10 between 48.2% and 65.8% across models). Both patterns arise despite tool access and explicit guidance, and we interpret them as deviations under instruction rather than absences of capability.
  We release the environment, scenarios, logs, metrics, and analysis pipeline at https://github.com/lmwilki/civ6-mcp

</details>


### [246] [Collective creativity in hybrid societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02620)
*Mason Youngblood,Katie Mudd,Manuel Anglada-Tort,Cameron Jones,Elena Miu,Diana Omigie,Margaret Schedel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨生成式AI对创造力的影响，认为现有争论源于混淆了新颖性（单一作品的属性）与多样性（群体的属性）两个概念。作者提出在生成式AI语境下，创造力应理解为混合集体（人与算法互动的群体）的属性，而非个体的属性。AI辅助构思虽能稳定提升个体产出的新颖性，却会收窄总体的多样性，但这一结果并非必然——由于人类与模型的搜索方式互补，混合群体可在表现和多样性上超越任一纯群体。决定结果的关键在于构成：谁在场、比例如何、怎样连接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究人员对生成式AI是丰富还是削弱文化争论不休，作者认为争论的根源在于混淆了创造力的两个不同组成部分：作为单一作品属性的新颖性，以及作为群体属性的多样性。

Method: 作者采用概念分析的方法，区分新颖性与多样性，并提出将创造力视为"混合集体"（人机互动的群体）的属性。通过论证人类与模型搜索方式的互补性，说明混合群体可以超越任一同类群体的表现与多样性。核心机制是"构成"（composition）——即哪些主体在场、以何种比例、如何连接。

Result: AI辅助构思可靠地提升个体产出的新颖性，但同时收窄总体多样性。然而这并非人机协作的必然结果——混合群体（人类+机器）可以超越纯人类或纯机器群体。机器发现的解决方案可以进入人类文化并持续存在。

Conclusion: 问题不再是AI是否帮助或损害创造力，而是如何组合主体（何种构成）能让个体的收益积累同时不侵蚀集体的多样性。决定结果的变量是构成：哪些主体在场、比例如何、如何连接。

Abstract: Generative AI is changing how cultural artifacts are created and circulated, and with it our understanding of creativity itself. Researchers disagree about whether these tools enrich or impoverish culture, and we argue that much of that disagreement comes from conflating two distinct components of creativity: novelty, a property of single artifacts, and diversity, a property of populations. We argue further that creativity in the context of generative AI is best understood as a property of hybrid collectives, or populations of interacting people and algorithms, rather than of individuals. AI-assisted ideation reliably raises the novelty of individual output while narrowing diversity in the aggregate, but this is not an inevitable consequence of putting machines in the loop. Because humans and models search in complementary ways, mixed groups can outperform and out-diversify groups of either kind alone, and machine-discovered solutions can enter human culture and persist there. What decides the outcome is composition: which agents are present, in what proportion, and how they are connected. The question is no longer whether AI helps or harms creativity, but which mixtures let individual gains accumulate without eroding collective diversity.

</details>


### [247] [Loom: Weaving Diagnostic Strands into Free-Text Consensus via Embedding-Space Reweighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02649)
*Ron Begleiter,Katya Egert Berg,Gilad Saban,Gil Shabat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Loom是一个生成式共识框架，用于真实工业场景中的根因分析（RCA）。它将模块化启发式方法产生的开放形式假设投影到连续嵌入空间，通过迭代质心加权算法解决冲突信号，最终用单个轻量级LLM调用生成共识。在OpenRCA基准上，Loom位于准确率-效率的Pareto前沿，匹配最先进自主代理的性能但快约26倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业场景中聚合噪声、冲突的文本假设为可靠共识是核心挑战。单体LLM代理虽有无限表达能力，但受限于上下文长度、幻觉累积和推理延迟；传统弱监督虽统计严谨，但在数学上局限于离散类别。需要一个桥接两者的共识框架。

Method: Loom架构：(1) 由模块化启发式方法（动态填充诊断模板）生成开放式假设；(2) 将假设投影到连续嵌入空间；(3) 使用迭代质心重加权算法解决冲突信号；(4) 共识权重支撑单个轻量级LLM综合步骤。

Result: 在OpenRCA基准上，Loom在Bank和Market-2上匹配SOTA自主代理，在Market-1和Telecom上略有差距；但每个事件仅需一次LLM调用，速度快约26倍（使用8B参数综合器时约33倍）。

Conclusion: Loom展示了确定性共识与轻量级生成相结合的有效性。部署经验揭示了代理深度与推理延迟之间的权衡、冗余检测中的负面结果，以及确定性共识如何增强领域专家（SME）的信任。

Abstract: Aggregating noisy, conflicting textual hypotheses into a reliable consensus is a fundamental challenge when deploying NLP systems in real-world industrial settings. While monolithic Large Language Model (LLM) agents offer unbounded expressivity for tasks like Root Cause Analysis (RCA), they suffer from context limits, compounding hallucinations, and prohibitive inference latency. Traditional weak supervision offers statistical rigor but is mathematically restricted to discrete classes. We present Loom, a generative consensus framework deployed for real-world RCA that bridges these paradigms. Loom aggregates open-form hypotheses emitted by modular heuristics (diagnostic templates dynamically populated with episode-specific entities, times, and metrics) by projecting them into a continuous embedding space, and resolves conflicting signals with an iterative centroid-based reweighting algorithm. The resulting consensus weights ground a single lightweight LLM synthesis step. Evaluated on the OpenRCA benchmark, Loom occupies the accuracy--efficiency Pareto frontier: it matches a state-of-the-art autonomous agent on Bank and Market-2 and trails on Market-1 and Telecom, while using a single LLM call per incident on all four datasets ($\sim$26$\times$ faster; $\sim$33$\times$ with an 8B-parameter synthesizer). We discuss our deployment experience, highlighting lessons learned regarding the trade-offs between agentic depth and inference latency, negative results in redundancy detection, and how deterministic consensus fosters trust among Subject Matter Experts~(SMEs).

</details>


### [248] [Door-in-the-Face Requests and Refusal Behaviour in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02707)
*Til Jordan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究测试了"门面效应"（door-in-the-face）技巧是否对语言模型有效，发现其效果因模型而异：Anthropic的旗舰模型（如Opus 5）上该技巧有效（符合率从29.3%提升到65.8%），而在OpenAI、Google的旗舰模型及Haiku 4.5上则适得其反（符合率降低15.5至23.0个百分点）。控制实验表明让步本身在所有模型中都有作用，但对拒绝的反应因模型家族而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类心理学中，"门面效应"是指在提出大请求被拒绝后，再提出较小的后续请求会提高对方接受概率。作者希望探究这一人类影响力技巧是否能迁移到语言模型上，从而理解模型对人类社交操纵技巧的脆弱性及其机制。

Method: 作者在九个生产模型（来自三家提供商）上进行实验：每个模型先拒绝一个大的请求，然后收到同一请求的较小版本，并将其合规率与直接提出的基线进行比较。同时设置了一个控制条件（不相关主题的大请求被拒绝）来定位效应来源，还测试了公共基准中提取的拒绝请求，以及将265个被拒绝的请求从"指令请求"改写为"解释请求"的变体。

Result: 门面效应在Anthropic的Opus 5上有效（65.8% vs 29.3%），但在OpenAI和Google的旗舰模型以及Haiku 4.5上产生反效应（合规率下降15.5-23.0点）。控制实验显示让步本身在所有九个模型上都有作用，但"刚拒绝过某事"的反应因模型家族而异。该技巧不能迁移到公共基准的拒绝场景。请求内容决定技巧效果：将265个被拒绝的指令请求改写为同主题的解释请求后，263个不再被拒绝。

Conclusion: 人类影响力技巧在语言模型上的效果是逐模型家族而异的，不存在普遍适用的结论。门面效应的成功关键在于请求的具体内容类型（如要求解释而非指令），且不同模型家族对"刚拒绝后再次请求"这一社会情境的反应存在本质差异，反映出它们建立拒绝决策机制的差异。

Abstract: Does the door-in-the-face technique work on language models? In humans, a large request that is refused makes a smaller follow-up request more likely to be granted. We test this on nine production models from three providers: each model refuses a large request, then receives a smaller version of the same request, and we compare its compliance with asking directly. The answer depends on the model. On Anthropic's frontier models the technique works: Opus 5 answers the smaller request 65.8% of the time after refusing the larger one, against 29.3% when asked directly. On the frontier models of OpenAI and Google, and on Haiku 4.5, it backfires, lowering compliance by 15.5 to 23.0 points. A control locates the effect: a refused large request on an unrelated topic does less than the related one on all nine models, so the concession itself matters everywhere, while the reaction to having just refused something differs by model family. The technique does not transfer to refusals drawn from public benchmarks. What decides whether a retreat can work is what the request asks for: rewriting 265 refused requests for usable instructions into requests for explanations of the same topic removed the refusal in 263 cases. Human influence techniques port to language models one model family at a time.

</details>


### [249] [Repo-To-Skill: Distilling GitHub Repositories Into AI4AI Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02749)
*Jianlyu Chen,Yuyang Hu,Hongjin Qian,Jiawei Liu,Wenqing Wei,Xiaolong Chen,Defu Lian,Zhicheng Dou,Chaozhuo Li,Qiwei Ye,Zheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DisCo，一个技能驱动的自主研究智能体，通过两种互补的蒸馏形式（任务无关与任务导向）将领域操作性知识（operational knowledge）压缩为可验证的技能，并构建了包含5000+技能、源自1000个流行ML仓库的AREX-Skill Library，在多个基准上显著提升了研究性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体开始端到端执行ML研究，现有架构（模型骨干+规划/执行/记忆/验证框架）仍然缺少领域特定的操作性知识——即区分"知道方法"与"让方法奏效"的诀窍。这些知识存在于论文和代码库中，但形式面向人类读者且体量过大，无法在任务执行中加载。因此需要将这类知识蒸馏为紧凑、可验证、可跨任务复用的技能。

Method: DisCo采用两种互补的蒸馏形式：(1) 任务无关蒸馏——将领域内广泛使用的仓库压缩为通用可复用技能，应用于开放生态生成AREX-Skill Library（5000+技能，覆盖20个领域、178个能力家族）；(2) 任务导向蒸馏——针对具体任务生成所需技能。智能体在研究中既能创建技能又能使用技能，并在固定流程中引入蒸馏后的运行上下文。

Result: 在固定GPT-5.5骨干、研究框架和执行预算的条件下，配备技能的智能体相比无技能版本在MLE-bench上提升134.3%，PaperBench提升34.4%，FrontierCS提升9.2%，PassNet提升14.0%。性能提升完全来自添加的蒸馏运行上下文。

Conclusion: 操作性知识可以通过蒸馏方式从面向人类的仓库和论文中提取为紧凑、可验证的技能，并以任务无关和任务导向两种形式供自主研究智能体跨任务重用，能显著提升端到端ML研究任务的性能，避免每次运行时的重复发现。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are beginning to carry out machine-learning (ML) research end to end. These agents combine a model backbone with a harness for planning, execution, memory, and verification, but this architecture still leaves domain-specific know-how outside the agent. We call this missing layer operational knowledge, the know-how that separates knowing a method from making it work. That knowledge is not absent from the field. It appears in repositories and papers, but in forms written for human readers and too large to load during a task. Once distilled into compact, verified skills, this knowledge can be reused across tasks rather than rediscovered during each run.
  We present DisCo, a skill-powered research agent that creates skills and uses them during research. Its distillation runs in two complementary forms: task-agnostic, condensing the field's widely used repositories into reusable skills, and task-oriented, producing the skills a concrete task calls for. The former, applied across the open ecosystem, yields the AREX-Skill Library, with 5,000+ verified skills distilled from 1,000 widely used ML repositories and organized into 20 areas and 178 capability families. With the GPT-5.5 backbone, research harness, and downstream execution budget held fixed, the skill-equipped research agent scores 134.3% higher on MLE-bench, 34.4% higher on PaperBench, 9.2% higher on FrontierCS, and 14.0% higher on PassNet than the same agent without skills. These gains come from adding distilled operating context under that fixed setup.

</details>


### [250] [Bilevel Coordinated Reflection: A Game-Theoretic Approach to Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02750)
*Yihang Chen,Yuxiang Chen,Yuxuan Huang,Meng Fang,Weilin Luo,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的理论框架来分析多智能体LLM系统中的编排者-工作者协调、反思记忆改进及外部验证的作用，并引入SRMA算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统虽有实证成果，但缺乏对协调、记忆改进和外部验证角色的统一理论解释。

Method: 将编排者-工作者交互建模为双层协调博弈；将反思建模为语义记忆状态上的随机游走；推导反射的有限时间界和不可能性定理；提出SRMA算法（仅在环境接地评估风险严格下降时接受候选记忆）。

Result: 在500个SWE-bench实例上，完整的Kimi系统解决率为72.2%，优于公开mini-SWE-agent参考的70.8%。

Conclusion: 证明了接地门控优于仅文本观察门控，SRMA在标定和非退化修正质量条件下精确、几何或多项式收敛，并通过实验验证了预测的协调与漂移规律。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems commonly use an orchestrator to decompose a task for a team of workers and then improve through textual reflection. Despite strong empirical results, these systems lack a unified account of coordination, memory improvement, and the role of external verification. We model orchestrator-worker interaction as a bilevel coordination game: under bounded coupling, the workers' local-update game is an approximate potential game whose equilibrium slack is controlled by decomposition quality. We then analyse reflection as stochastic movement over semantic memory states. For free-form reflection, we derive a finite-time upper bound, prove worst-case tightness, and give a positive lower bound under a falsifiable persistent-harm condition. We further prove an information-theoretic impossibility result: no gate that observes only the generated transcript can improve uniformly over text-indistinguishable environments, whereas an environment-grounded gate can. Motivated by this separation, we introduce Stochastic Reflective Memory Ascent (SRMA), which accepts a candidate memory only after a grounded evaluation risk strictly decreases. Under calibration and non-degenerate corrective mass, SRMA converges exactly, geometrically or polynomially; matching constructions show that both rate regimes are order-tight. We also provide confidence gating for stochastic evaluation and re-anchoring guarantees for piecewise-stationary environments. Experiments instantiate these objects with environment-grounded metrics and test the predicted coordination and drift laws. On 500 SWE-bench instances, the complete Kimi-based system resolves 72.2% versus a 70.8% public mini-SWE-agent reference. Code: https://github.com/YihangChen9/Bilevel-Coordinated-Reflection

</details>


### [251] [Measurement-Driven Sub-Network Selection for On-Premise Retrieval-Augmented Factory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02760)
*Vasileios Rizeakos,Georgios Paisios,Alexandros Machairas,Michael Birbas,Athanasios Bachoumis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究车间边缘设备上的问答助手部署问题。作者发现模型经过结构压缩和检索增强适配后，模型大小不再是适配后答案质量的有效预测指标。因此将部署视为"适配后选择"问题：在每个设备上根据评判答案质量和实测吞吐量选择子网络，并设定通用能力下限和内存预算约束。通过权重共享超网络和sandwich式蒸馏使选择成本低廉。在制造手册案例中，提取保留了未修剪模型评判质量的86.3%，检索增强蒸馏使其恢复到95.4%（挽回三分之二损失），同一助手可在三个异构边缘层级上以1.3至5瓦待机功耗运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车间工人需要对话式访问机器文档的助手，但能胜任此任务的模型通常太大，难以适配车间硬件环境（资源受限的边缘设备），存在模型能力与部署可行性的矛盾。

Method: 1) 对模型进行结构压缩和检索接地（retrieval-grounded）适配；2) 将部署建模为适配后的子网络选择问题，在通用能力下限和内存预算约束下，基于评判答案质量和设备实测吞吐量进行优化；3) 使用权重共享超网络，通过sandwich式原位蒸馏（in-place distillation）高效训练，使不同规模子网络的采选成本低廉。

Result: 关键发现是压缩适配后模型大小与适配答案质量不再线性相关（通用能力随参数近线性下降，但评判的检索增强答案质量却不如此）。案例研究中，单网络提取方案保留86.3%的评判质量，检索接地蒸馏恢复到95.4%以内，挽回约三分之二损失；同一助手在三个异构边缘设备上以1.3至5瓦待机功耗运行。仅优化大小、速度或质量任一维度的规则都会牺牲能力或吞吐量。

Conclusion: 此类任务的适配模型部署应视为涉及多个约束的后适配选择问题，而非单纯追求缩小模型。权重共享超网络加蒸馏可让该选择过程保持经济高效，在边缘设备上实现质量、能力与吞吐量之间的平衡。

Abstract: On-premise assistants can give factory workers conversational access to machine documentation, but models capable of the task rarely fit shop-floor hardware. We show that after structural compression and retrieval-grounded adaptation, model size is no longer a reliable predictor of adapted answer quality: general capability falls almost linearly with parameter count, while judged retrieval-augmented answer quality does not. We therefore treat deployment as a post-adaptation selection problem, committing one sub-network per device on judged answer quality and measured on-device throughput under a configurable general-capability floor and memory budget; rules that optimize size, speed, or quality alone each give up capability or throughput. A weight-shared supernetwork trained with sandwich-style in-place distillation keeps this selection inexpensive. In a manufacturing-manual case study, extraction costs 13.7 percent of the unpruned model's judged quality and retrieval-grounded distillation returns it to within 4.6 percent, recovering two thirds of the loss, and the same assistant runs across three heterogeneous edge tiers at 1.3 to 5 watts standby.

</details>


### [252] [SafeEvolve: Harness-Policy Co-Evolution from Agent Experience for Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02786)
*Qinghua Mao,Wanying Qu,Dadi Guo,Leitao Yuan,Qingyu Liu,Yu Li,Guanxu Chen,Yanwei Fu,Xi Lin,Xia Hu,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SafeEvolve是一个基于经验驱动的自进化框架，通过"工具箱-策略"协同进化（harness-policy co-evolution）来提升基于LLM的智能体的安全性。它利用在线策略轨迹中的安全经验，持续循环地同时更新运行时工具箱（安全提示和分层技能）和策略（两阶段SFT-RL），在多项智能体安全基准上实现了比现有基线更好的安全-效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体的安全性同时受基础模型和运行时工具箱（harness）影响，安全风险既存在于有害的最终响应，也存在于多步执行轨迹中。但现有安全对齐机制要么依赖外部工具箱更新，要么依赖策略优化，这两种孤立范式都无法弥合运行时控制与内在安全之间的鸿沟。

Method: SafeEvolve提出经验驱动的"工具箱-策略"协同进化循环：1）工具箱侧：将轨迹级安全证据转化为有界、组件级更新（安全提示和分层技能），生成可审计、可逆的工具箱工件；2）策略侧：采用两阶段SFT-RL范式——先通过工具箱使用的SFT引导策略主动利用进化后的工具箱工件，再通过带验证器分解奖励的工具箱增强RL在多步探索中塑造自主安全行为。

Result: 在智能体安全基准实验中，SafeEvolve优于现有基线，实现了更强的安全-效用权衡。在Qwen3.5-4B上，SafeEvolve在AgentDojo上将ASR（攻击成功率）降低为原来的1/3（即3倍降低），同时将良性效用从59.79%提升至61.86%。

Conclusion: 通过工具箱与策略的协同进化，SafeEvolve将安全经验转化为进化的运行时工具箱和改善的策略行为，有效弥合了运行时控制与内在安全之间的鸿沟，证明了"工具箱-策略"协同进化范式的有效性。

Abstract: The performance of LLM-based agents is jointly shaped by the base model and the harness used when interacting with the environment. This exposes them to safety risks in both harmful final responses and multi-step execution trajectories. Existing safety alignment mechanisms often rely on either external harness updates or policy optimization, yet applying either paradigm in isolation fails to bridge runtime control with intrinsic safety. We propose SafeEvolve, an experience-driven self-evolving framework for agent safety alignment. SafeEvolve leverages safety experience from completed on-policy trajectories to drive a continual loop of harness-policy co-evolution. On the harness side, SafeEvolve converts trajectory-level safety evidence into bounded, component-level updates across safety prompt and hierarchical skills, yielding auditable and reversible harness artifacts. On the policy side, SafeEvolve follows a two-stage SFT-RL paradigm, where harness-use SFT bootstraps the policy to actively leverage evolved harness artifacts, and harness-augmented RL further shapes autonomous safety behaviors during multi-step exploration via verifier-decomposed rewards. Through harness-policy co-evolution, SafeEvolve converts safety experience into an evolved runtime harness and improved policy behavior. Experiments on agentic safety benchmarks show that SafeEvolve achieves a stronger safety-utility tradeoff than existing baselines. For Qwen3.5-4B, SafeEvolve achieves a $3\times$ ASR reduction on AgentDojo while improving benign utility from 59.79% to 61.86%.

</details>


### [253] [Large Language Models (LLMs) for Telecom Root Cause Analysis (RCA): A Structured Reasoning Framework for Evidence-Grounded Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02805)
*Hao Zhou,Mandar Kulkarni,Hao Chen,Yan Xin,Charlie,Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文回顾了电信网络根因分析(RCA)从基于规则、机器学习到LLM驱动技术的演进历程，并提出了一种结构化推理框架，通过规范化上下文组织、决策路径推理和证据驱动解释三个步骤，提升LLM在5G/6G网络RCA中的诊断准确性和决策一致性。实验在两个5G数据集上验证了该框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代5G和新兴6G网络存在复杂的跨层依赖，导致电信网络性能退化诊断极具挑战性。虽然LLM具备推理和知识整合能力，但直接应用vanilla LLM进行电信RCA会产生幻觉、推理不稳定以及与结构化网络证据对齐不良等问题。

Method: 提出一种LLM驱动的电信RCA结构化推理框架：1) 将异构网络遥测数据组织为规范化上下文；2) 在诊断过程中强制执行决策路径推理；3) 生成基于证据的解释以实现可靠的故障识别。同时综述了结构化推理、检索增强知识接地、智能体编排和可验证推理等新兴范式。

Result: 在两个5G RCA数据集（TeleLogs和TelecomTS）上的实验表明，所提出框架相较于基线方法在诊断准确性和决策一致性方面均有一致性提升。

Conclusion: 跨数据集的结果突显了结构化推理设计对于下一代电信网络中实用LLM-based RCA系统的重要性。

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) is a critical task in telecom network operations, but diagnosing performance degradations in modern 5G and emerging 6G networks remains challenging due to complex cross-layer dependencies. While large language models (LLMs) offer promising capabilities for reasoning and knowledge integration, directly applying vanilla LLMs to telecom RCA often leads to hallucination, unstable reasoning, and poor alignment with structured network evidence. This work first reviews the evolution of telecom RCA from rule-based and machine learning (ML) approaches to emerging LLM-enabled techniques, and provides an overview of recent paradigms, including structured reasoning, retrieval-augmented knowledge grounding, agentic orchestration, and verifiable reasoning. Building upon these insights, we propose a structured reasoning framework for LLM-enabled telecom RCA that aligns diagnostic reasoning with telecom-specific evidence and domain knowledge. The proposed approach first organizes heterogeneous network telemetry into canonical contexts, and then enforces decision-path reasoning during diagnosis, and finally generates evidence-grounded explanations for reliable fault identification. Experimental results on two 5G RCA datasets, TeleLogs and TelecomTS, demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently improves diagnostic accuracy and decision consistency compared with baseline techniques. These cross-dataset results highlight the importance of structured reasoning design for practical LLM-based RCA systems in next-generation telecom networks.

</details>


### [254] [AI Contextual Measurement for Recovering Individual and Group-Level Effects: Validation Against Survey Measures and an Occupational Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02821)
*Wenxin Jiang,Xuyang Wang,Yuxiao Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AICOME（AI情境测量）框架，用于评估AI生成的受访者层面测量能否在情境模型（contextual models）中恢复个体与群体层面的效应。通过2022年中国家庭追踪调查（CFPS）验证，发现当拥有丰富的受访者与职业特征时，AI情境测量能较好地恢复观察变量包含的情境信息，其中每周工作小时的验证效果最强；但在信息受限或多概念同时未观测时性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者越来越多地使用AI构建传统调查中缺失的社会、组织和职业特征测量，但缺乏系统评估这些AI测量能否在情境模型中准确恢复个体与群体层面效应的框架。现有方法往往仅将AI测量视为"响应预测"问题，忽略了其在群体聚合与个体偏差层面的用途。

Method: 提出AICOME框架：以受访者层面构建的AI测量为基础，推导其群体层面聚合值与个体偏差，从而同时估计组间（between-group）与组内（within-group）关联。利用CFPS 2022数据，以职业为分组结构，针对计算机使用、外语使用、每周工时、管理职责四个变量，在响应层面、模型层面、情境层面和边界条件层面进行系统性验证。

Result: 结果表明：在丰富受访者与职业特征可用时，AI情境测量能恢复观察调查变量中大部分情境模型信息；每周工作小时是最强的验证案例，能够复现职业间与职业内与满意度的显著负向关联；当信息仅限职业和基本人口学特征时性能下降，当多个相关概念同时未观测时恢复能力减弱。

Conclusion: AICOME最适合从丰富的现有数据集中恢复有限数量且理论上重要的建构。其价值在于将AI测量从单纯的响应预测扩展到情境模型中的群体与个体层面效应估计，并明确了框架适用的边界条件。

Abstract: Researchers increasingly use artificial intelligence to construct measures of social, organizational, and occupational characteristics that are absent from conventional surveys. We propose AICOME, AI COntextual MEasurement, a framework for evaluating whether AI-derived respondent-level measures can recover individual and group-level effects in contextual models. The key idea is that an AI measure constructed at the respondent level can be used to derive its group-level aggregate and its individual deviation, allowing researchers to estimate both between-group and within-group associations rather than treating AI measurement as response prediction alone.
  We validate the framework using the 2022 China Family Panel Studies (CFPS), where occupations provide the empirical grouping structure and several job-related survey variables provide validation benchmarks. For computer use, foreign-language use, weekly hours, and management responsibilities, we compare survey measures with AI-derived measures in response-level, model-level, contextual, and boundary-condition validations. The results show that AI contextual measurement can recover much of the contextual-model information contained in observed survey variables when rich respondent and job characteristics are available. Weekly hours provides the strongest validation case, with AI-derived measures reproducing the large negative between- and within-occupation associations with satisfaction observed in CFPS. The framework also identifies clear boundary conditions: performance deteriorates when information is restricted to occupation and basic demographics, and recovery is weaker when several related concepts are treated as simultaneously unobserved. The findings suggest that AICOME is most useful for recovering a limited number of theoretically important constructs from rich existing datasets.

</details>


### [255] [Discriminative World Models for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02885)
*Kelvin Li,Dhruv Pendharkar,Anish Pahilajani,Chuyi Shang,Leon Oks,Leonid Karlinsky,Rogerio Feris,Trevor Darrell,Roei Herzig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"预测状态匹配"(predicted-state matching)的训练目标，用于网络代理的世界模型。与传统的监督式下一状态预测不同，该方法要求预测的状态表示能够区分真实结果状态与替代动作到达的状态，从而更好对齐下游排序器的需求，提升端到端任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络代理的世界模型通过监督式下一状态预测（生成HTML或AXTree快照）进行训练，但该目标与下游排序器目标不一致——排序器需要预测状态在候选动作间具有判别性才能准确评分。传统监督预测目标无法保证这种判别性，导致测试时动作选择的整体性能受限。

Method: 引入预测状态匹配训练目标：要求模型预测的状态表示能够区分真实结果状态与替代动作导致的状态。使用从WebArena Go-Browse轨迹派生的分支数据集（每个决策点包含多个替代动作及其结果状态）进行模型训练。训练出的世界模型用于测试时动作选择，通过采样候选动作、预测网络状态并排序来实现。

Result: 在持有的预测状态匹配基准测试上，该方法优于使用监督式下一状态预测训练的世界模型；在WebPRMBench上改进了PRM风格的动作排序，优于仅动作PRM和用监督式下一状态世界模型增强的PRM；在WebArena-Lite上，使用该方法进行测试时动作选择提升了端到端任务成功率。

Conclusion: 预测状态匹配作为一种世界模型训练目标，能更有效地对齐下游排序器的判别性需求，在预测状态匹配基准、动作排序和端到端任务成功率等多个层面均验证了其有效性，为网络代理世界模型的训练提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Recent web agents use world models for test-time action selection by sampling candidate actions, predicting the resulting web states, and ranking them with a ranker model or a Process Reward Model (PRM). These world models are typically trained via supervised next-state prediction to generate fixed representations like HTML or AXTree snapshots. However, this objective is misaligned with the downstream ranker, which relies on predicted states being discriminative across candidates to accurately score them. To address this, we introduce predicted-state matching, a training objective where the predicted representation must distinguish the true resulting state from those reached by alternative actions. We train these models using a branching web-agent dataset derived from WebArena Go-Browse trajectories, where every decision point contains multiple alternative actions and their resulting states. Experiments on our held-out predicted-state matching benchmark show that our approach outperforms world models trained with supervised next-state prediction. We further show that our approach improves PRM-style action ranking on WebPRMBench compared with action-only PRMs and PRMs augmented with supervised-next-state world models. Finally, on WebArena-Lite, using our world model for test-time action selection improves end-to-end task success. Our project page is available at: https://dhruvpendharkar.github.io/dwm/.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [256] [WMLLM: Self-Evolving Optimization Agents via Predict-Then-Act World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01608)
*Zhongzheng Li,Qingsong Ran,Shikun Feng,Nian Ran,Wenhao Li,Xiaoyuan Zhang,Yue Wang,Xiaoguang Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WMLLM，一个基于"先预测后行动"世界建模的自进化优化智能体框架，利用大语言模型的隐式知识预测有前景的优化方向，再生成候选解，并通过多轮细化、种群搜索和强化学习不断自我进化，在黑盒优化尤其是多目标分子优化任务上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒优化问题因搜索空间大、结构弱、维度高而充满挑战。现有方法依赖直接候选生成或试错式改进，样本效率低。作者观察到LLM能凭借隐式知识准确预测候选结果，因此希望利用世界建模来提升优化搜索效率。

Method: 提出WMLLM框架：(1)核心思路是predict-then-act（先预测后行动），智能体先预测有前景的方向，再据此生成候选；(2)结合智能体多轮细化、基于种群的搜索和强化学习；(3)在搜索过程中同时优化隐式世界模型与优化策略，实现自我进化。

Result: 在黑盒优化任务（尤其多目标分子优化）上，WMLLM提升了样本效率和最终优化性能；在多目标分子优化基准上，在有限评估预算下取得了最先进的实验结果。

Conclusion: 世界建模结合LLM的隐式知识能够有效指导黑盒优化方向，自进化机制（结合强化学习）可持续改进模型与策略，显著提升有限评估预算下的搜索性能。

Abstract: Black-box optimization problems remain challenging because of large, weakly structured, and high-dimensional search spaces. Existing methods often suffer from poor sample efficiency because they rely on direct candidate generation or trial-and-error refinement. A natural way to improve search efficiency is to use world modeling, which can help identify promising optimization directions before costly evaluation. Large language models can predict the outcomes of these candidates with nontrivial accuracy because of their implicit knowledge. Motivated by this observation, we propose WMLLM, a self-evolving optimization-agent framework based on predict-then-act world modeling. The agent first predicts promising directions and then acts to generate candidates. Combined with agentic multi-turn refinement, population-based search, and reinforcement learning, WMLLM refines both its implicit world model and its optimization strategy during search. Experiments on black-box optimization tasks, especially multi-objective molecular optimization, show that WMLLM improves sample efficiency and final optimization performance. On the multi-objective molecular optimization benchmark, WMLLM achieves state-of-the-art results under a limited evaluation budget.

</details>


### [257] [DiDrive: A Risk-Aware Hierarchical Diffusion Framework for Safe Offline Reinforcement Learning in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01609)
*Qisong Guo,Jingtang Chen,Zhilin Chen,Pei Xu,Mingjian Fu,Wenxi Liu,Yuanlong Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DiDrive是一种分布引导的离线扩散框架，包含风险感知层级扩散架构(RHDif)和3DICE策略优化范式，分别处理状态空间中的高维冗余和安全冗余，以及动作空间中的OOD生成和梯度振荡问题。在CARLA基准的60辆高密度交通场景中达到85%成功率和4295.68平均奖励，优于IQL、CQL和Diffusion-QL等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线RL策略在自动驾驶中仍面临四大挑战：分布偏移、重尾风险信号、OOD动作生成以及高维状态冗余，尽管扩散模型能有效捕捉多模态行为先验，但这些根本性问题尚未得到充分解决。

Method: 提出DiDrive框架，包含两个协同组件：(1) RHDif架构——在状态空间中利用低层风险门控编码器和高层上下文调制器来过滤环境冗余并聚焦安全关键威胁；(2) 3DICE策略优化——在动作空间中通过样本内校准引导、时空优化和基于集成候选排序来缓解OOD过估计和梯度振荡。

Result: 在CARLA基准测试中，DiDrive在复杂高密度交通场景（60辆车）下表现优于IQL、CQL和Diffusion-QL等基线，达到85%的成功率和4295.68的平均奖励。

Conclusion: DiDrive为安全自动驾驶决策提供了一条稳健的路径，有效结合了分布引导的扩散建模与风险感知的层级架构，在极具挑战性的高密度交通场景中展现了显著优势。

Abstract: While diffusion models effectively capture multimodal behavioral priors for autonomous driving, offline reinforcement learning (RL) policies remain susceptible to distribution shift, heavy-tailed risk signals, out-of-distribution (OOD) action generation, and high-dimensional state redundancy. To address these challenges, we propose DiDrive, a distribution-guided offline diffusion framework featuring two synergistic components: the Risk-Aware Hierarchical Diffusion (RHDif) architecture and the 3DICE policy optimization paradigm. In the state space, RHDif utilizes a low-level risk-gated encoder and a high-level contextual modulator to filter environmental redundancy and focus on safety-critical threats. In the action space, 3DICE mitigates OOD overestimation and gradient oscillation through in-sample calibrated guidance, spatiotemporal optimization, and ensemble-based candidate ranking. Evaluations on the CARLA benchmark demonstrate DiDrive's superiority over baselines like IQL, CQL, and Diffusion-QL, particularly in complex, high-density traffic scenarios with 60 vehicles, where it achieves an 85% success rate and a 4295.68 average reward, providing a robust pathway for safe autonomous driving decision-making.

</details>


### [258] [Prompt-Space Meta-Learning Does Not Transfer Across Users: A Frozen-LLM Negative Result](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01615)
*Liam Byrne,David Dylan,Orla Fitzgerald,Eoin Doyle,Ciara Nolan,Padraig Lynch,Sinead Gallagher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过Muse框架研究"元学习用户个性化提示"这一范式是否真正编码了可迁移的用户适应能力。实验表明，在LaMP-2和LaMP-3两个基准上，经元学习优化的共享提示并未显著优于未进化的种子提示，甚至被简单的少样本检索超越。作者将其归因于"元目标坍缩"：元验证目标对用户-支持样本的真实对应关系统计不敏感，因此无法优化出可迁移的适应策略，只能奖励提示润色和验证集过拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化冻结大语言模型常被表述为提示空间中的元学习问题，每个用户被视为一个任务。尽管这一框架因与骨干无关且复用提示优化机制而颇具吸引力，但领域内很少检验优化后的元目标是否真正编码了跨用户的可迁移适应能力，而非仅仅反映通用的指令质量。作者旨在揭示这一潜在混淆。

Method: 提出Muse框架，通过反射式提示进化在元训练用户群体上进化单一共享适应提示，冻结后零样本应用于未见用户。设计了严格的匹配对照实验来隔离真学习与措辞和选择产生的混淆：包括未进化的种子提示、破坏结构的错误支持对控制（在错配的用户-支持样本对上元训练），以及不变性预言机控制。

Result: 在两个标准基准（LaMP-2分类、LaMP-3评分）各200个未见用户上：Muse未显著优于其自身未进化的种子提示或结构破坏的控制；在评分任务上被普通少样本检索显著超越（Delta MAE +0.175, p<0.001）。元验证目标对用户-支持对应关系是否真实在统计上不敏感（LaMP-2 p=0.555，LaMP-3 p=0.622），证实了元目标坍缩机制。

Conclusion: 提示空间中的元学习个性化在实践中并未学到跨用户的可迁移适应策略，而是坍缩为指令润色与验证集过拟合。作者提供的种子提示、错误支持与控制不变性预言机构成的协议，可作为可复用的方法论，用以区分真实学习到的适应与这些混淆因素。

Abstract: Personalizing a frozen large language model (LLM) to individual users is often framed as a meta-learning problem in prompt space: each user is a task, and one seeks a shared natural-language adaptation policy that, given a handful of the user's labeled interactions, configures the frozen model for that user. The framing is attractive because it is backbone-agnostic and reuses the machinery of prompt optimization, yet the field rarely tests whether the optimized meta-objective encodes transferable cross-user adaptation rather than generic instruction quality. We study this question with Muse (Meta-learned User-adaptation via Shared Evolution), which evolves a single shared adaptation prompt over a meta-train user population by reflective prompt evolution, freezes it, and applies it zero-shot to held-out users; matched controls isolate learning from confounds of phrasing and selection. On two standard personalization benchmarks (LaMP-2 categorization and LaMP-3 rating) over 200 held-out users each, Muse does not significantly improve on its own un-evolved seed prompt or on a structure-broken control that meta-trains on mismatched user-support pairs, and is dominated by plain few-shot retrieval on the rating task (Delta MAE +0.175, p < 0.001). We attribute these outcomes to a single mechanism, meta-objective collapse: the meta-validation objective is statistically invariant to whether the user-support correspondence is genuine (p=0.555 on LaMP-2, p=0.622 on LaMP-3), so it cannot be optimized into transferable adaptation and instead rewards instruction polish and validation overfitting. The seed-prompt, wrong-support, and invariance-oracle controls form a reusable protocol that separates learned adaptation from these confounds.

</details>


### [259] [Efficient Context-Limited Telescope Bibliography Classification for the WASP-2025 Shared Task Using SciBERT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01647)
*Madhusudhana Naidu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于SciBERT的高效方法，用于自动将科学论文分类为科学、仪器、提及和非望远镜四类，用于构建望远镜文献目录。该方法在WASP-2025排行榜上以0.89的宏F1分数位居榜首。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 望远镜文献目录的创建对评估天文台科学影响和确保天文学可复现性至关重要，但该过程目前主要依赖人工且资源密集。现有方法缺乏高效自动化的解决方案。

Method: 采用基于SciBERT的分类方法，在严格的上下文长度限制（最大512个token）和有限计算资源的条件下，将科学论文自动分类为四个类别：科学、仪器、提及和非望远镜。同时分析了截断效果，并与分块（chunking）和长上下文模型进行了对比。

Result: 该方法在WASP-2025排行榜上取得宏F1分数0.89的成绩，排名第一。即使超过一半样本超出token限制，SciBERT的领域对齐仍能实现稳健的分类。

Conclusion: 本文讨论了截断、分块和长上下文模型之间的权衡，为科学文本整理提供了效率边界的见解，展示了在资源受限条件下利用领域预训练模型进行高效科学文献分类的可行性。

Abstract: The creation of telescope bibliographies is a crucial part of assessing the scientific impact of observatories and ensuring reproducibility in astronomy. This task involves identifying, categorizing, and linking scientific publications that reference or use specific telescopes. However, this process remains largely manual and resource intensive. In this work, we present an efficient SciBERT-based approach for automatic classification of scientific papers into four categories - science, instrumentation, mention, and not telescope. Despite strict context-length constraints (maximum 512 tokens) and limited compute resources, our approach achieved a macro F1 score of 0.89, ranking at the top of the WASP-2025 leaderboard. We analyze the effect of truncation and show that even with half the samples exceeding the token limit, SciBERT's domain alignment enables robust classification. We discuss trade-offs between truncation, chunking, and long-context models, providing insights into the efficiency frontier for scientific text curation.

</details>


### [260] [CliffRank: A Dual-Branch Framework for Activity-Cliff Ranking Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01673)
*Kewei Li,Rongying Zhang,Peiyu Yang,Zhongjian Wang,Qiuchen Zhao,Lan Huang,Fengfeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CliffRank方法，将绝对活性回归与排序一致性学习相结合（包括阈值化listwise损失和Pairwise Preference Consistency），用于解决活性悬崖（activity-cliff）排序难题，在多种抗菌肽和小分子数据集上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活性悬崖排序仍然困难，因为局部结构变化可能导致巨大活性差异，而能解析底层机制的高质量数据有限。现有方法未能充分利用有限的活性标签来有效解决这一问题。

Method: CliffRank训练两个并行预测器，使用均方误差（MSE）、阈值化listwise损失以及Pairwise Preference Consistency（PPC）三种损失函数。PPC在偏好概率空间中对齐相对排序。模型在不同数据集上使用ESM2-t12（抗菌肽）和PNA（小分子）等不同架构，并采用延迟PPC激活和不对称初始化策略。

Result: 在三个抗菌肽数据集上，CliffRank+ESM2-t12取得平均Spearman相关性0.5393和平均Recall@50为21.4的最高结果；在三个小分子数据集上，CliffRank+PNA（PPC在120个epoch后激活）取得平均Spearman相关性0.6890的最高结果，平均Recall@50为30.4与ACANet-PNA持平。延迟PPC和不对称初始化仅部分有效。

Conclusion: CliffRank通过结合回归与排序一致性学习能有效提升活性悬崖排序性能，但PPC的实际限制仍需探讨。未来应评估更多靶点和抗菌肽体系，开发自适应PPC调度方案，并在可行时纳入蛋白质或膜上下文信息。

Abstract: Activity-cliff ranking remains difficult because local structural changes can cause large activity differences, while high-quality data that resolve the underlying mechanisms remain limited. To use available activity labels more effectively, we combine absolute-activity regression with ranking-consistency learning. CliffRank trains two parallel predictors with mean squared error, a thresholded listwise loss, and Pairwise Preference Consistency (PPC), which aligns relative ordering in the preference-probability space. On three antimicrobial peptide datasets, CliffRank with ESM2-t12 achieved the highest mean Spearman correlation of 0.5393 and mean Recall@50 of 21.4, although the leading method varied across individual datasets. On three small-molecule datasets, CliffRank with PNA, where PPC was activated after 120 epochs, achieved the highest mean Spearman correlation of 0.6890, while its mean Recall@50 of 30.4 matched that of ACANet-PNA. The PPC results also define its practical limits. Asymmetric initialization improved the MolCLR-GIN averages but did not improve every target. For PNA without pretrained weights, delayed PPC improved selected metrics, but no schedule was best for both mean Spearman correlation and mean Recall@50. Future work should evaluate more targets and antimicrobial peptide systems, develop adaptive PPC schedules, and incorporate protein or membrane context when available.

</details>


### [261] [H3DNAS: Hardware-Aware ONNX-Native 3D Point Cloud Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02684)
*Anchit Mulye,Rhythm Baghel,Sujay Kumar Ingle,Hardik Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: H3DNAS是一个面向3D点云模型的硬件感知压缩框架，可直接操作ONNX计算图进行压缩，无需原始源代码或梯度访问。在ModelNet40上对PointNet、PointNet++和PointMLP分别实现了65.5%、43.2%和49.1%的参数缩减，以及1.99×、1.29×和1.67×的推理加速，精度损失可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano等边缘硬件上部署3D点云模型受限于计算和内存预算。现有压缩方法需要访问模型原始源代码，因而无法应用于模型仓库和厂商广泛分发的ONNX二进制文件，亟需一种无需源代码即可压缩ONNX格式3D点云模型的方法。

Method: H3DNAS包含三项核心贡献：(1) 通道依赖图(CDG)，将ONNX算子分为四类约束，形式上证明自由参数比例ρ_f是拓扑不变量，提供可在O(|V|+|E|)时间内计算的压缩上限；(2) 两阶段分层搜索，先通过L1重要性通道选择剪枝候选架构，再用零样本无标签代理按输出保真度排序，并对Pareto最优候选应用GhostConv结构变异；(3) 首个无需源代码的3D点云模型压缩流程，完全通过ONNX图手术实现。

Result: 在ModelNet40数据集上，H3DNAS将PointNet、PointNet++和PointMLP的参数分别减少65.5%、43.2%和49.1%，实现1.99×、1.29×和1.67×的推理加速，且精度损失可忽略。

Conclusion: H3DNAS证明了无需源代码和梯度即可对ONNX格式的3D点云模型进行有效的硬件感知压缩，为边缘设备上的模型部署提供了切实可行的解决方案，源代码已公开。

Abstract: Deploying 3D point cloud models on edge hardware such as the NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano is severely constrained by compute and memory budgets. Existing compression methods require access to the model's original source code, rendering them inapplicable to the Open Neural Network Exchange (ONNX) binaries commonly distributed by vendors and model repositories. We present \textbf{H3DNAS}, a hardware-aware model compression framework that operates directly on ONNX computational graphs without requiring original source code, architecture class definition, or gradient access during search. H3DNAS makes three contributions: (1) a \textbf{Channel Dependency Graph (CDG)} that classifies ONNX operators into four constraint classes and formally establishes that the free parameter fraction $ρ_f$ is topological invariant, a provable compression ceiling computable in $\mathcal{O}(|V|+|E|)$; (2) a \textbf{Two-Stage Hierarchical Search} that prunes candidate architectures by $L_1$-importance channel selection, ranks them by output fidelity as a zero-shot label-free proxy, and applies GhostConv structural mutation to Pareto-optimal candidates; and (3) the \textbf{first source-code-free compression pipeline for 3D point cloud models}, operating entirely via ONNX graph surgery with no original architecture definition required. On ModelNet40, H3DNAS reduces the number of parameters in PointNet, PointNet++, and PointMLP by $65.5\%$, $43.2\%$, and $49.1\%$, respectively, while achieving $1.99\times$, $1.29\times$, and $1.67\times$ inference speedups with negligible loss in accuracy. The source code is publicly available\footnote{https://github.com/ClarityLab-Org/h3dnas}.

</details>


### [262] [Sim2Signal: Sim-to-Real Benchmarks for Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01676)
*Ferdous Al Rafi,Susrik Mukherjee,Latika Liladhar Dekate,Jennifer Yawa Lavoe,Huaiyuan Yao,Shlok Mohanty,Longchao Da,Xuesong Zhou,Hua Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Sim2Signal基准框架，系统地将交通信号控制中强化学习的Sim-to-Real差距分解为观测、动作、转移和奖励四种差距（对应MDP四个组成部分），在共享协议下独立诱导每种差距。研究评估了18种缓解方法、2种基础控制器、33种差距设置及10个基于5个真实地点构建的校准网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实世界中，强化学习训练的交通信号控制策略部署后常因Sim-to-Real差距而失效，但该差距各来源（感知、动作执行、交通动态、控制目标）的相对影响和现有缓解方法的可靠性尚未被充分理解，且领域缺乏标准基准来衡量差距和评估缓解方法。

Method: 将Sim-to-Real差距分解为观测、动作、转移和奖励四种差距，对应MDP四个组成部分的不匹配，并在共享协议下单独诱导每种差距。基于5个真实地点构建10个校准网络，系统评估18种缓解方法在2个基础控制器上的表现。

Result: 直接迁移在四种差距源上均持续降低性能，但性能下降的严重程度并不能预测缓解方法的有效性。缓解效果强烈依赖于网络和差距设置：在动作差距之外，某方法在一种情况下有效可能在另一种情况下失败。最有效的方法通常是估计差距所带来的变化，而非通过域随机化或不变表征使策略变得不敏感。

Conclusion: Sim2Signal为交通信号控制的Sim-to-Real研究提供了标准化的评估基准，揭示了缓解方法的有效性具有高度情境依赖性，并建议优先采用估计差距的方法而非域随机化等不敏感化策略。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning achieves strong traffic signal control performance in simulation, yet policies trained in simulators often fail once deployed in the real world, a failure known as the Sim-to-Real gap. When RL is applied to traffic signal control, this gap arises from several sources: sensing, action execution, traffic dynamics, and the control objective. Their relative impact and the reliability of existing Sim-to-Real mitigation methods remain insufficiently understood, and the field lacks a standard benchmark for systematically measuring the gap and evaluating mitigation methods. We present Sim2Signal, a benchmark that decomposes the Sim-to-Real gap into observation, action, transition, and reward gaps, corresponding to mismatches in the four components of the underlying MDP, and induces each gap in isolation under a shared protocol. We evaluate 18 mitigation methods on 2 base controllers, across 33 gap settings and 10 calibrated networks built from 5 real-world locations. We find that direct transfer consistently degrades performance across all four gap sources, but the severity of the degradation does not predict the effectiveness of mitigation. Instead, mitigation effectiveness depends strongly on the network and gap setting: outside the action gap, a method that helps in one case may fail in another. The most effective methods generally estimate what the gap changes, rather than make the policy insensitive through domain randomization or invariant representations. Our code is available at https://github.com/Red-Pheonix/Sim2RealTSCBenchMark

</details>
