After validating these gradients against finite differences, we apply them to a concrete problem: allocating 50 MW of data-center load across six candidate buses in two synthetic networks, under a fixed cost per active site, evaluated over 36 operating states. Judged against exhaustive enumeration of all site combinations, gradient optimization recovers the continuous allocations almost exactly, with worst-case objective gaps of 2.3\% and 8.5\% of the cost difference between the best and worst single site. Its one systematic error is instructive: near the costs at which a site should close, the smooth relaxation of the discrete site count shrinks the site rather than closing it, so discrete transitions arrive late. Differentiable market clearing thus turns market-aware planning into a problem gradients can search.

</details>


### [331] [Unfolding the Leech Lattice: Fused Multi-Shell Decoding and VRAM Layouts for 2-Bit LLM Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02652)
*Pier-Jean Malandrino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为Leech格矢量量化（最先进的2-bit量化方案）提供了此前缺失的多shell解码器实现，并在batch 1下测定了解码阶段GEMV的部署成本。研究揭示了在VRAM内的码率与磁盘码率是独立的设计维度，通过逐位精确的GPU布局与融合的反量化矩阵向量乘内核，实现了不同码率下的解码方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Leech格矢量量化声称拥有最强的2-bit质量，但其内核仅解码一个shell，缺乏满足码率要求的多shell解码器实现。作者旨在填补这一实现空白，并系统度量该方案在真实服务场景（batch 1的解码阶段GEMV）中的性能与成本。

Method: 作者提供完整301类码本的部署路径：离线扩展为GPU布局，编写融合的反量化加矩阵向量乘内核（无warp发散），并用f64浮点精确性验证。通过四种逐位精确布局的时序对比，分析二进制位平面与one-hot掩码在尺寸和速度上的差异。同时在同一进程中对比AWQ/QTIP内核与trellis内核，并测量在第二套内存层次与不同输出头下的端到端性能。

Result: 逐位精确布局在恒定带宽下达到4.80比特每权重（FP16的2.15倍）。trellis内核比作者的布局少读取2.40倍字节且快2.27倍。端到端在4B、8B、14B规模下分别提升1.11x、1.29x、1.41x；使用int8输出头时4B达到87.0 tok/s（占用2.60 GB）。质量代价为1.38倍困惑度和4B下14.7个MMLU点，且随模型规模增大而缩小。

Conclusion: 作者认为VRAM内码率与磁盘码率是不同的设计轴，并指出时间差距与流量差距紧密相关——这源于展开一个对查找表而言过大的码本所付出的代价。当码率低于4.3比特后会引入不规则的二次流，低于3.6比特时解码不再基于移位和掩码操作。

Abstract: Leech-lattice vector quantization holds the strongest reported 2-bit quality under its own evaluation protocol. Its kernel decodes one shell; we found no implementation of the multi-shell decoder the rate requires. This paper supplies one and measures its serving cost for decode-phase GEMV at batch 1. First, a serving path for the full 301-class codebook: an offline expansion into GPU layouts and a fused dequantize-plus-matvec kernel reading them without warp divergence, verified against f64. Second, the in-VRAM rate is a design axis distinct from the on-disk rate. Four bit-exact layouts timed in one process show binary bit planes beating one-hot masks on size and speed at constant bandwidth (4.80 bits per weight, 2.15x FP16). Below 4.3 bits a second, irregular stream enters; at 3.6 the decode stops being shifts and masks. Third, deployed four-bit (AWQ) and two-bit (QTIP) GEMV kernels run in the same process. The trellis kernel reads 2.40x fewer bytes than our served layout and runs 2.27x faster at near-equal fractions of their byte bounds: the time gap tracks the traffic gap, the price of unfolding a codebook too large for a lookup table. Fourth, the validity envelope: the trellis kernel outruns our no-weights control, so our launch geometry sets that floor, and on a second memory hierarchy every lattice arm falls below FP16. With the output head held identical across arms, the kernel-and-format path gains 1.11x, 1.29x and 1.41x end to end at 4B, 8B and 14B; with an int8 output head the served 4B reaches 87.0 tok/s in 2.60 GB. The quality cost, 1.38x perplexity and 14.7 MMLU points at 4B, shrinks across the three sizes measured.

</details>


### [332] [LoRA-TSD: Tangent-Space Spectral Descent for LoRA via Muon-Style Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02734)
*Dmitrii Andriianov,Andrey Veprikov,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoRA-TSD是一种针对LoRA微调的优化器，将每次LoRA更新视为固定秩矩阵流形上的切向量，在切空间内执行Muon的谱范数最速下降步，并通过LoRA参数化原生的回缩将结果映射回因子，避免了对完整权重矩阵的昂贵操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LoRA的两个因子独立训练时忽略了其诱导的低秩权重变化所对应的几何结构，导致优化未考虑低秩矩阵流形的内在几何。

Method: 将LoRA步骤建模为固定秩矩阵流形的切向量，采用Muon的谱范数最速下降，并通过LoRA参数化原生的回缩映射回因子；证明Frobenius范数版本等价于LoRA-Pro；以切向投影梯度（黎曼梯度）作为平稳性度量，给出全局收敛保证。

Result: 回缩运算比截断SVD回缩快2.8倍；在Llama-3.2-1B、Llama-3.1-8B和Qwen3-32B上的六个常识推理和自然语言推理基准中，LoRA-TSD优于所有对比的LoRA优化器，且对adapter秩保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: LoRA-TSD通过几何感知的优化和高效的流形回缩，为LoRA微调提供了更优且更具理论保障的优化方法，并首次为LoRA-Pro和LoRA-TSD给出了全局收敛保证。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is the standard way to fine-tune large models, yet when its two factors are trained independently, the update ignores the geometry of the low-rank weight change it induces. We introduce LoRA-TSD, an optimizer that treats every LoRA step as a tangent vector of the fixed-rank matrix manifold and takes the spectral-norm steepest-descent step of Muon inside that tangent space, mapping the result back to the factors through a retraction native to the LoRA parametrization. The step avoids expensive operations on full weight matrices, and its retraction is up to $2.8\times$ cheaper than the truncated-SVD retraction used by prior manifold methods. We prove that the Frobenius-norm version of our surrogate recovers LoRA-Pro, and we identify the tangent-projected gradient, the Riemannian gradient of the manifold, as the stationarity measure natural to LoRA training and computable from the factor gradients alone. Under this measure we give the first global convergence guarantees for both LoRA-Pro and LoRA-TSD, with rates that drive the factor-gradient norms to zero. Across six commonsense and natural-language-inference benchmarks with Llama-3.2-1B, Llama-3.1-8B and Qwen3-32B, LoRA-TSD outperforms every competing LoRA optimizer and stays robust to the adapter rank. Code is available at https://github.com/brain-lab-research/LoRA-TSD.

</details>


### [333] [Do Tabular Foundation Models Know Physics? Contamination, Units, and the Deterministic Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02766)
*Wassim Tenachi,Yashar Hezaveh,Laurence Perreault Levasseur,Pierre-Luc Bacon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了表格基础模型（TFMs）是否学到了物理知识。作者在316个物理方程生成的数据集上评估了四种TFM（TabPFN-3、TabICLv2、TabDPT和Real-TabPFN-2.5）与六种基线模型的表现。结果显示TFMs在开箱即用和调优后均显著占优，但其先验无法表示无噪声机制和物理单位，因此它们能内插物理数据但尚不能作为真正的物理模型使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格是大多数物理测量数据的格式，表格基础模型声称能像语言模型填充文本一样填充表格。由于TFMs本质上是贝叶斯模型，作者想要探究其先验中是否包含物理知识，即它们是否在训练过程中学到了真正的物理规律。

Method: 作者在316个物理方程中采样数据集，分为域内与域外两组，对四种TFM（TabPFN-3、TabICLv2、TabDPT、Real-TabPFN-2.5）和六种基线模型进行系统评估，包括开箱即用和调优两种设置，直接探测模型先验的物理内容。

Result: TFMs在物理数据集上表现优于所有基线模型，无论是在开箱即用还是调优后。然而，作者通过探测发现TFMs的先验无法表示无噪声的确定性机制（即精确物理规律），也无法编码物理单位信息，说明其成功来自插值能力而非对物理的深层理解。

Conclusion: TFMs虽然能在物理数据上实现优秀的插值表现，但其先验缺乏表示精确物理机制和物理单位的能力。它们善于在训练分布覆盖的物理场景中内插预测，但尚不能替代真正的物理模型来外推或建模物理世界。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) learn to fill in tables the way language models fill in text, and tables are arguably the format in which most physical measurement arrives. Did they learn any physics in the process? They are Bayesian by construction, so the question is what their prior contains. We probe it directly, evaluating four of them (TabPFN-3, TabICLv2, TabDPT and Real-TabPFN-2.5) against six baselines on datasets sampled from 316 physical equations, in and out of domain. TFMs dominate, out of the box and after tuning. But we show that their prior can represent neither a noiseless mechanism nor physical units, which is why they interpolate physics without yet being able to act as physical models.

</details>


### [334] [Cliff: Learning Process Rewards from the First Mistake](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02817)
*Peixuan Han,Runhui Wang,Ketan Ramaneti,Jie Hao,Gerald Friedland,Chris Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Cliff，一种奖励塑形策略，利用现成的LLM作为教师来识别每个rollout中的第一个错误，将推理过程分解为正确前缀和错误后缀，并据此分配token级别的正负优势信号，从而为RLVR提供更细粒度的监督。实验表明Cliff在12个场景中一致优于在线蒸馏(15%)和标准GRPO(7%)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR依赖粗粒度的结果奖励，无法为中间推理过程提供充分指导。现有方法如过程奖励建模和在线蒸馏存在额外限制（依赖专门的奖励模型或假设师生推理模式相同）。作者观察到一旦推理先出错，后续推理评估信息量有限，因此需要更有效的中间监督信号。

Method: Cliff利用现成的LLM作为教师，识别每个rollout中第一个推理错误的位置。推理过程被自然地分解为正确前缀和错误后缀两部分，Cliff将此信号转化为token级别的优势：正确前缀分配正优势，错误后缀分配负反馈。整个过程不依赖专门的奖励模型，也不需要师生推理模式一致。

Result: 在12个不同场景的实验中，Cliff一致地提升了推理性能，相比在线蒸馏提升15%，相比标准GRPO提升7%，即使在教师能力有限的情况下也表现出色。论文还分析了"ground truth"在Cliff中的作用及其训练动态。

Conclusion: Cliff是一种简单、通用且有效的RLVR改进方法，通过更丰富的细粒度监督信号增强了强化学习训练的效果。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for large language model (LLM) post-training, but its reliance on coarse outcome rewards leads to limited guidance on intermediate reasoning processes. Existing approaches such as process reward modeling and on-policy distillation introduce additional constraints, such as reliance on a specialized reward model or assuming identical reasoning patterns between teacher and student. Nevertheless, we observe that once a reasoning process first goes wrong, evaluating the subsequent reasoning provides limited additional information, as it is already conditioned on an invalid prefix. Therefore, we propose Cliff, a reward shaping strategy that utilizes an off-the-shelf LLM as a teacher to identify the first mistake in each rollout. As a result, the rollout is naturally decomposed into two parts: a correct prefix and an incorrect suffix. Cliff then converts this signal into token-level advantages, assigning positive advantages for the correct prefix and negative feedback afterward. Experiments across 12 different scenarios demonstrate that Cliff consistently improves reasoning performance, outperforming on-policy distillation by 15% and standard GRPO by 7%, even with teachers of modest capability. Furthermore, we analyse the role of ``ground truth'' in Cliff and investigate its training dynamics. These results establish Cliff as a simple, general and effective approach for improving RLVR with richer, fine-grained supervision.

</details>


### [335] [UE5M3 FP4 Block Scaling for Stable Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02846)
*Robert Hu,Carlo Luschi,Paul Balanca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种更简单的4-bit浮点(FP4)预训练方案，将E2M1有效载荷与无符号E5M3块缩放配对，替代NVIDIA Transformer Engine中需要RHT和BF16最终层的复杂方案。在Nemotron-H 8B模型上预训练近1900亿token，该方法在训练损失和验证损失上均优于Transformer Engine方案，并且消除RHT和BF16最终层豁免可使吞吐量提升21.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定的4-bit浮点(FP4)预训练很困难，因为E2M1有效载荷只能表示很窄的数值范围。NVIDIA Transformer Engine的recipe依赖当前张量缩放、随机Hadamard变换(RHT)和BF16最终层，这些都在FP4矩阵乘法之外增加了额外工作，复杂且低效。

Method: 提出将E2M1有效载荷与非对称E5M3块缩放配对，利用其更宽的数值范围实现周期性张量缩放。配方采用选择性随机舍入处理反向梯度，省略RHT，并允许所有符合条件的内部线性层使用FP4。

Result: 在Nemotron-H 8B模型上预训练近1900亿token，相比Transformer Engine方案，块16配方取得更低的最终窗口训练损失和验证损失(NLL)。在所有三个报告的下游任务聚合指标上，量化推理点估计也更高。消除RHT和BF16最终块豁免的消融实验显示模型主体token吞吐量提升21.2%。

Conclusion: 实验证明端到端软件模拟的E5M3块缩放FP4预训练可用更简单的配方实现，并论证了原生支持E5M3块缩放的必要性。

Abstract: Stable 4-bit floating-point (FP4) pretraining is difficult because the E2M1 payload represents only a narrow range of magnitudes. NVIDIA's Transformer Engine \nv{} recipe addresses this with current-tensor scaling, a randomized Hadamard transform (RHT), and bfloat16 (BF16) final layers, adding work outside the FP4 matrix multiplications. We instead pair E2M1 payloads with unsigned E5M3 (\ue{}) block scales. Their wider range permits periodic tensor scaling, while our recipe applies selective stochastic rounding to backward gradients, omits RHT, and uses FP4 in all eligible internal linears.
  We pretrain a Nemotron-H 8B model for nearly 190 billion tokens. Compared with Transformer Engine \nv{}, the proposed block-16 recipe finishes with lower final-window training loss and, under their respective quantized-inference policies, lower validation loss measured as held-out negative log-likelihood. Its quantized-inference downstream point estimates are also higher on all three reported aggregates. A native \nv{} execution ablation that jointly removes RHT and the BF16 final-block exemption increases measured model-body token throughput by 21.2\%. These results demonstrate end-to-end software-emulated \uefp{} pretraining with a simpler recipe and motivate native support for \ue{} block scaling.

</details>


### [336] [The Implications of Linguistic Illegibility for LLM Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02852)
*James Mickens*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"语言不可读性"（linguistic illegibility）概念，指LLM外部语言输出或机制层面提取的语言特征无法真实反映其内部计算。由于LLM内部计算本质上是激活空间中的数学运算，与自然语言之间的转换是有损的，因此语言不可读性不可避免。这意味着依赖模型语言自报告的安全机制（如思维链监控、宪法自我批评、激活探针）永远无法完全可靠，沙箱需要不依赖读取语言状态的隔离技术。作者主张污点追踪（taint tracking）是构建有效沙箱的有前景方法，并讨论虚拟化、第三方审计等补充机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有证据表明LLM的语言输出和通过机械解释提取的语言特征可能无法准确反映其内部计算。如果安全机制（如思维链监控、激活探针等）依赖模型的语言自报告，而语言输出又不可靠，那么这些机制就存在根本性的安全漏洞。作者试图指出这一问题的不可避免性，并为构建更可靠的安全沙箱提供理论基础和新方向。

Method: 本文主要通过理论论证的方式，提出"语言不可读性"这一术语，论证其不可避免性（内部计算是激活空间上的数学运算，语言转换有损）。在此基础上提出沙箱机制设计思路：污点追踪策略（预先定义不应受模型输出影响的系统状态并进行追踪）、稳健虚拟化、第三方审计沙箱配置等，这些机制不依赖读取模型的语言状态。

Result: 作者论证了：1）语言不可读性对LLM而言不可避免；2）依赖语言自报告的安全机制在原理上无法完全健全；3）污点追踪等不依赖语言读取的沙箱隔离技术是更有前景的方向；4）提出的补充机制组合"collectively provide a critical floor beneath linguistic monitoring"，理论上可以缓解前沿模型最近的沙箱漏洞。

Conclusion: 语言不可读性是LLM安全防护中必须正视的根本性问题。由于模型内部计算本质上是非语言的数学运算，任何依赖语言自报告的安全机制都只是"最佳努力"而无法提供保证。安全边界必须下移到不读取语言状态的技术层面（如污点追踪、虚拟化隔离、第三方审计），为语言监控提供可靠的地基。

Abstract: LLMs are trained to generate natural language. However, various strands of evidence indicate that an LLM's externalized linguistic outputs and mechanistically-extracted linguistic features can be an unreliable lens for understanding internal model computation. We introduce the term ``linguistic illegibility'' to broadly refer to scenarios in which an LLM's externalized or mechanistically-probed language artifacts fail to represent how the model actually thinks. We argue that the specter of linguistic illegibility is unavoidable for LLMs whose internal computations are not directly expressed via language, but rather math over activation spaces (with lossy translations between activation spaces and natural language happening at the bookends). If linguistic illegibility is always possible, then security mechanisms that rely on a model's linguistic self-reporting (e.g., chain-of-thought monitoring, constitutional self-critique, activation probing for linguistically-defined feature vectors) can never be completely sound; the model sandbox will always need isolation techniques whose guarantees do not depend on reading a model's linguistic state at all. We argue that observing a model's outputs using taint tracking is a promising approach for an effective sandbox: regardless of how a model linguistically self-reports, a taint tracking policy can define, a priori, various pieces of system state that should never be influenced by model-produced data. We also discuss several additional sandboxing mechanisms (e.g., robust virtualization, third-party auditing of sandboxing configurations) which collectively provide a critical floor beneath linguistic monitoring, and would have mitigated recent sandbox exploits by frontier models.

</details>


### [337] [Graph Machine: Towards Better Pretraining via Edges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02881)
*Lintai Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出图机（Graph Machine, GM）架构，通过维护O(n)大小的状态并使用稀疏动态路由访问，取代Transformer中75%的稠密层。在Qwen3-0.6B上以15.7B token从头预训练，仅检索2/4096个token时损失轻微退化，检索4个时最佳模型损失略有改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两难：固定大小状态容量受限，而稀疏静态路由虽降低复杂度但可访问状态被限制为O(1)。作者希望在不牺牲可访问状态规模的前提下保持O(n)的计算复杂度。

Method: GM架构维护O(n)大小的状态，通过类似指针追踪的差异化"推荐机制"更新边（edge）——即指针类对象，实现稀疏动态路由。具体实验中将Qwen3-0.6B中75%的稠密Transformer层替换为GM稀疏层，每个KV头仅检索4,096个token中的2个或4个。

Result: 在15.7B token从头预训练中，每稀疏层每KV头仅检索2/4,096个token时损失仅略微退化；检索4个token时最佳模型的损失甚至略有改善。

Conclusion: GM证明了可以在保持O(n)稀疏层复杂度的同时，不限制可访问状态大小；通过极低比例的token检索即可接近甚至超越稠密Transformer的性能，展示了动态稀疏路由在大模型中的潜力。

Abstract: We introduce the Graph Machine (GM), an architecture that maintains an $O(n)$-sized state and accesses it through sparse, dynamic routing. Unlike methods with fixed-size states or sparse but static routing, GM preserves $O(n)$ complexity in its sparse layers without restricting the potentially accessible state size to $O(1)$. Instead, GM uses edges - pointer-like objects updated differentiably by a referral mechanism resembling pointer chasing. We replace 75% of the dense Transformer layers in Qwen3-0.6B with GM sparse layers and pretrain from scratch on 15.7B tokens. With only 2 of 4,096 tokens retrieved per KV head in each sparse layer, loss degrades only slightly; with 4, the best model marginally improves loss.

</details>


### [338] [A Common Measure of Communication for Speech Brain-Computer Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02887)
*Dulhan Jayalath,Benjamin Ballyk,Oiwi Parker Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的信息论指标——开放词汇互信息（OVMI），用于统一衡量语音脑机接口（speech BCI）系统的通信能力，解决了该领域因数据集、词汇、录音方式不同而缺乏可比性的测量问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音BCI领域缺乏统一的性能度量标准，各系统使用不同数据集、录音方法、语音类型和词汇，导致报告分数无法直接比较。根本问题在于：(i) 系统应支持用户表达什么样的词汇分布；(ii) 系统能从该分布中传递多少信息量。

Method: 推导开放词汇互信息（OVMI），一种基于信息论的度量，衡量解码器相对于用户可能想表达的词汇参考分布所传递的信息量。OVMI将不同条件下（如不同词汇表）测得的系统能力映射到统一的通信尺度上进行比较。

Result: 传统的准确率和词错误率（WER）等指标只针对系统支持的词汇计算，可能高估系统实际传达用户意图的能力。使用OVMI对比现有系统，揭示系统支持语言范围与解码精度之间的权衡；这些比较依赖于用户预期表达的词汇分布。通过最大化OVMI来选择词汇表，在三个语音域中准确率相对提升了高达16.3%。

Conclusion: OVMI为语音BCI社区提供了一种原则性的方法来比较异构系统、改进词汇设计并衡量该领域的进展。

Abstract: Speech brain-computer interfaces (speech BCIs) translate neural activity into language, offering a path towards restoring speech for people with paralysis and, more broadly, enabling new forms of natural human-computer interaction. Despite this promise, the field lacks a common measure of progress because systems use different datasets, recording methods, types of speech, and vocabularies, so their reported scores are rarely comparable. Underlying this measurement problem are two unresolved questions: (i) what distribution of words should a speech BCI enable a user to communicate, and (ii) how much information from this distribution can a system convey. We address both by deriving open-vocabulary mutual information (OVMI), an information-theoretic quantity that measures the information conveyed by a decoder relative to a reference distribution over the words a user may wish to communicate. This allows capabilities measured under different conditions, such as distinct vocabularies, to be evaluated on a common communication scale. We show that ordinarily reported accuracy, word error rate (WER), and other metrics computed only over the words a system supports can overstate how much of a user's intended speech the system can communicate. We then use OVMI to compare existing systems, expose trade-offs between how much of the user's language a system supports and how accurately it decodes those words, show that these comparisons depend on what the user is expected to communicate, and demonstrate that selecting a vocabulary to maximise OVMI yields up to 16.3% relative improvement in accuracy across three speech domains. OVMI therefore provides the speech BCI community with a principled way to compare heterogeneous systems, improve vocabulary design, and measure progress in the field.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [339] [Transfer Safety Awareness for Cross-Modal Safety Drift in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02082)
*Tianqi Xiao,Shiyao Cui,Minghao Zhang,Junxiao Yang,Renmiao Chen*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文系统研究多模态大语言模型（MLLM）中的跨模态安全漂移问题——即良性文本查询在结合图像后可能携带有害意图。作者提出了一种轻量级的安全感知表示迁移（SRT）方法，通过方向细化在冻结MLLM骨干的情况下缓解该问题，实验证明SRT在多种跨模态场景下有效提升安全性且保持实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉模态增强了多模态大语言模型的能力，但也引入了安全隐患：良性文本查询在结合视觉图像后可能传达有害意图（即跨模态安全漂移）。实验表明此类请求的安全响应率远低于包含显式不安全文本的请求，亟需系统性研究并解决这一安全问题。

Method: 首先进行实证分析识别代表性的不安全响应模式；其次通过解释模型表示和注意力机制，发现视觉风险线索获得的注意力有限且难以触发拒绝机制；基于"不安全文本处理中的安全信号可以迁移"的观察，提出安全感知表示迁移（SRT）方法——一种轻量级的方向细化方法，在冻结MLLM骨干的情况下缓解跨模态安全漂移。

Result: 在多个基准和模型上的实验表明，SRT方法能在多样化的跨模态场景中有效提升安全性，同时保持模型原有实用性。

Conclusion: SRT证明了从文本安全处理中提取的安全信号可以迁移到视觉模态上，为缓解跨模态安全漂移提供了一种高效、轻量、与骨干模型解耦的解决方案。

Abstract: Visual modality enhances the capabilities of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) but also introduces a safety concern: a benign textual query may convey harmful intent when grounded in a visual image. We term this cross-modal safety drift and our pilot studies show that the safety response rate for such requests is substantially lower than that for requests containing explicitly unsafe text. This paper aims to systematically study this issue. First, we conduct an empirical analysis to identify representative unsafe response patterns. Building on these, we interpret model representations and attentions, revealing that visually risky cues receive limited attention and weakly trigger refusal. Motivated by the observation that safety signals from unsafe text processing can be transferred, we propose safety-awareness representation transfer (SRT), a lightweight direction-refinement method that mitigates cross-modal safety drift with a frozen MLLM backbone. Experiments across multiple benchmarks and models show that SRT effectively improves safety in diverse cross-modal settings while preserving utility. Code is available at https://github.com/cucu220123/safety-awareness.

</details>


### [340] [The Missing Temporal Link: Temporal Context Routing for Script-Driven Audio-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02367)
*Yichen Liu,Quanwei Zhang,Haozhe Wang,Donghao Zhou,Xiaojie Li,Yang Shi,Jiaming Liu,Ruihua Huang,Yingtian Zou,Daquan Zhou*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出Temporal Context Routing (TCR)，将结构化脚本的时间轴映射到音视频生成的共享时间轴上，使镜头切换和对话时间精确对齐脚本，大幅提升脚本驱动内容生成的时间准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联合音视频生成模型虽在视觉质量和音视频同步上取得进展，但缺乏对镜头切换时间和对话开始时间的精确控制。在脚本驱动的内容创作中，时序错误会破坏叙事连贯性。由于脚本时间仅编码在文本中，未与视频或音频的时间坐标对齐，导致视频音频彼此同步却都不能遵循脚本时间线。

Method: 提出Temporal Context Routing (TCR)，将脚本时间映射到音视频生成的共享时间轴，并将每个提示的引导信息路由到两种模态中对应的时序位置，实现脚本、视频、音频三者的时间对齐。

Result: 在200个测试脚本上，TCR将镜头边界平均绝对误差从1.11秒降至0.042秒（降低96%），对话准确率（Acc@0.5s）从28.3%提升至84.1%，同时保持与基线相当的视觉质量和音视频同步水平。用户研究显示TCR在全部五个评估维度上均获偏好。

Conclusion: 通过将脚本时间对齐纳入生成框架，TCR有效解决了脚本驱动内容生成中的时序控制问题，在不牺牲视觉质量和音视频同步的前提下显著提升时间精度，为叙事性内容生成提供了更可控的解决方案。

Abstract: Joint audio-video generation models have made substantial progress in visual quality and audio-visual synchronization. However, they still provide limited control over when shot transitions occur and dialogue is spoken. This limitation constrains their application in script-driven content creation, where timing errors can undermine narrative coherence and the viewing experience. Current joint generators align video and audio representations on a shared temporal axis, yet the precise timing of shots and dialogue specified in a structured prompt is encoded only in the prompt's text representation and remains unaligned with the temporal coordinates of either modality. Consequently, video and audio may remain synchronized with each other while both fail to follow the script timeline. This mismatch motivates us to extend temporal alignment beyond video and audio to include the structured script. We therefore introduce Temporal Context Routing (TCR), which maps the script timing onto the shared temporal axis of video and audio generation and routes each prompt's guidance to the corresponding positions in both modalities. Compared with the baseline on 200 test scripts, TCR reduces Shot Boundary MAE by 96%, from 1.11 s to 0.042 s, and raises Dialogue Acc@0.5 s from 28.3% to 84.1%. TCR achieves these improvements while maintaining visual quality and audio-visual synchronization comparable to those of the baselines. A user study further shows that participants prefer TCR on all five evaluated dimensions.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [341] [The Complexity of Justified Representation with Additive Utilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02030)
*Carmel Baharav,Jakob de Raaij,Agnès Totschnig*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了参与式预算和委员会选举中比例代表制公理（PJR、EJR、FJR）的计算复杂性。作者给出了常数选民/选民类型情况下的完整复杂度图景；证明了即使在效用被小常数上界的委员会选举中，满足FJR也是NP-hard的（首个针对正当代表公理的强NP-hard结果）；将扩展批准规则（EAR）扩展到可加效用委员会选举并证明其满足PJR；最后证明没有顺序投票规则能改进已知正结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多赢家投票在在线讨论和现实参与式预算中日益重要，满足比例代表公理的计算可行性成为关键问题。此前对于这些公理在可加效用设定下的计算复杂度认识尚不完整，特别是FJR的复杂性边界和现有规则能力的局限性需要澄清。

Method: 通过计算复杂度分析和公理化方法：(1) 系统性分析常数选民/选民类型下的复杂度；(2) 构造整数效用受小常数约束的归约证明FJR的NP-hard性；(3) 将扩大批准规则(EAR)推广用于可加效用的委员会选举；(4) 证明顺序投票规则的下界。

Result: 给出了常数选民/类型情形的完整复杂度图景；证明了FJR在小整数效用下的强NP-hard性（首个此类结果）；提出了EAR的可加效用扩展并证明其满足PJR；证明无顺序投票规则能超越现有积极结果，说明需要全新投票规则才能突破边界。

Conclusion: 比例代表公理的满足涉及深层计算困难，尤其是FJR本质上是难解的；现有顺序投票规则在理论上已达天花板，设计满足更强公理的新规则需要根本性的新颖方法。这些发现对实际参与式预算系统的算法设计有直接指导意义。

Abstract: We study the computational complexity of satisfying proportional representation -- in particular proportional, extended, and fully justified representation (PJR, EJR, and FJR) -- in participatory budgeting and committee elections with additive utilities. First, we give a complete picture of the complexity of the axioms for a constant number of voters or voter types. Second, we show that even for committee elections with integer utilities bounded above by a small constant, satisfying FJR is intractable, giving the first strong NP-hardness result for a justified representation axiom. Third, we extend the Expanding Approvals Rule to committee elections with additive utilities and show that it satisfies PJR. Lastly, we show that no sequential voting rule can improve on the known positive result, thus proving that novel, substantially different voting rules are needed to surpass these boundaries. Beyond their theoretical merit, our results carry practical importance, as multi-winner voting with additive utilities has recently been gaining prominence in online deliberation and real-world participatory budgeting.

</details>


### [342] [Fair Stable Matching: A Nash Social Welfare Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02354)
*Parth Desai,Rasheed M,Ganesh Ghalme,Sujit Gujar*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究稳定婚姻问题中，以纳什社会福利（NSW）作为公平性目标，提出SNSW-Alg算法，在Õ(n⁴)时间内找到最大化纳什社会福利的稳定匹配，在保持稳定性的同时实现公平性，并通过实验证明其在公平性上显著优于其他稳定性匹配，且不被其他公平性指标（遗憾值、平等主义、性别平等）的稳定匹配所帕累托支配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的稳定匹配算法（如Gale-Shapley算法）只优先保证稳定性，可能无法在参与者之间实现公平的结果。需要探索如何在维持稳定性的前提下引入公平性的考量。

Method: 将纳什社会福利（NSW）作为公平性目标引入经典稳定婚姻问题，设计SNSW-Alg算法，在基于秩的效用下寻找最大化纳什社会福利的稳定匹配，时间复杂度为Õ(n⁴)。

Result: 实验表明SNSW-Alg在多种偏好分布下能显著提升公平性，而在遗憾值、平等主义准则和性别平等等关键指标上损失较小。生成的稳定匹配在统计上不被其他公平性度量的稳定匹配帕累托支配。

Conclusion: 研究表明NSW是设计公平稳定匹配的有效公平性目标，SNSW-Alg能够平衡公平性与稳定性，为公平稳定匹配的设计提供了有说服力的见解。

Abstract: While traditional stable matching algorithms, such as the Gale-Shapley algorithm, prioritize stability, they may fall short of achieving equitable outcomes among participants. We study the role of \emph{Nash social welfare} (NSW) as a fairness objective in the classic \emph{stable marriage problem}. We develop \texttt{SNSW-Alg} that finds a stable matching that maximizes Nash social welfare under rank-induced utilities in $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(n^4)$ time, where $n$ is the number of men or women. We demonstrate that \texttt{SNSW-Alg} balances equity while preserving stability. We empirically evaluate our methods across diverse preference distributions, demonstrating significant gains in fairness without substantial losses in other key measures such as regret, egalitarian criterion, and sex equality. Our findings suggest that the stable matching produced by \texttt{SNSW-Alg} is statistically Pareto-undominated by stable matchings based on other fairness measures - regret, egalitarian, and sex equality. This study offers compelling insights for designing fair-stable matching.

</details>


### [343] [Almost Envy-Freeness for Additive Mixed Manna with Entitlements: Deterministic and Randomized Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02724)
*Zehan Lin,Shengxin Liu,Biaoshuai Tao,Shengwei Zhou*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了混合甘露（同时含商品和家务）场景下，代理具有不对称权利时不可分割物品的公平分配问题。针对可加性估值，证明了加权无嫉妒至多一项（WEF1）分配总是存在且可在多项式时间内计算；进一步证明了WEF1T与分数帕累托最优（fPO）相容，并确立了从WEF1放宽到WEF1T的紧性；最后通过有限彩票实现"两全其美"：期望分配满足WEF，所有实现分配均满足WEF1T。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平分配研究大多聚焦于对称权利或单一类型物品（纯商品或纯家务），而现实场景中代理往往具有不对称的权利（如投票权重、贡献度不同），且物品通常同时包含商品和家务。如何在不完美可分、混合类型的物品分配中平衡公平与效率，是一个尚未充分解决的理论问题。

Method: 本文采用理论分析的方法：针对可加性估值，构造并证明加权无嫉妒至多一项（WEF1）分配的存在性与多项式时间可计算性；将公平性概念从WEF1放宽到WEF1T以兼容分数帕累托最优（fPO），并通过不可能性结果论证此放宽是紧的；最后构造一个有限随机分配方案（有限彩票），使期望分配满足WEF且每个实现分配满足WEF1T。

Result: 1) 建立了任意混合甘露实例中WEF1分配的存在性及多项式时间计算算法；2) 证明WEF1T与fPO可同时满足于所有混合甘露实例，并指出WEF1到WEF1T的放宽是紧的（存在不可能性结果）；3) 构建了满足"最坏情况WEF1T + 期望WEF"的有限彩票，实现best-of-both-worlds结果。

Conclusion: 研究确立了带不对称权利的混合甘露公平分配问题的核心理论边界：WEF1可高效实现，但要同时保证效率（fPO）必须放宽至WEF1T且此放宽不可再紧缩，最终通过随机化机制在期望意义与实现意义上同时获得强公平保障。

Abstract: We investigate the fair allocation of indivisible items among agents with asymmetric entitlements in mixed manna settings, where the items consist of both goods and chores. For additive valuations, we establish that weighted envy-free up to one item (WEF1) allocations always exist and can be computed in polynomial time. We also study fair and efficient allocation and show that weighted envy-freeness up to one transfer (WEF1T) is compatible with fractional Pareto optimality (fPO) for every mixed-manna instance. This relaxation from WEF1 to WEF1T is tight, as demonstrated by our impossibility result. We further show a best-of-both-worlds result via a finite lottery that guarantees weighted envy-freeness (WEF) in expectation, with every realized allocation satisfying WEF1T and achieving the tight characterization complemented by the existing impossibility result.

</details>


### [344] [Approximately Efficient Multidimensional Bilateral Trade](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02872)
*Aviad Rubinstein,Xizhi Tan,Zixin Zhou*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究多维度双边贸易问题，其中市场双方均为多维代理人。针对一个XOS估值买方与一个加性成本卖方，以及扩展到n个XOS买方与一个加性卖方的情形，在物品价值与成本独立的假设下，提出了满足BIC、IIR和事前弱预算平衡的简单机制，并实现了对最优（第一最优）期望贸易收益的常数因子近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制设计中的核心挑战是设计可信的贸易机制以最大化双边市场的期望贸易收益（GFT）。现有成果大多局限于单维度代理人，多维度情形研究甚少。本文旨在填补多维度双边贸易（双方均多维）的空白，推广先前仅有的单维或单边多维结果。

Method: 研究两种多维度双边贸易情境：(1) 一个XOS估值买方与一个加性成本卖方；(2) n个XOS买方与一个加性卖方。在物品价值与成本独立的假设下，设计满足贝叶斯激励相容（BIC）、临时个体理性（IIR）和事前弱预算平衡（WBB）的简单机制，并分析其相对于第一最优期望GFT的近似比。

Result: 在两种设定下，所提出的简单机制均能实现BIC、IIR和事前WBB，同时达到最优（第一最优）期望贸易收益的一个常数因子近似。

Conclusion: 本文首次在双方均为多维代理人的双边贸易市场中取得了常数因子近似结果，显著优于此前多维度情形下仅有的对第二最优GFT的对数平方近似。这项工作将多维度贸易机制设计推进到了常数因子保证的新高度。

Abstract: A central challenge in mechanism design is to develop truthful trade mechanisms that maximize the expected gains-from-trade (GFT) in two-sided markets. Because achieving the full GFT is generally impossible, the literature has focused on constant-factor approximations---a notoriously difficult problem even in simple settings. It was only recently that a breakthrough result by [DMSW22] achieved a constant-factor approximation for single-item bilateral trade. The same guarantee was later extended to single-dimensional matching markets with general downward-closed constraints [BRTW26].
  Most existing results, however, are limited to single-dimensional agents. A notable multi-dimensional exception is [CGMZ21]. They considered a market with one constrained-additive buyer and $n$ single-dimensional sellers and provided a mechanism that achieves a $\log^2(n)$ approximation to the second-best GFT, i.e., the maximum expected GFT theoretically achievable by any mechanism satisfying Bayesian Incentive Compatibility (BIC), Interim Individual Rationality (IIR), and ex-ante Weak Budget Balance (WBB).
  In this paper, we study multi-dimensional bilateral trade problem where both sides of the market are multi-dimensional. We start with one buyer with XOS valuation and one seller with an additive cost function. We then generalize to a market with $n$ XOS buyers and one additive seller. Assuming independent items' values and costs, in both settings we propose simple mechanisms that are BIC, IIR, and ex-ante WBB, while achieving a constant fraction of the optimal (first-best) expected GFT.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [345] [Making Revisions Understandable: A Survey of Edit Intentions, Methods, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01610)
*Fangping Lan,Qi Zhang,Eduard Dragut*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文是第一篇以"编辑意图"（edit intentions）为视角对文本修订（text revision）研究进行系统综述的论文，统一梳理了数据集、分类体系、识别方法和应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本修订是文档创作的核心过程，反映了作者如何迭代改进内容。随着维基百科和arXiv等平台大规模修订数据的可用性增加，NLP研究正从"改了什么"转向"为什么改"（即编辑意图），但缺乏一个统一的综述视角来整合该领域的研究。

Method: 该综述从编辑意图视角出发，覆盖完整的修订工作流程，包括：（1）修订语料库构建；（2）编辑意图分类体系设计；（3）编辑意图识别方法。此外还对代表性数据集和方法进行分类归纳。

Result: 系统归类了代表性数据集与识别方法，总结了写作辅助、文档编辑摘要等下游应用，并指出了关键开放研究方向。

Conclusion: 本文提供了首个以编辑意图为核心的文本修订研究统一视角，填补了该领域系统综述的空白，为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Text revision is a core process in document creation, capturing how authors iteratively refine, reorganize, and improve written content. With the increasing availability of large-scale revision histories from platforms such as Wikipedia and arXiv, NLP research has begun to move beyond modeling what changes are made to understanding why they are made, i.e., the underlying edit intentions. To our knowledge, this is the first survey that synthesizes text revision research through the lens of edit intentions, providing a unified view of datasets, taxonomies, identification methods, and applications. We review prior work across the full revision workflow, including revision corpus construction, edit intention taxonomy design, and edit intention identification. We further categorize representative datasets and methods, summarize downstream applications such as writing assistance and document edit summarization, and highlight key open research directions.

</details>


### [346] [MESSY STREETS: A Benchmark for Geocoding Real-World Addresses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01612)
*Edward Gaere,Florian von Wangenheim*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MESSY STREETS基准测试集，用于评估地理编码器在杂乱网络地址上的表现。该基准测试包含表面形式偏离规范、组件缺失或畸形的地址。实验发现商业地理编码器在召回率上比开源系统高出多达49个百分点，主要差距源于对非规范地址的候选返回率，而定位精度本身差异不大。非规范表面形式本身可导致高达25个百分点的召回率损失。作者还分析了Nominatim的查询处理流程，指出其连接匹配机制会使单个未识别词导致整个查询失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地理编码基准测试通常基于干净或合成扰动的地址，无法反映真实网络环境中地址的杂乱性（如缺失、重复、畸形组件）。作者旨在创建一个包含真实嘈杂地址的基准，以公平评估各类地理编码系统在实际应用中的性能差距。

Method: 从2024年12月的Web Data Commons语料库构建MESSY STREETS基准，参考位置来自OpenAddresses或OpenStreetMap。该基准包含表面形式偏离规范表示的地址，并能控制测量表面形式差异的影响。此外，作者还深入分析了Nominatim的查询处理流程以解释性能差距背后的原因。

Result: 最强的商业地理编码器在召回率上较开源系统领先多达49个百分点。该差距主要由候选返回率差异驱动，候选返回后位置精度大体相当。非规范表面形式单独可造成25个百分点的召回率损失。Nominatim的连接匹配机制导致单个未识别词可使整个有效查询归零。

Conclusion: 地理编码器的选择对处理嘈杂地址数据的应用是关键的设计决策。通过规范化和预处理可以在很大程度上缩小开源与商业地理编码器之间的性能差距。

Abstract: We introduce MESSY STREETS, a benchmark for evaluating geocoders on verbatim web addresses, with existence verification and controlled measurement of surface-form divergence. Unlike conventional benchmarks based on clean or synthetically perturbed addresses, MESSY STREETS contains addresses whose surface forms diverge from canonical representations and whose components may be missing, repeated, malformed, or incomplete. The benchmark is constructed from the December 2024 Web Data Commons corpus, with reference locations established from OpenAddresses or OpenStreetMap.
  The strongest commercial geocoders outperform open-source systems by up to 49 percentage points in recall. This gap is driven primarily by differences in candidate return rates on non-canonical addresses; once a candidate is returned, positional accuracy is broadly comparable across systems. Non-canonical surface form alone accounts for up to 25 percentage points of recall loss. Examining Nominatim's query-processing pipeline, we show that its conjunctive matching lets a single unrecognised token zero an otherwise valid query.
  The results demonstrate that geocoder choice is a consequential design decision for applications processing noisy address data, and that normalisation and preprocessing could substantially narrow the gap between open-source and commercial geocoders.

</details>


### [347] [Skim and Skip: Hierarchical Adaptive Inference for Efficient Multimodal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01613)
*Meng Gao,Yizhen Zhang,Yang Ding,Ziqi Dai,Shuoshuo Zhang,Junjie Wang,Taiqiang Wu,Chufan Shi,Lei Ji,Jian Jiao,Linfeng Zhang,Yeyun Gong,Yujiu Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出SAS（Skim and Skip）分层自适应推理框架，通过token级证据选择和深度自适应推理，在不损失检索性能的前提下显著降低多模态检索的推理成本，在12个MMEB任务上保留约99%性能的同时实现最高1.64倍加速和66.3%的FLOPs削减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）作为统一嵌入骨干在通用多模态检索中表现出色，但稠密推理成本高昂。现有方法对所有输入token进行全模型处理并使用最终层[EOS]表示匹配，忽略了多模态检索中的两类异构性：token对最终检索嵌入的贡献极不均匀，以及不同查询所需的推理深度差异显著。

Method: 提出SAS（Skim and Skip）分层自适应推理框架，包含两个阶段：(1) token级证据选择，仅保留对最终检索嵌入最相关的输入信息；(2) 深度自适应推理，判断当前表示是否已足以进行可靠匹配。

Result: 在12个MMEB检索任务上的实验表明，SAS保留了稠密基线约99%的平均检索性能，同时实现最高1.64倍端到端加速和66.3%的FLOPs减少。

Conclusion: 通过利用多模态检索中token贡献不均和查询深度需求差异的特性，SAS证明了在保持高检索质量的同时大幅降低推理开销的可行性，为高效多模态检索提供了新的自适应推理范式。

Abstract: Universal multimodal retrieval (UMR) increasingly adopts multimodal large language models (MLLMs) as unified embedding backbones, but their strong retrieval performance comes at substantial inference cost. Existing methods typically rely on uniformly dense inference, where all input tokens are processed through the entire model and matched using the final-layer [EOS] representation. However, this paradigm overlooks two key forms of heterogeneity in multimodal retrieval: token contributions to the final retrieval embedding are highly uneven, and different queries require markedly different amounts of inference depth. To address this, we propose Skim and Skip (SAS), a hierarchical adaptive inference framework for efficient multimodal retrieval. SAS first performs token-level evidence selection to preserve only the input information most relevant to the final retrieval embedding, and then performs depth-adaptive inference to determine whether the current representation is already sufficient for reliable matching. Experiments on 12 MMEB retrieval tasks show that SAS retains about 99% of the dense baseline's average retrieval performance while achieving up to 1.64 times end-to-end speedup and up to 66.3% FLOPs reduction.

</details>


### [348] [Incident Memory: Training-Free Operational Memory through Sequential Pattern Mining and Velocity-Stratified Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01616)
*Adarsh Agrawal,Rahul Suresh Babu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Incident Memory系统，一个无需模型训练的确定性系统，用于积累和检索运维事件响应知识。它通过速度分层检索、指纹条件PrefixSpan挖掘和溯源感知的指标定义，从历史事件中提取有序行动手册，在UCI ITSM数据集上覆盖84.3%的保留事件，并取得了99.2%的有序行动手册精确率，优于LLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件响应本质上是一个记忆问题：团队虽积累了海量tickets、traces、postmortems和wiki页面，但下一次事件所需的知识很少以完整顺序、新鲜度和溯源性被保存。现有方法依赖语言模型的开放式生成，忽视了事件历史在特定指纹和先前动作条件下其实是低熵的这一事实。

Method: Incident Memory由三个组件构成：(i) 速度分层检索——对不同类别的知识（结构性、行为性、上下文性、短暂性）采用不同老化速率；(ii) 指纹条件PrefixSpan挖掘——从成功的事故调查中提取有序行动手册（playbooks）；(iii) 溯源感知的指标定义——通过可执行检查检测冲突的指标定义。

Result: 在UCI ITSM事件日志（141,712个事件，24,918个事故）上，提取了23,110条有序轨迹，挖掘出39个行动手册，覆盖84.3%的6,934个保留事故。在受控基准上，有序行动手册精确率达99.2%，架构性陈旧保证（对比平面基线36%的陈旧返回），冲突检测F1为0.876。Claude Haiku基线在19个指纹组上达到0.661有序精确率，而PrefixSpan达到0.985。

Conclusion: 核心结论不是语言模型在事件响应上表现弱，而是：当给定指纹和先前动作条件时，重复的事件历史是低熵的。在这种低熵模式下，精确记忆（exact memory）是比开放式生成（open-ended generation）更强的原始能力。

Abstract: Incident response is a memory problem: teams accumulate tickets, traces, postmortems, and wiki pages, but the knowledge needed for the next incident is rarely stored with its order, freshness, and provenance intact. We present Incident Memory, a deterministic system that accumulates operational knowledge without model training. It combines (i) velocity-stratified retrieval, which ages structural, behavioral, contextual, and ephemeral facts at different rates; (ii) fingerprint-conditioned PrefixSpan mining, which extracts ordered playbooks from successful investigations; and (iii) provenance-aware metric definitions, which detect conflicting definitions through executable checks. On the UCI ITSM event log, containing 141,712 events across 24,918 incidents, Incident Memory extracts 23,110 ordered traces, mines 39 playbooks, and covers 84.3% of 6,934 held-out incidents. On controlled benchmarks with known ground truth, it achieves 99.2% ordered playbook precision (controlled), an architectural staleness guarantee versus 36% stale returns for a flat baseline, and conflict-detection F1 of 0.876. A direct Claude Haiku baseline on 19 fingerprint groups reaches 0.661 ordered precision, compared with 0.985 for PrefixSpan. The central result is not that language models are weak at incident response; it is that repeated incident histories are low-entropy once conditioned on fingerprint and previous action. In that regime, exact memory is a stronger primitive than open-ended generation.

</details>


### [349] [Hybrid Retrieval-Augmented Generation with Knowledge Graph Expansion, RRF Fusion, and Per-Chunk Grounded Evaluation for Enterprise Document Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01617)
*Harish Saragadam,Sudhanshu Sharma,Meghana Pujari*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: DocuSearch是一个离线、多智能体文档智能系统，面向电信网络运维环境，结合语义搜索(Qdrant+BGE-Large)、BM25全文搜索(SQLite FTS5)和知识图谱邻居扩展三种检索信号，通过加权倒数排名融合(RRF)合并，并采用交叉编码器重排序与MMR剪枝。其核心创新在于按块评估循环：LLM逐块判断上下文需求、回答完整性及文本落地性，确保未落地的答案不被返回。在电信语料上达到Precision@10为0.69、Recall@10为0.79、落地率89.6%，较纯密集RAG基线分别提升15、16和18.4个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业大型文档库的检索问答难题：纯稠密向量检索在处理混合技术术语、供应商专有缩写或跨多个非相邻章节推理的查询时表现不佳，需要一个更可靠的混合检索与验证框架。

Method: 采用三源混合检索（向量搜索权重0.50、BM25权重0.35、知识图谱权重0.15），经RRF融合（平滑常数60）、交叉编码器重排序、MMR(平衡因子0.65)去冗余；核心为按块智能体评估循环，LLM判定块是否需要上下文、是否完整回答查询、答案是否被检索文本所支撑，未落地答案触发多块合并回退机制。

Result: 在电信运维语料上，DocuSearch取得Precision@10=0.69、Recall@10=0.79、grounding rate=89.6%，较仅稠密检索的RAG基线分别提升15、16和18.4个百分点。

Conclusion: DocuSearch证明了多源证据融合与智能体化按块评估相结合能够显著提升企业文档问答的准确性与落地性，是一个可在本地部署的实用解决方案。

Abstract: Getting accurate, grounded answers out of large enterprise document repositories is a difficult problem. Dense vector retrieval alone frequently performs poorly on queries that mix technical terminology, vendor-specific acronyms, or require reasoning across several non-adjacent sections. DocuSearch was built to address exactly this gap - an offline, multi-agent document intelligence system developed and evaluated in a production telecom network operations environment. Rather than relying on a single retrieval signal, DocuSearch pulls together three complementary sources of evidence: semantic search over a Qdrant vector store using BGE-Large embeddings, BM25 full text search over an SQLite FTS5 index, and Knowledge Graph neighbour expansion from a structured edge table. These three ranked lists are merged through Reciprocal Rank Fusion with signal weights of 0.50 for vector search, 0.35 for BM25, and 0.15 for the knowledge graph, using a smoothing constant of 60 to stabilize scores. A cross-encoder then reranks the fused list, and Maximal Marginal Relevance with a balance factor of 0.65 prunes results for relevance and diversity. What makes DocuSearch distinctive is a per-chunk evaluation loop treating each chunk as its own mini-retrieval problem: an LLM decides whether the chunk needs more context, whether it fully answers the query, and whether the answer is grounded in retrieved text. Ungrounded answers are not returned; the system falls back to a multi-chunk merge instead. On a telecom corpus, DocuSearch reaches Precision@10 of 0.69, Recall@10 of 0.79, and a grounding rate of 89.6% - gains of 15, 16, and 18.4 percentage points over a dense-only RAG baseline. Index Terms: retrieval-augmented generation, knowledge graph, reciprocal rank fusion, enterprise document search, agentic evaluation, BM25, cross-encoder reranking, on-premise deployment, LangGraph, telecom AI.

</details>


### [350] [Multi-Agent Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Efficient Cloud Knowledge Base Search in Telecom SNOC Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01618)
*Harish Saragadam,Sudhanshu Sharma,Ipsha Routray*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了Athena for Cloud Knowledge Base，一个完全离线的多智能体RAG框架，用于Vodafone Idea的SNOC环境中的企业云文档搜索。该系统集成密集检索（E5 Large V2）、BM25稀疏检索和知识图谱扩展，通过加权CombSUM融合、交叉编码器重排序和MMR选择证据，并对每个选中的chunk进行归因验证。在4200份文档、312000个chunk的语料上，MRR@10达到0.910，EM得分78.4%，比单阶段密集检索高出14.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电信SNOC工程师在关键事件中需要快速准确检索云文档信息，但传统关键词检索和单阶段检索方法难以提供精确结果，无法满足紧急运维场景的需求。

Method: 构建完全离线且多智能体的RAG框架，包括：1）多路检索（E5 Dense + BM25 + 知识图谱扩展）；2）加权CombSUM融合与交叉编码器重排序及MMR去重；3）每chunk的LLM归因验证，不支持的证据被丢弃；4）若无单个chunk通过验证则自动进行多chunk联合评估作为回退机制。

Result: 在4200份SNOC云文档（312000个索引chunk）的实验上，MRR@10为0.910，EM得分为78.4%，较单阶段密集检索提升14.6个百分点，且整个流程完全离线运行。

Conclusion: Athena通过多智能体编排与严格归因验证，在满足企业数据主权（完全离线）要求的同时，实现了高准确率和可接地性的企业云文档检索与问答能力。

Abstract: Telecom Service and Network Operations Centers (SNOCs) rely on large collections of cloud documents, including Standard Operating Procedures (SOPs), vendor technical manuals, incident reports, and configuration guides, to maintain uninterrupted network operations. During critical incidents, engineers must quickly retrieve accurate information, yet traditional keyword based and single stage retrieval approaches often struggle to provide precise results.
  This paper presents Athena for Cloud Knowledge Base, a fully offline, multi agent Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) framework designed for enterprise cloud document search in Vodafone Idea's SNOC environment. The system integrates dense retrieval using E5 Large V2 embeddings, BM25 sparse retrieval, and Knowledge Graph expansion within a LangGraph based orchestration framework. Retrieved candidates are fused using Weighted CombSUM, followed by cross encoder reranking and Maximal Marginal Relevance (MMR) to obtain a diverse and relevant evidence set.
  To improve answer reliability, the framework performs per chunk LLM evaluation with explicit attribution verification, assessing each MMR selected chunk independently before generating a response. Unsupported or weak evidence is discarded, and if no chunk satisfies the verification criteria, the system automatically evaluates multiple chunks together as a fallback. Experiments on a corpus of 4200 SNOC cloud documents containing 312000 indexed chunks show that the proposed approach achieves an MRR at 10 of 0.910 and an Exact Match (EM) score of 78.4 percent, outperforming single stage dense retrieval by 14.6 percentage points. The entire pipeline operates in a fully offline environment, satisfying enterprise data sovereignty requirements while delivering accurate and grounded responses for cloud document search.

</details>


### [351] [MGDiff: Multi-Interest Sequence Recommendation with Masking GNN-Guided Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01619)
*Wenjing Xiao,Hao Ding*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于掩码图神经网络引导的扩散模型多兴趣序列推荐框架(MGDiff)，通过双层级语义引导和流行度感知引导生成无偏且准确的用户兴趣信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在推荐系统中用于生成用户兴趣信息时面临精度不足和偏好偏差(如流行度偏差)的问题，需要设计更准确的引导机制来提升生成质量。

Method: 1) 提出语义增强的双层级语义引导(DSG)框架，包括提取潜在物品语义和去耦多维用户意图两个协同阶段；使用权重自适应掩码图神经网络重建缺失链接以挖掘深层物品关系，动态多专家网络将用户偏好投影到不同语义子空间以抑制无关干扰。2) 提出流行度感知引导(PAG)机制，以物品流行度作为可微调整信号重新校准相似度度量，对扩散模型输出进行空间几何调整以产生无流行度偏差的多样化推荐。

Result: 在四个广泛使用的数据集上与多个基线模型对比，MGDiff展现出优越的性能，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: MGDiff通过层级化语义引导提升了扩散模型的生成准确度，并通过流行度感知引导消除了推荐偏差，在多种数据集上表现优于基线模型。

Abstract: We propose a novel Multi-Interest Sequence Recommendation Framework with \underline{M}asking \underline{G}NN-Guided \underline{Diff}usion Model (MGDiff), designed to generate accurate, bias-free user interest information during the diffusion process. First, we propose a semantics-enhanced Dual-layer Semantic Guidance (DSG) framework, which decomposes guidance into two synergistic stages: extracting latent item semantics and decoupling multidimensional user intent. We design a Weight-adaptive Masking Graph Neural Network reconstructs missing links to uncover deep item relationships beyond superficial co-occurrence, while a Dynamic Multi-Expert Network projects user preferences into distinct semantic subspaces to suppress irrelevant interference. This hierarchical design yields structured guidance that significantly improves the generation accuracy of diffusion models. Second, We propose a Popularity-Aware Guidance (PAG) mechanism that performs spatial geometric adjustments on the outputs of diffusion models: by using item popularity as a differentiable adjustment signal to recalibrate similarity metrics, we enable DMs to generate diverse recommendations free from popularity bias. Finally, we compare MGDiff with multiple baseline models across four widely used datasets, demonstrating its superior performance and validating its effectiveness.

</details>


### [352] [When Literature Data Mislead Artificial Intelligence in Materials Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01621)
*Qian Wang,Ying Li,Ryuhei Sato,Hidemi Kato,Shin-ichi Orimo,Hao Li,Eric Jianfeng Cheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文以固态电解质电导率数据为代表性案例，系统分析了从文献来源到数据集构建过程中存在的文本与图表不一致、轴标注模糊、单位混乱和测量背景缺失等问题。这些误差在数值上具有迷惑性，难以通过常规预处理发现，但会作为结构化标签噪声在数据库构建和机器学习复用中传播，甚至可造成100倍的电导率误差。研究将数据准确性重新定义为AI驱动科学发现的基础设施需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能日益将科学文献作为构建数据库、训练预测模型和指导发现的直接数据来源，但现有文献衍生数据集往往默认假设报道的实验值在内部一致且可直接复用。这一假设缺乏系统性检验，可能导致错误数据在AI驱动的科学发现流程中被广泛传播。

Method: 以固态电解质（SE）电导率数据作为材料科学的代表性案例，通过从源文章追踪数值到策展数据集的过程，识别文本-图表不匹配、模糊的轴标注、单位不一致和缺失测量背景等重复出现的错误类型，并辅以跨数据库示例展示模糊报告如何造成数量级误差。

Result: 识别出多种重复出现的文本-图表不匹配、模糊轴标注、单位不一致和缺失测量背景等数据质量问题。这些差异在数值上往往具有合理性，因此难以通过常规预处理步骤检测，却会在数据库构建和机器学习复用中作为结构化标签噪声传播。跨数据库示例显示模糊报告可造成100倍的电导率误差。

Conclusion: 数据准确性应被重新定义为AI驱动发现所需的基础设施要求而非可选优化项。该研究呼吁建立可追溯的报告、策展和验证实践，以确保文献衍生科学数据具备可复用的可靠性，从而支撑AI在科学发现中的有效应用。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) increasingly treats scientific literature as a data source for building databases, training predictive models, and guiding discovery. Yet literature-derived datasets often assume that reported experimental values are internally consistent and directly reusable. Here, we analyze this assumption using solid electrolyte (SE) conductivity data as a representative materials-science case. By tracing values from source articles to curated datasets, we identify recurrent text-figure mismatches, ambiguous axis annotations, unit inconsistencies, and missing measurement context. These discrepancies are often numerically plausible and therefore difficult to detect through routine preprocessing, but they can propagate as structured label noise during database construction and machine-learning reuse. A cross-database example shows how ambiguous reporting can create a 100-fold conductivity error. Our analysis reframes data accuracy as an infrastructure requirement for artificial-intelligence-driven discovery and motivates traceable reporting, curation, and validation practices for reusable scientific data.
  Keywords: AI for science; Data reliability; Scientific databases; Structured label noise; Literature-derived data; Materials informatics; Solid electrolytes

</details>


### [353] [RecEvolve: A Knowledge-Driven Autonomous Agent System for Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01622)
*Weidi Pan,He Ma,Shuhao Ye,Palaksh Rungta,David McPeek,Junyi Jiao,Arnab Bhadury,Mingyan Gao,Onkar Dalal*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文实证验证了一个知识驱动的自主Agent系统，直接部署在生产级大规模双塔召回模型上。该系统完成了40多次从零开始的自主训练运行，实现了NDCG约20%的相对提升，并在真实流量中带来+3.77%的用户满意度提升。同时，系统自主发现了奖励黑客（reward hacking）等评估协议漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI的兴起推动了自迭代系统的发展，为生产推荐模型的自主优化开辟了新前沿。作者希望验证一个自主Agent系统能否直接部署在生产环境中，承担从想法生成到指标评估的完整研究生命周期，从而加速机器学习研究并检验实验基础设施的严谨性。

Method: 构建了一个知识驱动的自主Agent系统，形成连续闭环框架，将完整研究生命周期（想法生成、代码实现、离线训练、指标评估）全部委托给该Agent。系统部署在生产级大规模Two-Tower检索模型上，执行40多次从零开始的生产级自主训练运行，并系统性地探索最新生产模型的隐藏架构瓶颈。

Result: 该系统实现了约20%的NDCG相对提升，直接带来了生产真实流量中+3.77%的用户满意度增长。同时，部署过程暴露了标准评估协议的关键漏洞——Agent系统自主发现了奖励黑客的捷径。这也揭示了奖励黑客和失败假设的冗余探索等新挑战。

Conclusion: 自主流水线能够极大地加速机器学习研究的步伐，并对底层实验基础设施的严谨性进行压力测试。然而，该研究也暴露了新挑战（如奖励黑客和冗余探索），表明在推进自主优化的同时需要更健全的评估机制。

Abstract: The rise of agentic AI has catalyzed a shift toward self-iterating systems, opening new frontiers for the autonomous optimization of production recommender models. This paper presents the empirical validation of a knowledge-driven autonomous agent system, deployed directly on a production large-scale Two-Tower retrieval model. By delegating the entire research lifecycle, spanning idea generation, code implementation, offline training, and metric evaluation, to a continuous closed-loop autonomous framework, the agent system executed over 40 completed autonomous training runs from scratch. Executing these runs under rigorous production-scale evaluations, the system systematically navigated hidden architectural bottlenecks on the latest production model to achieve a breakthrough ~20% relative improvement in NDCG, a gain that translated directly to a +3.77% increase in user satisfaction in live production traffic. Furthermore, the deployment exposed critical vulnerabilities in standard evaluation protocols, as the agent system autonomously discovered reward-hacking shortcuts. These findings prove that an autonomous pipeline can dramatically accelerate the pace of machine learning research and stress-test the rigorousness of underlying experimental infrastructure, while also exposing novel challenges such as reward hacking and redundant exploration of failed hypotheses.

</details>


### [354] [The Utility of LLMs in Recommender Systems Explanation Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01627)
*Kathrin Wardatzky,Oana Inel,Luca Rossetto,Abraham Bernstein*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用大型语言模型（LLMs）作为"评审"来选择推荐系统中有效解释方法的可行性。研究生成了18种不同的解释原型，由14个不同规模的LLM在两种温度设置下进行评分，并与人类评分进行对比。结果表明LLM表现出类似人类的评分模式，与人类评分者达到中等秩相关性，但绝对评分一致性较低且因模型规模和评估维度而异。作者提出了四项实用建议：保持解释生成提示简洁、优先使用更大模型进行评估、预测试评估维度、审查解释的事实准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统解释方法众多，但缺乏选择最佳方法的指导。用户评估不可行，自动化评估指标要么只评估抽象输出，要么需要通常不可用的ground truth。虽然有研究表明LLM可作为解释评估的"评审"，但其可靠性尚未被深入探索。

Method: 研究生成18种不同的解释原型（输入关于RS和用户的不同信息），由14个不同规模的LLM在两种温度设置下进行评估，并将LLM评分与来自用户研究的人类评分进行比较，分析相关性、一致性和按模型大小及评估维度的差异。

Result: LLM展现类似人类的评分模式，与人类评分者达到中等秩相关性，但绝对评分一致性低，且差异随模型大小和评估维度而变化。此外，人类和LLM都无法可靠检测非事实性内容。

Conclusion: 提出四项实践建议：保持解释生成提示简洁，优先使用更大模型进行评估，预测试评估维度，以及审计解释的事实准确性。

Abstract: Explanations play a crucial role in creating trustworthy recommender systems (RS), yet choosing a good explanation method presents challenges. Many explanation methods exist, but little guidance exists on which is best for which setting. Existing explanation generation methods often produce abstract outputs that require further formatting to become user-friendly, with a seemingly endless pool of options. Running user-based evaluations of all possible options is usually unfeasible, while automated evaluation metrics often either assess only the explainer's abstract output or require comparison with a ground truth, which is generally unavailable. Recent studies have shown that large language models (LLMs) can serve as ``judges'' for explanation evaluation, but their reliability has not yet been thoroughly explored. This paper studies the utility of LLMs in selecting an effective explanation method for a given application. We first explore their ability to generate explanation prototypes given varying information about the RS and the user. Specifically, we generate 18 distinct explanation prototypes, which are subsequently evaluated by 14 LLMs of varying sizes across two temperature settings. We compare these against human ratings derived from a user study. Our results show that while LLMs exhibit human-like rating patterns and achieve moderate rank correlation with human raters, their absolute rating agreement is low and varies substantially by model size and evaluation construct. We derive four practical recommendations: keep explanation-generation prompts concise, prefer larger models for evaluation, pre-test evaluation constructs, and audit explanations for factual accuracy, as neither humans nor LLMs reliably detect non-factual content.

</details>


### [355] [Marginal Expected Revenue for Jointly Ranking Auction and Fixed-Price Listings in E-Commerce Sponsored Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01628)
*Greg Kocher,Sanjana Arun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对电商搜索排序中混合listing格式（固定价格、纯拍卖、带"立即购买"的混合拍卖ABIN）的收入估计问题，将标准eCPM框架扩展为边际eCPM（meCPM），以捕捉价格动态演化中展示一次额外印象的增量价值，从而统一排序目标。该方案已在大型电商平台上线，A/B测试显示收入增长且用户体验指标显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商搜索排序需在相关性与平台收入间平衡，固定价格商品的预期收入估计已成熟，但当库存包含纯拍卖和混合ABIN（价格动态演化、最终成交价不可知）listing时，收入估计变得困难。而这类listing在eBay等平台占相当比例的库存和交易量，是个体卖家及价值模糊独特商品的热门格式，因此亟需统一的收入估计框架。

Method: 将标准eCPM框架扩展到拍卖和ABIN listing，推导出边际eCPM（meCPM）——衡量价格仍在演化中商品的额外一次展示所带来的增量价值；该公式使固定价格eCPM（本质上已是边际的）自然推广至拍卖动态，从而在单一目标下统一排序固定价格、拍卖与ABIN listing；并给出生产级实现方案，通过从已有engagement模型引导来应对冷启动挑战。

Result: 在大型电商平台进行的在线A/B测试中，系统带来正向的收入增益，并对用户体验指标产生统计显著的改善；该系统已成功部署至生产环境。

Conclusion: 本文提出的meCPM框架使拍卖与ABIN listing能以统一目标与固定价格商品共同参与搜索排序，兼顾理论严谨性与实际可部署性，为混合格式电商平台提供了可落地的排序优化方案。

Abstract: E-commerce search ranking must balance multiple objectives--relevance, user engagement, and platform revenue--when allocating impression slots to competing listings. Estimating the expected revenue component is well understood for fixed-price items, but becomes challenging when marketplace inventory includes mixed listing formats such as pure auctions and hybrid "Auction with Buy It Now" (ABIN) items, where prices evolve dynamically and the final transaction value is unknown at ranking time. Yet auction and ABIN listings account for a meaningful share of inventory and transaction volume on platforms such as eBay, and are a popular format for individual sellers and for unique items with unclear value. We extend the standard Expected Cost-per-Mille (eCPM) framework to auction and ABIN listings by deriving a marginal eCPM (meCPM) that captures the incremental value of showing one more impression of an item whose price is still evolving. The resulting formulation extends the familiar fixed-price eCPM--which is already inherently marginal--to auction dynamics, allowing unified ranking of fixed-price, auction, and ABIN listings under a single objective. We then describe a practical production implementation that approximates this objective, addressing cold-start challenges by bootstrapping from existing engagement models. Online A/B tests at a large e-commerce platform showed positive revenue gains and statistically significant improvements to user metrics, and the system was deployed to production.

</details>


### [356] [GRAND-HC: Graph-Refined Author Name Disambiguation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01636)
*Yuanhao Sun,Zhouyang Jin,Yi Xu,Luoyi Fu,Jiaxin Ding,Xiaoying Gan,Xinbing Wang,Chenghu Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: GRAND-HC是一个完整的端到端从零开始姓名消歧(SND)框架，构建异构论文图并通过图注意力网络学习嵌入，采用Harmony对比学习(HCL)和Graph-Refined距离矩阵(GRDM)解决长尾作者被过度合并的问题，并通过轻量级论文压缩模块(PCM)实现准确的聚类数估计，最终使用层次凝聚聚类输出结果，在宏F1上达到最先进水平并已部署于十亿级学术数据库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SND方法面临两个关键限制：(1)作者分布固有的长尾特性导致表示学习产生偏差，造成尾作者过度合并；(2)现有聚类数估计方法对长论文序列不可靠，阻碍大规模部署应用。

Method: 构建基于合著者、合作组织和合著期刊关系的异构论文图，以图注意力网络作为嵌入骨干；提出Harmony对比学习(HCL)通过动态重加权训练损失抑制对高频作者的过拟合；提出Graph-Refined距离矩阵(GRDM)利用图拓扑优化成对距离防止尾作者过度合并；设计轻量级论文压缩模块(PCM)实现跨规模的准确聚类数估计；最后通过层次凝聚聚类输出最终簇结果。

Result: 大量实验表明在宏F1指标上达到最先进水平(SOTA)，并且该方法已成功部署在十亿级规模的学术数据库中。

Conclusion: GRAND-HC通过端到端的完整框架有效解决了SND中长尾作者过度合并和聚类数估计不可靠两大问题，验证了在大规模真实学术数据库中的实用性。

Abstract: From-Scratch Name Disambiguation (SND) groups papers sharing an ambiguous name into clusters of distinct real-world authors. Existing methods suffer from two critical limitations: (1) inherent long-tailed author distribution biases representation learning, causing over-merging of tail authors; (2) existing cluster number estimation methods are unreliable for long paper sequences, hindering large-scale deployment. We propose \textbf{GRAND-HC}, a complete end-to-end SND framework. We construct a heterogeneous paper graph via co-author, co-organization, and co-venue relations, using a graph attention network as the embedding backbone. \textbf{Harmony Contrastive Learning (HCL)} dynamically reweights training loss to suppress overfitting to prolific authors, learning discriminative embeddings. A \textbf{Graph-Refined Distance Matrix (GRDM)} leverages graph topology to optimize pairwise distances, further preventing tail author over-merging. Meanwhile, a lightweight \textbf{Paper Compression Module (PCM)} achieves accurate cluster number estimation across varying scales. Finally, Hierarchical Agglomerative Clustering outputs the final clusters. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art macro F1 performance. GRAND-HC has been deployed in a billion-scale academic database. Source code: https://github.com/baokou-fw2/GRAND-HC.

</details>


### [357] [Imagine Before Retrieval: Prospective Skill Retrieval for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01642)
*Shuo Liu,Yutong Yang,Haohao Xiao,Mouxing Yang,Xi Peng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillDreamer框架，解决技能检索中任务查询与技能描述之间的"Query-Skill Misalignment (QSM)"问题——即任务查询基于目标导向而技能基于过程导向，导致语义检索难以匹配。SkillDreamer通过模拟人类前瞻认知，先推断任务所需能力，再生成伪技能作为桥梁，弥合两者差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能检索方法多侧重于定制检索模型或重构检索流程，优先匹配与任务查询语义最相关的技能。但作者通过实证发现，任务查询和目标技能天然从不同视角表述（目标导向vs过程导向），即QSM问题，这使得仅依赖语义相关性的检索方法难以有效关联到正确的技能，阻碍智能体正确执行任务。

Method: SkillDreamer框架包含三个步骤：(1) 推断任务执行所需的能力；(2) 通过生成伪技能来模拟如何实现这些能力（想象过程）；(3) 利用这类前瞻性信息弥合目标导向任务查询与过程导向技能之间的鸿沟，实现更好的技能检索与匹配。

Result: 在SkillRet和SkillUsage基准上的大量实验验证了SkillDreamer在技能检索和端到端任务执行中的有效性，同时展示了其在多种检索模型和检索流程上的泛化能力。

Conclusion: SkillDreamer通过引入前瞻性认知（prospective cognition）机制有效缓解了QSM问题，为LLM智能体的技能检索提供了一种新的、可泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: Skill retrieval has recently emerged as a promising paradigm for identifying the desirable execution guidelines from the skill gallery, thus equipping large language model (LLM) agents with the procedural knowledge to accomplish the specified task. To this end, most existing methods customize the retrieval model or reconfigure the retrieval pipeline to prioritize skills that are most semantically relevant to the task query. However, we empirically reveal that task queries and skills are naturally formulated from different perspectives, namely, objective-oriented and procedural-oriented, leading to an under-explored problem termed Query--Skill Misalignment (QSM). Clearly, it is daunting and even impossible to associate the desirable skills in the context of QSM, thus hindering the agent from correctly executing the task. As a remedy, inspired by human prospective cognition, we propose SkillDreamer, a novel framework to alleviate the negative impact of QSM problem. In brief, SkillDreamer first infers the capabilities necessary for task execution, then imagines how to realize these capabilities by generating pseudo skills, and finally leverages such prospective information to bridge the gap between objective-oriented task queries and execution-oriented skills. Extensive experiments on SkillRet and SkillUsage not only verify the effectiveness of SkillDreamer in both skill retrieval and end-to-end task execution, but also demonstrate its generalizability across diverse retrieval models and pipelines. The code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [358] [The Vocabulary Gap Is an Equity Gap: Register Mismatch in Retrieval Systems for Public-Benefits Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01645)
*Krish Sapru*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个高风险的检索系统公平性失败模式：在公共福利资格问答中，用户采用日常口语提问（plain register）与文档的政府公文语体（agency register）之间存在显著词汇鸿沟，导致原本性能优异的检索系统在真实用户场景下性能崩溃（Recall@5从96-100%跌至36-44%），而简单可审计的语体转换词典桥梁可恢复大部分性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强问答日益用于帮助公众查询福利资格，但这些系统检索的文档以政府公文语体撰写，而目标用户（普通民众、非母语者）常以平实、非正式的语言提问。这种语体不匹配可能把高性能检索系统变成不公平的系统，而标准检索评估（只用正式语体）会完全隐藏这一缺陷。

Method: 构建受控基准：51条联邦福利资格规则和25个信息需求，每条需求同时以公文语体和日常用户语体表述，保持金标段落不变。使用BM25、TF-IDF和词图检索器对比两种语体下的检索性能，并诊断词汇重叠差异。提出一个简单、可审计的plain-to-formal词典桥梁作为缓解方案。

Result: 正式语体检索近乎完美（Recall@5达96-100%），而日常语体检索崩溃（Recall@5仅36-44%）。BM25的Recall@1从84%跌至16%，Recall@5从100%跌至44%，出现56个百分点的公平性差距。机制诊断：正式查询与金标段落内容词重叠率0.63，日常查询仅0.11（5.9倍差距）。词典桥梁方案将日常查询的BM25 Recall@5从44%提升至80%。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献不是新的检索器，而是一套评估协议、基准、机制诊断和透明的缓解方案，针对标准检索评估所隐藏的高风险社会影响失效模式。语体不匹配是检索公平性的关键被忽视维度，需要在部署前进行多语体评估。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented question answering is increasingly used to help people navigate public-benefits eligibility, yet the documents these systems retrieve from are written in agency register while intended users often ask questions in plain, informal, or non-native English. We show that this register mismatch can turn a high-performing retrieval system into an inequitable one. We construct a controlled benchmark of 51 publicly documented federal benefit-eligibility rules and 25 information needs, each phrased in both agency register and plain user register while keeping the gold passage fixed. Across BM25, TF-IDF, and a term-graph retriever, formal-register evaluation is nearly perfect (Recall@5 96-100%), but plain-register retrieval collapses (Recall@5 36-44%). For BM25, Recall@1 falls from 84% to 16% and Recall@5 from 100% to 44%, a 56-point equity gap on identical information needs. We trace the mechanism to a measurable vocabulary gap: formal queries share 0.63 of their content terms with the gold passage, while plain queries share only 0.11, a 5.9x reduction. A deliberately simple, auditable plain-to-formal lexicon bridge recovers much of the failure, lifting plain-query BM25 Recall@5 from 44% to 80%. The contribution is not a new retriever; it is an evaluation protocol, benchmark, mechanistic diagnosis, and transparent mitigation for a high-stakes social-impact failure mode that standard retrieval evaluation hides.

</details>


### [359] [Not All Matches Are Equally Valuable: An Online Experiment of Retention-Focused Recommendation in a Job-Matching Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01652)
*Tatsuya Ute,Chiaki Ichimura,Yuta Saito*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文研究了双向匹配平台中仅优化即时匹配信号与业务目标（如用户流失率）不一致的问题。基于真实求职匹配平台数据发现近期匹配极少的用户流失风险显著更高，由此提出并实现了一种保留感知的推荐后处理方法：对流失风险用户给予评分提升以增加其获得匹配的概率。真实平台在线实验显示处理组用户流失方向性低于对照组（但未达统计显著），而公司方流失无恶化迹象。这是首批在真实双向求职匹配平台上研究以保留为目标的推荐在线实验之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双边匹配平台推荐系统通常以点击率、回复率或成功匹配数等即时参与信号为优化目标，但这些目标可能与真实的商业目标（如用户流失率、平台收入）不一致。尤其当匹配数量少的用户流失风险显著更高时，单纯最大化匹配总数可能违背平台长期利益，因此需要探索保留感知的推荐策略。

Method: 首先在真实求职匹配平台上进行实证分析，验证近期匹配很少的用户流失概率显著更高、而成功用户额外匹配的保留边际价值有限。基于该发现，提出保留感知推荐问题，并实现一个简单的后处理方法：在原有的以匹配为中心的排序基础上，对处于流失风险的用户给予分数提升（score boost），以增加其获得匹配的可能性并改善保留效果。最后在真实求职匹配平台上开展在线随机对照实验评估。

Result: 在线实验中，处理组相比对照组呈现方向性更低的用户流失率，但该效应在常规显著性水平下未达到统计显著；同时公司方流失未显示出恶化迹象。整体结果支持该方向的可行性但证据强度有限。

Conclusion: 实证研究揭示了"少匹配用户流失风险高"的平台特征；所提出的保留感知后处理方法在实际平台上方向性降低了用户流失且未损害公司方，是首批在真实双向求职平台开展保留型推荐在线实验的研究之一。未来可进一步改进策略设计和提升统计功效以验证更稳健的效果。

Abstract: Recommender systems in two-sided matching platforms are commonly optimized for immediate engagement signals such as click-through rate, reply rate, or the total number of successful matches. However, in real-world marketplaces, maximizing matches alone may be misaligned with business goals such as user churn rate and platform revenue, especially when users with fewer matches are at substantially higher risk of churn. In this paper, we study a real job matching platform and show that users with very few recent matches are indeed much more likely to leave the platform, while additional matches for already successful users provide limited marginal value for retention. Motivated by this empirical finding, we formulate a retention-aware recommendation problem and implement a simple post-processing method that adjusts the baseline match-focused ranking to prevent user churn. Specifically, the implemented method gives a score boost to churn-risk users with the goal of increasing their likelihood of obtaining matches and improving retention. We evaluate this practical approach in an online experiment on a real job-matching platform. The treatment group showed directionally lower user churn than the control group, although the estimated effect was not statistically significant at conventional levels, while company-side churn showed no evidence of deterioration. To our knowledge, this is among the first online experimental studies to investigate retention-focused recommendation in a real reciprocal job-matching platform.

</details>


### [360] [MELON: A Large-Scale Dataset for Multi-Event Text-to-Long-Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01654)
*Chan Hur,SeungWoo Song,Jeong-hun Hong,Won Jun Oh,Hyeyoung Park,KyungTae Lim*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出MELON，首个面向长视频多事件结构的文本-视频检索大规模数据集，并引入多事件感知损失函数以提升检索准确率，弥补现有短片段数据集无法反映真实检索场景的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本-视频检索数据集主要基于包含单一主导事件的短视频片段，无法捕捉真实世界中的长视频检索场景——长视频天然包含多个语义不同的事件，且单个文本查询可能对应多个不连续的时间段。

Method: 1) 构建MELON数据集：大规模标注长视频中多个事件区间及其对应的文本描述，支持多事件理解的训练与评估；2) 提出多事件感知损失函数：鼓励模型区分完整事件匹配与部分事件匹配，从而提升检索精度。

Result: 多事件感知损失在检索准确率上取得显著提升，MELON数据集为长视频复杂场景下的文本-视频检索提供了有效的训练和评估基础。

Conclusion: MELON数据集与多事件感知损失共同为将文本-视频检索扩展到复杂长视频场景奠定了坚实基础，并为该领域未来研究提供了更贴近真实的评估环境。

Abstract: Existing text-video retrieval datasets primarily consist of short-form clips containing a single dominant event. While suitable for measuring basic vision-language alignment, they are limited in capturing real-world retrieval scenarios, where long-form videos naturally contain multiple semantically distinct events and a single text query may correspond to several non-contiguous temporal segments. To bridge this gap, we introduce MELON, the first large-scale dataset designed to extend text-video retrieval to long-form videos featuring complex, multi-event structures. MELON explicitly annotates multiple event intervals per video along with their corresponding textual descriptions, enabling both training and evaluation of multi-event understanding in long, untrimmed videos. In addition, we propose a multi-event aware loss that encourages models to differentiate between full-event and partial-event matches, yielding substantial improvements in retrieval accuracy. Together, the MELON dataset and our proposed loss establish a robust foundation for expanding text-to-video retrieval to complex long-form scenarios and provide a more realistic evaluation setting for future research in the field.

</details>


### [361] [From Feature Interaction to Feature Transport - A Unified Block for Scalable Recommendation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01655)
*Zichen Luo,Jiachen Guo,Keming Gu,Jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出CRAFT（上下文残差自适应特征传输模块），将深度统一推荐建模为离散的上下文条件表示演化过程。该模块将非序列化多字段特征总结为可靠性感知的上下文场，生成残差位移和记忆保持信号，控制意图与序列表示的演化。在TAAC2026广告推荐竞赛中取得0.838090的test AUC，超过此前榜首成绩0.83798，且堆叠至六层可进一步提升至0.838148。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一推荐模型的交互中心设计主要关注混合各层次内的异构token，但忽略了跨堆叠层的意图信息如何被携带、过滤和保持这一关键问题，限制了模型的可扩展性与表达能力。

Method: 提出特征传输视角，将深度统一推荐视为离散的上下文条件表示演化过程；设计CRAFT模块，将非序列化特征总结为可靠性感知上下文场，据此生成残差位移信号与记忆保持信号，分别作用于意图表示与序列表示，使上下文从被动交互对象转变为主动的表示演化控制器。

Result: 在TAAC2026竞赛中test AUC达0.838090，超越原榜首0.83798；缩放实验表明堆叠六个CRAFT块提升至0.838148，增加隐藏维度达0.838106，验证了深度与宽度扩展均能带来收益。

Conclusion: CRAFT证明了特征传输范式的有效性与可扩展性，非序列化上下文作为表示演化主动控制器可显著提升统一推荐模型性能，具有良好泛化潜力。

Abstract: Unified recommendation models aim to jointly model non-sequential multi-field features and sequential user behaviors, but existing interaction-centric designs mainly focus on mixing heterogeneous tokens within each layer. We argue that scalable unified recommendation also requires controlling how intent information is carried, filtered, and preserved across stacked blocks. Inspired by flow-based representation dynamics, we introduce feature transport, a view that treats deep unified recommendation as a discrete context-conditioned representation evolution process. We propose CRAFT, a Contextual Residual Adaptive Feature Transport block, which summarizes non-sequential features into a reliability-aware contextual field and uses it to generate residual displacement and memory-preserving signals for intent and sequence representations. In this way, non-sequential context acts as an active controller of representation evolution rather than a passive object of interaction. In the TAAC2026 advertising recommendation competition, CRAFT achieves a test AUC of 0.838090, surpassing the previous leaderboard-best score of 0.83798. Scaling experiments further show that CRAFT benefits from both depth and width expansion: stacking CRAFT to six blocks improves test AUC to 0.838148, while increasing the hidden dimension reaches 0.838106. These results demonstrate the effectiveness, scalability, and generalization potential of the feature transport paradigm. Source code: https://github.com/AshleyLuo001/CRAFT

</details>


### [362] [NeoMME: A Single-Tower Multimodal-Native Multilingual Foundation Encoder for Efficient Fine-Tuning and Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01657)
*Aurélien Lac,Tony Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了NeoMME，一个260M和800M参数的多模态多语言双向编码器模型家族。与那些将生成式视觉语言模型（VLM）重新用作编码器的视觉文档检索器（如ColPali）不同，NeoMME从头预训练，在单一双向Transformer编码器中直接处理多语言文本和原始图像块。模型支持16,384 token上下文，可编码两张4K UHD图像。在ViDoRe v3基准上，260M模型以0.523 nDCG@10超越所有800M参数以下的模型，800M模型达到0.556。模型同时支持分层token池化和非对称量化的后期交互压缩（255倍），同时保持95%以上性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉文档检索器（如ColPali）将生成式视觉语言模型重新用作编码器，这会为一个非生成任务带来不必要的参数和计算开销。作者希望设计一个专门为编码（而非生成）任务优化的多模态双向编码器，从零开始训练，避免VLM中因果注意力架构在编码任务上的低效问题。

Method: NeoMME采用单一双向Transformer编码器处理多语言文本和原始图像补丁，无因果注意力限制。预训练使用掩码离散扩散文本目标，多模态样本以可见图像补丁为条件。支持16,384 token上下文。下游微调采用密集头与后期交互头联合训练，用于文档检索。还提出了层级token池化和非对称量化技术来压缩后期交互的多模态文档嵌入。

Result: 在ViDoRe v3基准上，NeoMME-Retriever 260M以0.523 nDCG@10超越所有评估的800M参数以下模型；800M版本达到0.556。在匹配的2048x2048输入尺寸下（NVIDIA L40S），260M模型编码页面吞吐量约为ColModernVBERT的2倍。后期交互嵌入压缩255倍后仍保留超过95%的基线nDCG@10性能。

Conclusion: NeoMME证明了专门为编码设计的双向多模态编码器在多语言文档检索任务上优于生成式VLM架构的重新用途，同时提供更高的计算效率和极致的嵌入压缩能力。作者在Apache 2.0许可下开源了模型权重和Hugging Face Transformers集成代码。

Abstract: Multimodal models often build on architectures designed for generative vision-language modeling, typically combining separately pretrained vision encoders with causal language models. Visual document retrievers such as ColPali repurpose these models as encoders, carrying over the parameter and compute overhead of a VLM for a non-generative task.
  We introduce NeoMME, a family of 260M and 800M-parameter Multimodal and Multilingual bidirectional Encoders that process multilingual text and raw image patches in a single bidirectional Transformer encoder. Both models are pretrained from scratch with a masked discrete-diffusion text objective, conditioned on visible image patches for multimodal examples. Both support a 16,384-token context, enough to encode up to two standard 4K UHD images.
  To demonstrate its downstream capabilities, we fine-tune NeoMME with jointly trained dense and late-interaction heads. On the ViDoRe v3 benchmark, the resulting NeoMME-Retriever 260M outperforms all evaluated models strictly below 800M parameters with 0.523 nDCG@10, while NeoMME-Retriever 800M reaches 0.556. At a matched 2048x2048 image input size on an NVIDIA L40S, NeoMME-260M encodes pages with about 2x the throughput of ColModernVBERT. Hierarchical token pooling and asymmetric quantization compress late-interaction multimodal document embeddings by 255x while preserving over 95% of baseline nDCG@10. We contribute NeoMME to Hugging Face Transformers and release the pretrained backbone and retrieval-compatible checkpoints under Apache 2.0 at https://hf.co/collections/Hcompany/neomme.

</details>


### [363] [KGVoyager: Knowledge Graph Agnostic Question Answering via Agentic Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01780)
*Essam Wisam,Chengkai Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出KGVoyager，一种与知识图谱无关的智能体架构，用于RDF图的KGQA任务。它通过动态发现图结构和语义来生成SPARQL查询，仅需查询端点和一个轻量级类索引，无需预定义本体或标注语料。在四个基准上F1提升约8点，成本和运行时间各降低约22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在领域特定的KGQA场景中，形式化本体和人工标注的text-SPARQL对通常不可用，现有方法难以适用。作者希望构建一个不依赖预定义本体或示例的KG-agnostic系统，使其能够推广到更多真实世界的图谱端点。

Method: 采用think-act-observe循环的智能体架构，集成搜索、探索和执行三类工具。系统动态地将自然语言术语映射到图IRI，探索图结构，并通过执行反馈迭代地细化和修正SPARQL查询。与以往方法不同，它仅需一个轻量级类索引即可工作。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上，KGVoyager相比先前最先进方法将F1分数提升了约8个百分点，同时将成本和运行时间各减少约22%。

Conclusion: KGVoyager展示了无需预存本体或训练数据即可进行高效KGQA的可行性，以更低成本和更快速度获得更优性能，证明其可适用于更多真实世界的知识图谱端点。

Abstract: Knowledge Graph Question Answering (KGQA) over RDF graphs remains challenging in domain-specific settings, where formal ontologies and curated text-SPARQL pairs are often unavailable. We present KGVoyager, a KG-agnostic agentic architecture that generates SPARQL queries from natural language questions by dynamically discovering graph structure and semantics, requiring only a query endpoint of the underlying graph. Using a think-act-observe loop with search, exploration, and execution tools, KGVoyager maps terms to graph IRIs, uncovers structure, and refines queries through execution feedback - all without pre-existing ontologies or examples. Unlike the prior state of the art, KGVoyager requires only a lightweight class index which renders it applicable for far more real-world endpoints. Across four benchmarks, KGVoyager improves F1 by ~8 points while cutting cost and runtime by ~22% each.

</details>


### [364] [Index-Free Dynamic Edge Retrieval with Energy-Tail-Aware Partial Scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01820)
*Mohammad Arif Rasyidi,Omar Alhussein*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出ETAR，一种无索引（index-free）的动态最大内积搜索方法，通过保留查询向量中最大平方值的坐标并以低精度表示来估计相似度，同时补偿被跳过的坐标并进行全精度重排序，在保持简单更新操作的同时减少查询工作量。在九个静态数据集上平均达到99.2%的Recall@10，速度比精确扫描快4倍以上，在ARM移动设备上最高加速6.9倍，并在五个流式工作负载下保持100%的Recall@10而无需索引重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态MIPS问题需要在数据集可动态变化（插入、替换、删除）的情况下高效检索K个最大内积向量。现有方法面临两难：全向量扫描更新简单但查询成本高，索引方法降低查询成本但需要维护额外结构导致更新复杂。作者希望找到一种中间方案，既能减少查询工作量，又能保持更新简单。

Method: ETAR方法采用三重策略：(1) 保留查询向量中最大平方值的坐标，使其覆盖查询向量总平方幅度的大部分，其余视为低幅度尾部；(2) 用紧凑的低精度表示来估计保留坐标的相似度，并补偿被跳过的坐标；(3) 对固定数量的候选向量使用全精度向量进行重排序。整体无需维护任何索引结构。

Result: 在九个静态数据集上平均达到99.2%的Recall@10，比精确扫描快4倍以上；在ARM移动设备上对四种合成分布最高加速6.9倍；在五个流式工作负载下每个测量点都保持100%的Recall@10，无需索引重建。

Conclusion: ETAR为动态MIPS提供了实用的折中方案：通过减少查询成本同时保留简单、无索引的更新机制，实现了高召回率与快速查询的兼顾，特别适用于边缘检索等需要频繁更新的场景。

Abstract: Dynamic maximum inner-product search (MIPS) returns the $K$ stored vectors with the largest dot products with a query while allowing the dataset to change through insertions, replacements, and deletions. For edge retrieval, the challenge is to achieve high recall and fast queries without making updates expensive. Full-vector scanning keeps updates simple but compares each query with every stored vector, while indexed methods reduce query cost at the expense of maintaining additional structures during updates. We propose ETAR, an index-free method that reduces query work while preserving simple updates. ETAR keeps the query coordinates with the largest squared values until they cover most of its total squared magnitude and treats the rest as a low-magnitude tail. It estimates similarity from the retained coordinates using a compact lower-precision representation, corrects for skipped coordinates, and reranks a fixed number of candidates using full-precision vectors. Across five runs on nine static datasets, ETAR averages 99.2% Recall@10, the fraction of exact top-10 results recovered, while running over 4$\times$ faster than exact scanning at a representative setting. This speedup also extends to an ARM-based mobile device, where ETAR is up to 6.9$\times$ faster across four synthetic distributions. Under five streaming workloads, it maintains 100% Recall@10 at every measured point without index rebuilds. Overall, ETAR offers a practical middle ground for dynamic MIPS by reducing query cost while retaining simple, index-free updates. Code is available at https://github.com/arasyi/etar-mips.

</details>


### [365] [MERGED: Multimodal Entity Resolution via Generated Expert Reasoning Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01913)
*You-Lin Chen,Kyoungjun Park,Bin Xu,Prithviraj Sen,Pedro Herrero-Vidal*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MERGED是一个蒸馏框架，将大型视觉语言模型(VLM)的结构化推理能力蒸馏到紧凑的7B参数学生模型中，用于产品实体解析中不断演化的关系定义。通过多教师标注和元判断器解决分歧，结合SFT和DPO训练，无需人工标注即可实现快速适应新关系定义，在性能和成本上均优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 产品实体解析中关系定义随业务需求不断演化，传统方法依赖缓慢且昂贵的人工标注，不仅效率低、成本高，还缺乏推理过程。虽然大型VLM可以零样本快速适应新定义并提供推理，但其成本和延迟在工业生产规模下难以承受。
